# trureturing / D5 —— 仓库规范全卷 v7.40(定本:宪法·地层·编码·执法·八官·管线·治理·引导)

> ⚑ **铭牌**。组织:**trureturing**(收据三张:27.99 真理为攀登而不可达之 ν、27.90 理论过自家分类器返回原点、仪文"账本的最后一行永远是下一轮的第一行")。仓库名:**trureturing**——**仓库即模空间,单库承全族**(v7.4 裁决,撤姊妹分库):`Metallic/`(G 层参数化机器)+ `D5/ D8/ …`(实例层,按需生长)+ `Moduli/`(跨理论比较定理之家);分库仅当已证实压力(治理/许可/规模),**裂由压力,不预裂**。
> README 首行:*trureturing — the last line of the ledger is always the first line of the next round.*
>
> **单一 spec 律**:本规范永远只有此一份文件,原位演进;版本头部递增,正文只陈述现行契约,历史归 git;不另立、不改名、不分裂——spec 自己遵守它给仓库立的法。
> **本卷自足**:据此一份文档即可建立完整仓库;仓库由 AI 八官管线驱动,自动搜论文、自动研究、自动写论文;内容在 harness 下自发增长而不乱。

---

# 第一部:宪法

## 1.1 三定律(一百五十余轮之结构经验)
①**骨骼 = 依赖偏序**:import 只许向下,编译器与 Lint 双层强制;
②**投影不当骨骼**:疆域、文档、书、论文皆为投影,不得反向决定结构;
③**生长自相似**:地址由算法算出(不开会);桶满则裂、只裂不迁;历史归 git。

## 1.2 平面总览(六平面二端口)
| 平面 | 目录 | 性质 |
|---|---|---|
| F 形式层 | `Metallic/ + D<disc>/ + Moduli/` | Lean;**唯一承重层**(模空间结构,v7.4) |
| B 散文层 | `Blueprint/` | 人读叙事;**镜像 F 之地址** |
| E 证据层 | `Evidence/` | 数值+实验;**镜像 F 之地址** |
| C 编年层 | `Chronicle/` | 评注/判词/史;**按时间索引,历史归 git** |
| L 摄入端口 | `Library/` | 文献进 |
| P 产出端口 | `Papers/` | 论文出 |
| Meta | `Meta/` | FILEMAP、领域及消化等声明性数据 |
| tools | `tools/` | harness 程序、验证器与测试 |
| agents | `agents/` | 八官宪章与上下文包 |

**镜像律**:B/E 不拥有自己的分类学——借用 F 层地址(未形式化者借其*未来*地址);一个数学单元 = 一个地址 × 至多三平面。
**编年律**:凡按时间发生者进 C 层按日期组织、不按主题重排;条目只保证当下正确,改删历史由 git 查证。

## 1.3 内容路由总表(任何产物一查定址)
定义/定理/证明→F(地层算法);开放问题→`X_Frontier/`;命名假设→`X_Assumptions/`;条件定理→`X_Certificates/`;数学叙事→B(镜像);数值/实验→E(镜像);常数表→`Evidence/values.json`;评注/判词→C(日期);文献→L(bibkey);论文→P(recipe-id);工具词表→Meta;宪章→agents;治理文件→`docs/`;旧卷→`docs/history/`(只读)。

## 1.4 状态即语法(评级零元数据)
无 sorry 且 axiom 闭包 ⊆ `{propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound}` = **已证**;axiom 闭包含 `AxiomDebt.lean` 登记之额外公理 = **承典**;签名携 `(h : Assumptions.X)` = **条件定理(证书)**;居 X_Frontier 带 sorry = **开放**;居 C 层 = **评注(永不承重)**。Mathlib 的标准三公理为底座,不改变状态;未登记额外公理一律拒收。状态徽章由语法自动生成,全库禁手写状态。

## 1.6 真理条款(承重 ⊊ 真理:Lean 之双角色,防口号误读)
Lean 于本库任**两职**:**公证**(证明——无 sorry 即已证)与**登记**(语言——陈述其不能证明者:Frontier 之 sorry 是被形式化*表示*的未知,非被验证的真理)。
由是澄清:"唯一真实源 = 库本体"指**载体唯一**(权威文本 = 本仓库之六平面),非"Lean 证明 = 真理全集";**F 层为唯一承重层,而承重 ⊊ 真理**——经验置信住 E 面与 REGISTRY,裁决与史住 C 面,皆真理之民、皆不承重。四种真理(定理/证书/开放/评注)各有语法归宿(1.4),一种不弃。
**真 ⊋ 证(Gödel)之结构化承认**:永久 sorry 白名单即 μ–ν 之沟的门牌——Hearts.lean 是本条款的建筑形态。**此节之由来:27.103 判"形式化不得为唯一真理源",v3.0 口号"唯一真实源=Lean 库本体"表述过松,经外部审(v7.10)拆焊成文——两判并立不悖:公证不得独尊,载体可以唯一。**

## 1.5 机器门控(四态;其余全自动)
X_Assumptions 变更;新增 axiom;论文签发;Hearts.lean 任何触碰均按 rule-22 machine-decide 四态语义处理;机器可判即执行判词,形式不可判即 `open`,能力/授权/资源缺口记具名 `open` 等灯亮,harness bug 自主修复,其余 lane 不阻塞。

# 第二部:目录与地层(harness 骨架)

## 2.1 完整树(v7.4 注:下示为 D5 实例侧之树;库顶层为 `Metallic/ + D5/ + Moduli/ + 平面与特区`,`GoldenLedger/` 即 `D5/` 之旧称,F 层 GID 从此 = 字面仓库路径去 `.lean` 后缀)
```
golden-ledger/
├── Trureturing.lean                     # 根导入
├── D5/                                  # (与 Metallic/、Moduli/ 并列;下示实例侧)
│   ├── S0/
│   │   ├── Carrier/{Ring,Conj,Norm,Units}.lean      # ℤ[φ] 四件(接 Mathlib.Zsqrtd)
│   │   └── Conventions/{WDigits,Notation}.lean      # W-位值宪法(W1–W3)
│   ├── S1/{Scale,Digit,Phase,Depth}/           # A/Z/G 轴与有限分辨率深度
│   ├── S2/{Word,Lattice,ModelSet,IFS,Pzg}/
│   ├── S3/{Arith,Diffraction,Spectral,Analytic,Solenoid,Fractal}/
│   ├── S4/{KTheory,Hecke,PhysicsDict}/
│   ├── X_Assumptions/{Convergence.lean,REGISTRY.md}
│   ├── X_Certificates/                              # 条件定理(引 X_Assumptions)
│   └── X_Frontier/                                  # sorry 唯一白名单
│       ├── Hearts.lean                              # O-5 独立性、O-6 正定性(冻结)
│       └── S3/ S4/ …                                # 各前沿镜像其地层
├── Blueprint/ (镜像 S*/X_*)   Evidence/ (镜像 + kernels/ + experiments/ + values.json)
├── Chronicle/<YYYY>/<MM>/<DD>-<slug>.md  (+ INDEX.md 由 CI 生成, LEGACY.md 旧评注映射)
├── Library/{queries.yaml, anchors.bib, notes/<bibkey>.md, <Domain>/<bibkey>.md}
├── Papers/{recipes/, frozen/<paper-id>/}(build/ 不入库)
├── Meta/{FILEMAP.toml, domains.yaml, Digestion/}
├── agents/{CONTEXT.md, scout.md…gate.md, theorist.md, echo-template.md, verdict-template.md}
├── docs/{CONTRIBUTING.md(防命理墙+可证伪七条), GOVERNANCE.md, history/}
├── lakefile.lean  lean-toolchain  .github/workflows/
```
**顶层永远 = S0–S4 + 三特区 + 八固定目录:骨架尺寸恒定,与内容量无关。**

## 2.2 地址算法(零会议)
地层是显式语义坐标,由疆域词表决定:`Meta/domains.yaml` 每个疆域必须携 `stratum` 字段,落格 `S<stratum>/<疆域>/<模块>.lean`。执法两条:(i)H1 闭包不变量:S_k 单元之库内 import 闭包 ⊆ S_{≤k}(同层互引合法,S0 亦然);(ii)一致性:单元 import 闭包之最高地层 ≤ 其疆域地层。`1 + max(import 之地层)` 仅为新概念选层之下界启发,不再定义地层。

## 2.3 生长律
目录内计容量文件 >96 或 SL-003 登记材料(`Meta/ci-checks.json`,含根目录与 `D5/` 下的 Lean 源及 `Reg/`)中的文件 >1000 行 ⟹ **局部分裂**(按子疆域,组名先入词表;分裂工具(`StrataLint split` 子命令,D5-T0004;成熟前以 git mv+手工 import 重写代行,SL-003 机器执法)单 PR 完成 mv+import 重写);**只裂不迁,永不全局重排;深度对数增长,结构演化 append-only。** 深度无上限:满桶在任一层皆可就地裂出子疆域,SL-003 只数目录内文件、不数子目录,故裂的代价恒为一层而非一次全局重排。阈值常量唯一住在 `tools/StrataLint.Engine/Rules/RepositoryRules.Structure.cs`:文件 >800 行为 Observe 软提示,>1000 行 Block;目录准入上限 96,改动触及的目录超限且含受保护基线没有的计容量路径即 Block,否则 Observe;全树另以 192 为容忍上限,超出即 Block。 容量只约束骨骼:JSON(含 JSONL/NDJSON/JSON5/JSONC)、YAML/YML、TOML、CSV/TSV、XML/TRX、Base64(`.b64`) 数据文件豁免 SL-003 文件行数软硬上限,扩展名不区分大小写,目录容量仍按既有规则计算。Blueprint Markdown 是 FILEMAP `kind=generated` 的人读投影,不另计容量——同一文档的结构名额已由其 `.scribe.cs` 定义支付;文档 GID 须指向既存 Lean 模块,且真实树上的 `.scribe.cs` 定义、反射发现项与同 stem `.md` 路径保持双射,若把源与投影重复计数,满额 Lean 桶的 Blueprint 镜像在半数模块蓝图化后即超限。该排除与双射只守结构槽及镜像存在性,不校验 `.md` 内容,也不赋予其内容或历史权威。

**文献笔记容量(delta-only)。** SL-003 对 `Library/**` 下新增或修改的 Markdown 文件检查候选全文，硬上限为 3,000 行；扩展名不区分大小写，空行计入，末尾换行符不额外计一行。超过上限即 Block，诊断包含路径、实测行数和上限。改名或复制的目标路径视为新增；修改已有长文时仍须满足候选全文上限，即使修改未增加行数。删除及未触及的历史长文不在本项检查范围，判官实现变更也不扩大选集。Library Markdown 不适用通用文件行数软硬上限，check-current 及 repository capacity-audit 不检查其行数，目录容量照常计算。超出单篇篇幅的内容拆为独立笔记并以引用衔接。

**理论文档容量(delta-only)。** SL-003 对 `docs/develop/theory/**` 中新增或修改的 Markdown 检查候选全文,硬上限为 5,000 行;扩展名不区分大小写,空行计入,末尾换行符不额外计一行。超过上限即 Block,诊断包含路径、实测行数和上限。删除路径不读正文;未触及的历史长卷、check-current 及 repository capacity-audit 不进行这项检查,判官实现变更也不扩大理论文档选集。新内容超出单卷篇幅时写入同主题续卷并通过引用衔接,保留既有条目、编号与引用。此谓词只约束文档大小,不校验理论数学内容,也不建立 Lean 对理论正文的依赖。

## 2.4 第五坐标:通用性(理论自分类之工程兑现)
文件头声明 `generality: G|I|E`——G 通用机器(任意实二次域/任意无理;**自然普遍性律:能免费一般化者必须一般化陈述**,证于 `Zsqrtd d` 末行特化);I 实例运气(h=1、模数 5;**I 承重须警示注**——现查唯一承重 I 为 h=1/UFD,推广至 h>1 需理想论翻修);E 极值指纹(Hurwitz/Markov 根/复杂度地板——理论签名,不可亦无需一般化)。实测分解 G80%/I9%/E9%,承重 13G+1I。**因子分解落位(v7.4)**:G 层就地为根包 `Metallic/`(不析出——析出仅当外部需求已证实,且走 lake package 边界非分仓);实例层 `D<disc>/`;**跨族比较定理居 `Moduli/`**(Hurwitz 极值、Markov 谱、Lagrange 谱、分类表)——分库将使全族最好的定理无家可归,故不分。

# 第三部:编码规范(A1–A15:一名一址,机器可判)

**A1 理论码** `THEORY := "D"<基本判别式> | "T"<次数>"D"<判别式>`——D5 金、D8 银、D13 铜;由分类器(6.205 不变量)签发,唯一典范可排序;姊妹实例化 = 换 D。**M0 admission 只实例化 D5**;`Metallic/`、`Moduli/` 与其余合法理论码保留为未实例化坐标,压力案 D5-T0009 成立前 route 与 check 均以 SL-021 拒收并报告该案,不得降格为“未知路径”。

**A2 全域标识符 GID(v7.11 规范虚拟地址)** `GID := THEORY "/" [PLANE "/"] PATH ["." DECL] ["--" TAG]`,PLANE∈{F(省),B,E,C,L,P};每个 PATH segment 必须非空且不得为 `.`/`..`;GID 与**语义目标**立总双射,逐平面唯一反解:F:`D5/<S 层>/<疆域>[/<子疆域>]*/<模块>[.<DECL>]` ↔ `D5/<S 层>/<疆域>[/<子疆域>]*/<模块>.lean` 中之文件或声明(子疆域链长度不设上限,兑现 2.3「深度对数增长」;每段 CamelCase,且**相邻两个桶段**(疆域与其后各子疆域,不含 `<S 层>` 与 `<模块>`)不得同名——重复段不携带坐标信息,地址即不再是其位置的最短名;模块名不参与该比较,故 `D5/S0/Carrier/Algebra/Algebra` 合法(桶对为 `Carrier`/`Algebra`,末段是模块),非相邻的重复亦合法(如 `D5/S0/Carrier/Algebra/Carrier/Ring`));B:`D5/B/<PATH>` ↔ `Blueprint/D5/<PATH>.md`;E:`D5/E/<PATH>.<DECL>--<KIND>` ↔ **唯一单文件** `Evidence/D5/<PATH>.<DECL>.<KIND>`(目录永不充当 E 目标,同一选择子只许一种工件类型),全局常数表为唯一专例 `D5/E/values--json` ↔ `Evidence/D5/values.json`;C:`D5/C/<YYYY-MM-DD>/<slug>` ↔ `Chronicle/<YYYY>/<MM>/<DD>-<slug>.md`;L 根桶:`D5/L/<bibkey>` ↔ `Library/notes/<bibkey>.md`,容量压力裂出的受控疆域桶:`D5/L/<Domain>/<bibkey>` ↔ `Library/<Domain>/<bibkey>.md`,其中 `<Domain>` 必须先入 `Meta/domains.yaml`,且既有根桶地址不迁;P:`D5/P/<paper-id>` ↔ `Papers/recipes/<paper-id>.yaml`,`D5/P/<paper-id>--frozen` ↔ 该冻结包唯一 `manifest.sha256`。F 层工件 GID 即字面 Lean 路径去 `.lean` 后缀,`.DECL` 是该文件内声明选择子;其余平面 GID 是虚拟地址,不得与物理路径混写。例:`D5/S3/Spectral/GapLabeling.gap_label_mem`、`D5/E/S3/Analytic/Cphi.result--json`、`D5/E/values--json`、`D5/C/2026-07-06/r168`、`D5/L/Zeros/coffey2007theta`、`D5/P/D5-P001--frozen`。**papergen/blueprint 只接受全 GID;跨库引用自带理论坐标。** **M0 admission 精确主张**:给定一个受支持且 machine-decide 判词为 admit 的语义 manifest,至多存在一种规范表示与恰一次 admission;不受支持或机器判词非 admit 的 manifest 按 fail-closed 得零次 admission。受 manifest 路由的 JSON/YAML 结构化语义工件现役强制 UTF-8、禁 BOM、对象键字典序、禁行尾空白且末尾恰一 LF;完整 Unicode NFC、默认值与 tag 顺序规范化延后 D5-T0015,故字节规范不得报 full active。
**A2.1 字符集律**(SL-015):除 `formula` 外,机器读字段(GID、键、任务/实验/论文码)字符集恒为 `[A-Za-z0-9_/.-]`——禁 `:`(Windows 文件名/git refname 非法)、`#`(YAML/shell 注释、URL fragment)、`@`(refspec 歧义)及一切需转义符;首段 `D<数字>` 即理论码(无歧义);声明分隔 `.` 与 Lean 全限定名同构;GID 可直接作 URL 段与无引号 YAML 值,物理路径只由 A2 双射求得;分支只用任务码,其新建形态由 A20 指向的 `WorktreeCommand` creation grammar 定义,GID 不入 refname;Unicode 仅居散文与 docstring。`formula` 为显式例外,使用独立 ASCII 算术文法:`expr := term (("+"|"-") term)*`;`term := factor (("*"|"/") factor)*`;`factor := number | ref | "sqrt" "(" expr ")" | "(" expr ")" | ("+"|"-") factor`;token 间允许空格,`number` 为十进制整数或小数,`ref` 必须是同记录 `refs` 中声明的 ASCII 键;除此之外的字符、函数或未绑定 ref 一律拒收。

**A3 地层码**(封闭集永不扩容)S0–S4;X_Assumptions/X_Certificates/X_Frontier。普通疆域之地层由 `Meta/domains.yaml` 的 `stratum` 显式给定;语义:S_k 之库内 import 闭包 ⊆ S_{≤k}(同层互引合法,S0 亦然),且闭包最高地层不得高于该疆域地层;X_C 另可引 X_A;X_F 引一切、被引于无。**目录名即纯地层码(v7.8 正名:助记词 Kernel/Axes/… 入各层 INDEX.md 首行)——F 层 GID 与字面仓库路径去 `.lean` 后缀同构。**

**A4 疆域码** 受控词表 `Meta/domains.yaml`(CamelCase+一行定义);新疆域 = machine-decide admission;词表外目录名 = SL-011 红。

**A5 文件与模块** CamelCase.lean;一文件一主概念;≤1000 行(>800 行 Observe);**文件头强制六行**(SL-012;v7.1 增 digest):
```lean
/- GID: D5/S3/Spectral/GapLabeling
   generality: G|I|E
   mirror-B: D5/B/S3/Spectral/GapLabeling
   mirror-E: D5/E/S3/Spectral/GapLabeling.result--json | none(waiver:<理由>)
   anchors: []
   digest: 谱隙处 IDS 取值于 Z+Zphi(隙标定;衍射-谱同迹像之谱侧) -/
```

**A5.1 用途准入(SL-031;政策原文 CLAUDE.md 第 5⁗ 条)** 受保护 immutable baseline 未冻结且本次 present byte-changed 的 D5 Lean 模块,与本次首次取得 `Golden/Frozen/state/<module>.lean.json` 的模块,取去重并集。修改正文、缺头、无 state、Modified change record、rename/copy 目的路径均参与;同时首冻按 FirstFreeze 判。未变历史不因 judge-only 变化被重审,baseline-frozen 的 utility 棘轮和 SL-008 保留。

选中模块须在 `anchors` 与 `digest` 间有一行 `utility: ...`。值为 `none`,或按顺序 `kind=<kind>; basis=<basis>` 后接可选的 `instance=<declaration-gid>`、`premises=<gid>,<gid>...`、`result=<declaration-gid>`、`claim=<declaration-gid>`;分隔恰为 `; `,可选键顺序固定,不接受重复/未知键。kind 闭集为 `bounded-enumeration|checker|numeric-reduction|certified-instance`;basis 为 `consumer=<declaration-gid>` 或 `refutes=<target>` 或 `terminal=<target>`,target 为 `gid:<declaration-gid>`、`atom:<64-lower-hex>`、`task:D5-Tnnnn`。C# `UtilitySyntax` 只判文法,`UtilityDeclarationValidator` 判用途。

普通正向有限实例不得准入:`certified-instance` / `bounded-enumeration` 仅可走有效 `refutes`,否则 `UTILITY-ORDINARY-INSTANCE-BANNED`;consumer、terminal、自指及附加 result 无豁免。checker / numeric-reduction 仍可承载一般基础设施;非 refutes 的 checker 须有 instance,归约须有 premises 且 basis=consumer。G/I/E 与用途正交,不以命名、数值语法或 tactic 判分类。缺失/坏 utility 或当前 report(含 `none`)阻断;所有 GID 必须唯一解析到当前 report 的 included 声明,atom/TASK 必须解析。已知普通类别换成其他 label 而头后正文逐字节不变时 `UTILITY-CLASSIFICATION-DOWNGRADE` 阻断;分类改变(包括缺失/坏 label)产生 `UTILITY-CLASSIFICATION-CHANGED`。每个选中模块有 `UTILITY-OBSERVED ... semantics=unverified-by-machine`。

所有 refutes 必须同时给 claim 与 result。claim 是唯一 included、无自由项/宇宙参数的闭合 Prop definition;result 是当前准入模块唯一 included、闭合 theorem。Lean 编译期内核检查 theorem 的证明项；inspector 逐字核对 claim 的编译类型为 `Prop`、result 的编译类型为 `Not claim`，不展开定义、不化简、不接受定义性别名或 Reg 证书,claim/result 公理闭包只能含既有许可公理。自我反驳、额外未证假设、非 theorem/外模块 result、正向结果伪标 refutes 均失败。`basis=refutes=gid` 的目标必须等于 claim。

`lean-utility-input` 使用唯一 C# utility parser 发出结构化义务,由既有 inspector 消费。报告 optional module 字段 `utility_refutation` 闭集为 `{claim_gid,claim_source_path,claim_source_sha256,result_gid,is_closed_negation}`;strict source-bound loader 校验当前头部与 claim 源码路径/哈希绑定。claim 即使不在 result 的 Lean import 闭包内也参与声明身份和闭合性检查,故模块 report facet 将该 claim 的编译导出及传递编译工件作为依赖,其变更、删除或传递输入变化使相关反驳重新检查,不只跟踪 imports。缺失该可选字段不满足 refutes 的证据要求。证据不进入 canonical statement material、declaration statement ID 或 frozen identity。ChangedContent 与 PreDeposit 共用解析、禁令、当前报告和 typed refutation 检查;FirstFreeze 另要求 consumer import 可达,以及 refutes=atom 的 coverage 精确指向指定 result 的当前 statement ID。前两阶段不要求 freeze 后才有的 coverage。

`make deposit` 把 BASE 解析为 commit SHA,传入 `deposit-header-check --target <path> --protected-base <sha>`;预检从 repository gateway 读取该 immutable protected baseline,仅其 frozen membership 获历史豁免,candidate 自带 pin 不获豁免,baseline 不可读即错误。预检 utility 失败使用相应 `DEPOSIT_HEADER_UTILITY_*` 诊断。该门不替代 SL-008 或最终 admission,不独立重构 ledger writer。

硬保证止于以上输入域、所报分类的禁令和指定 claim/result 关系。fresh `none`、正文变化后的重分类是否真实,atom/TASK 或独立源断言是否忠实映射到 claim,每个普通实例是否确为反例的一部分,consumer 是否实际使用结论与数值前提是否正确,仍由独立评审逐声明判。无关反例不得掩护普通实例;机器通过不构成一般性或全部用途语义的证明。

**A5.2 Information registration and declared templates.** A declared readout names exactly one enrolled template and its ordered arguments. Templates, enrollments and binding declarations are content-plane inputs to the judge; no content declaration, Boolean, callback or family table grants admission. The report reads the compiler's typed contract heads and owner facts, then decodes the corresponding constructor trees and safe constant references from the serialized declaration parts. Source declaration spelling, modifiers, suffixes, notation, commands and source re-reading do not participate. Mathematical fields carry typed terms and proofs checked during Reg compilation; the report consumes those compiled fields without constructing or rechecking proofs. A value that requires evaluation, a missing compiled part, an unknown format or a read failure receives a named `contract.decode_failed` or raw-artifact diagnostic and has no fallback path. `.unknown` honestly records an open residual; it supplies no missing proof. Registration fields retain the descriptor, primitives, variation, sensitivity, source selection, family record, escape origin and continuation. Inline mathematical bridges are ordinary named Lean declarations.

**A5.3 Template enrollment judgment (E1–E8).** Enrollment checks each compiled definition once per assessment and retains a private checked plan in the invocation-local index. Occurrence assessment selects that one plan by canonical Name and instantiates its retained obligations; it does not search the template population or re-audit the selected definition's body. The byte-radix index bounds lookup by the selected key length, independently of the number of enrolled templates. Unknown forms fail closed; a frozen, governed or upstream identity alone supplies no grammar rule.

The following finite-plan rules continue to govern finite and structural realizations. The source-bound variant below shares their enrollment, occurrence, certificate and consumer pipeline.

- **E1 — telescope and result.** The template is a safe, nonrecursive `def` or `abbrev` with an available body, rigid universe parameters and an ordered Pi telescope retaining every `binderInfo`; a domain may refer only to earlier slots. Admitted slots are carriers in successor universes, data, functions, predicates, `Fintype`/`DecidableEq` dictionaries, indexed decision families, proofs of admitted propositions, and explicit arena/signature interfaces. All domains and the result satisfy E2; the result interface is exactly `PrimitiveRealization signature` or `StructuralPrimitiveRealization arena signature`. Arbitrary `Prop` parameters, unindexed `Decidable φ`, arbitrary typeclass slots, Laws, bridges, theorem units, Name/String/Expr/hash/certificate inputs and unrelated return interfaces have no rule. `DecidablePred` and `DecidableRel` undergo the same indexed-dictionary judgment as explicit Pi syntax: an index must survive typing normalization of the proposition. Raw domains are checked and retained before that test. A quantified proposition used as a parameter type is a proof slot, not a function slot. These typing operations do not authorize binding-comparator normalization.
- **E2 — types, propositions and dictionaries.** The closed grammar admits symbolic slots, universe sorts, Pi/arrow over admitted domains, `Unit`, `PUnit`, `Bool`, `Nat`, `Fin n`, `Prod`, `Sum`, `Option`, `Subtype`, and checked arena/signature/realization interface types and projections, including `State`, `Index`, `Output`, `AnchorIndex` and `PrimitiveAxis`. Propositions admit symbolic predicate applications, equality on admitted data, `True`, `False`, `And`, `Or`, `Not`, `Iff`, Pi/Exists over admitted data, and Nat index bounds. Every child is checked. Dictionaries are symbolic slots or the fixed constructive Unit/PUnit/Bool/Fin/Prod/Sum/Option/Subtype combinators. A combinator matches only its pinned standard Name, universe arity, proof-erased raw type/body identities and actual declaring module; a same-typed instance does not match. Missing pins are incomplete and mismatched identities are unclassified. Arbitrary closed proposition constants, statement-bearing opaque carriers, closed decisions and classical choice have no rule. The lowering table belongs to judge grammar, never content enrollment data.
- **E3 — bodies.** Admitted nodes are typed variables, admitted constructors and structural index literals, lambdas, retained metadata, retained lets with checked type/value/body, applications of admitted functions/predicates/dictionaries, checked interface-record construction, fixed interface projections, and field projections of constructor-enrolled types (`E3.constructor_projection`: a raw projection node or a projection-function application saturated through the self argument compiles to the structural read of that field, function-valued fields included; recursors, `casesOn` and `match` on those types stay rejected under E4c, as do class projections and projections of types not enrolled by E4c). Constructor parameters, motives, instances and every field are checked, including fields later discarded by extraction. Explicit Nat telescope positions, implicit constructor arguments that occur in the constructor's result type (such as the cardinality of `Fin.mk`) and the propositions of proof leaves provide structural-index context; other positions reject non-index numeric literals. Strings, names and quoted expressions have no rule. Executable `decide φ d` requires symbolic object dependence in both the proposition and its decision family; a closed decision fails with `E3.closed_decision`.
- **E4 — eliminations.** The base grammar admits Prod/Subtype projections, Unit/PUnit elimination, Bool/Option/Sum cases, `Fin.elim0` at its kernel type, and the fixed nonrecursive constructor cases with checked motives and branches. Every branch is checked, including dead branches. General recursion, `Nat.rec`, Eq/cast elimination into executable data, choice, Quot elimination and proof-to-data elimination have no rule. A generated matcher must expose an admitted kernel form. The explicit `constructors 1 [...]` extension admits non-mutual, unindexed inductive descriptions with direct strictly positive recursive fields; E4c checks non-parameter constructor fields and direct recursive arguments, while actual uses still pass the shared expression compiler. Only an original AST slot or a direct recursive constructor field supplies descent authority to its kernel recursor; motives and branches are checked and recursive results are symbolic slots. An enrolled inductive whose constructors all have zero fields is an enumeration: its recursor (`E4c.enumeration_dispatch`, reached also through `casesOn` and compiled `match`) is finite dispatch admitted on any admitted major premise with a constant motive, exactly as Bool/Option/Sum cases are; enrolled types with fields keep the descent-authority requirement, and dependent motives and non-enrolled types stay rejected. Indexed/GADT families, nested or function-valued recursion, arbitrary fixpoints/evaluators and unknown extension versions are unsupported.
- **E5 — expansion and proof leaves.** Safe saturated definition applications expand by bounded capture-avoiding substitution of their full telescope. All raw arguments are checked before expansion, including unused arguments. Public Pi domains, body-lambda domains, instantiated helper result types and checked argument obligations remain in the plan even when typing considers them definitionally equal. Recursive/mutual and opaque definitions have no expansion rule. At a proof-valued subterm, check its entire proposition type in type position and retain `ProofLeaf(type)`; the proof implementation is opaque: it is not entered, contributes no dependency, is not compared and is not hashed (τ=0 owner ruling 2026-09-18: the judge judges data, proofs are irrelevant). A proposition expression is not erased merely because its type is `Prop`. Two proof leaves are equal exactly when their propositions are raw-equal; proof-to-data eliminations remain E4 rejections. Independent subtype bounds/signature proof fields are permitted, but their implementations cannot be observed.
- **E6 — theorem independence.** Proof subterms are erased to their propositions before identity checks; raw identity rejection on data precedes projection reduction and definition substitution. The executable/body-type graph rejects registered theorem/proof identities, companions, certificates, statement/hash/query APIs, identity-bearing carriers and theorem-specific decisions. Arbitrary closed theorem constants used as executable sources lack a rule even before registration; the same constants in proof position are opaque leaves whose propositions are checked. The common judge-identity predicate also rejects plans, frames, indexes, dependency identities, occurrence events/keys, binding records/claims/results/certificates, joined records and P1 semantic certificates, including constructors and named/raw projections; selected-plan lookup, observation, inventory, assessment and export are not content primitives. A negative identity test grants no admission. Retained lets check the raw value identity before the complete type/value/body. Proof implementations remain outside traversal; only their propositions pass the occurrence and type checks.

**Independent infinite sources.** E2 also admits a carrier constant in occurrence arguments and forwarding-helper types when its declaring module is independent of the target theorem's module under the import graph; its arguments are checked in type position. This gives an infinite source type its own identity without equating it to a finite `Arena.State`. The pinned standard `Classical.decEq` is an additional E2 dictionary combinator when applied in type position. E5 also admits a safe, nonrecursive, unapplied source definition of function type as an atomic readout when its complete type passes this compiler, an input binder contains a carrier outside the listed finite grammar, its result has no loose binders, and its declaring module is independent of the target theorem's module. A carrier occurring only in the output or a dictionary binder does not suffice. Its native type and body identities remain dependencies. The rule does not apply to template enrollment. The source must be outside the registration and judge packages and outside the target theorem's module; its imports must not reach that theorem's module. Its body is not classified by E3-E4, so this rule certifies source independence and binding identity, not the internal algorithm of an analytic readout. All other E6 identity exclusions still apply to raw arguments and checked types. This extension does not admit a local or target-dependent function through an opaque-source exception.

**One raw-argument compiler.** Enrollment, declared supplied arguments, forwarding-helper type checks and literal-receiver checks use the same E2–E5 compiler and checked constructor descriptions. Occurrence argument checking adds the prohibition on the target theorem, retained raw statement, its proof/decision/certificate and identity-bearing carriers. No whole-readout allowance table supplies a declared-template rule. All retained type nodes, proof-leaf types, public/body domains and audit inputs undergo slot/universe substitution in their original binder contexts and occurrence-relative identity checks, including on an identical-descriptor comparison path. Unknown identity is unclassified, exact target identity is forbidden, and inference or budget failure is incomplete (`dtr.instantiated_type`). A symbolic structural type instantiated to the target statement cannot escape through proof erasure, an unused parameter or an intermediate beta step.

**E7 — producer evidence.** The private `CheckedTemplatePlan` constructor alone publishes enrollment evidence. Its data retains schema/grammar/compiler/toolchain identities, definition and enrollment owners, rigid telescope and slot types, constructor descriptions, proof-erased raw type/body SHA-256 identities, dependency inputs, checked body/type plans, retained proposition/type obligations, charged work and rule IDs. Raw identity uses domain-separated, length-prefixed Expr/Level serialization with anonymous binder names and positional universe parameters; it preserves binderInfo, metadata, let structure, instances, literals and argument order, erases proof subterms to their propositions, and does not reuse a pretty-printed statement hash. Dependency identities contain canonical Name, actual declaring module, raw type identity and executable body identity; proof implementations contribute neither a dependency nor a body identity. Plans retain no source-file byte hashes; their identities and assessment depend on compiled information. Lake traces govern recompilation and reissue evidence.

The persistent `TemplatePlanFrame` contains canonical Name bytes, a token-interned payload and its SHA-256 identity. The encoder and decoder use one current frame layout and version tuple, distinct from the report format identifier in A14.9. The decoder accepts exactly that layout and tuple. Literal tokens use canonical decimal UTF-8 byte length, a colon and exact bytes; repeated tokens use backward-only `@<first-literal-index>:` references. Duplicate literals, leading zeros, invalid UTF-8, unknown markers/references and trailing bytes reject. The fixed-width charged-work literal is outside interning. Both serialization passes debit token processing and emitted bytes and must agree on work. Ownership is checked against the compiled input owner and the raw declaration-to-module table, not supplied owner strings. No older frame layout is an alternative reader path.

**E8 — bounds and failure.** A template frame's key, payload, digest and framing together must fit 65,536 bytes; canonical Name length is at most 1,024 bytes, telescope slots at most 64, visited definition constants at most 4,096, and charged expression/construction/byte work at most 524,288 units. Structural depth is at most 256, including Name/Level encoding. An assessment retains at most 8,388,608 frame bytes; limits are checked before decoding, and the stream stops at its first failure. Decoding bounds tokens, counts, nesting and allocation to at most 32 times payload bytes. Substitution, abstraction, de Bruijn lifting, universe instantiation, materialization, identity encoding and comparison share the caller's remaining quota, charging before construction; compression grants no extra budget. Universe substitution permits only Lean's cheap `mkLevelMax'`/`mkLevelIMax'` simplifications, not term normalization. Enrollment and occurrence assessment each impose a cumulative `maxHeartbeats` ceiling of 100,000 public-option units (1,000 internal allocation heartbeats per unit), carried explicitly through their calculator contexts and respecting smaller nonzero enclosing limits. Nested checks cannot reset the outer total; it is checked after the final subcall. Options can only lower these limits.

Unsupported/malformed forms yield `unclassified_form`; established E6 identities yield `forbidden_dependency`; missing bodies/owners/inputs, failed inference, metavariables, stale evidence or exhausted bounds yield `incomplete_closure`. Available unsafe/opaque/recursive definitions are unsupported, not missing input. For established simultaneous causes the priority is incomplete, then forbidden, then unclassified. Failed enrollment emits no plan; it does not create a review-pending admission state.

**Source-bound family plan.** `D5.S3.ConceptDynamics.InformationEscape.DependentFamily` owns the dependent signature, realization, arena and proof record. Only roles and anchors have finite enumerations; parameters, states and outputs may be infinite. The shared template `realize S readout anchor` enrolls through a typed `Contract.TemplateEnrollment` decoded from its compiled owner. Enrollment checks a safe nonrecursive three-argument constructor transport with standard axiom closure; it cannot enroll a theorem, callback or supplied Boolean as authority. `Reg/Support/DependentFamily` enrolls the template and source-owner mirrors supply the audit records.

`SourceSelection` gives the actual compiler source owner, increasing dependency-closed lexical binder coordinates, and one raw source path plus state-binder ordinal per readout role. By default the path starts at `ConstantInfo.type`. An optional `definition` entry names exactly one safe definition in the same compiler source module whose type is literally `Prop` and whose constant, at the theorem’s unchanged universe parameters, is literally the selected raw occurrence. The explicit occurrence `path` is either `[]` for the complete raw theorem type or `["arg"]` for the sole argument of literal raw `Not`; no other entry context is supported. One replacement opens that definition body and preserves the surrounding raw negation; every readout path descends strictly into that entry. Only that original definition body is entered once; definitional equivalence cannot select an alias, and theorem/proof bodies, applied claims and recursive unfolding are not entry routes. The producer retrieves that original type with rigid universes, checks the coordinate projection and inverse, constructs the dependent parameter packing, and checks each actual observation and its state/output types in its lexical scope. Coordinates may occur below the leading telescope, but every selected binder must have the same raw ancestor-body path in every readout scope; equal names and domains in sibling scopes do not establish correspondence. Local lets are transported only through their original values in their earlier lexical contexts, with capture-avoiding lifting and inverse replay for coordinate domains, states, outputs and observations. A let, proof or dictionary is never an independently variable coordinate. The raw theorem type, owner and universes remain the binding root. The registered Law at actual must reconstruct the complete original statement, including hypotheses, dictionaries and every conclusion clause. Reconstruction preserves local-let values and checks binder modes below lets as well as logical clauses. A forward implication alone is insufficient. The kernel-checked record supplies the exact equivalence bridge, positive Law at actual, a rejected complete-family intervention, per-role sensitivity with all other readouts/anchors fixed, and actual observational dependence. Variation is over complete families: no variation is required in a degenerate fiber such as history length zero. Source linkage follows compiler declaration types and repository data bodies. Source-linked operands remain opaque; transparent aliases and unlinked inductive constructor field types are scanned through raw data/type dependencies, including discarded arguments and proof propositions, with proof bodies opaque. Source-proposition identity uses bounded comparison and normalization of rigid compiled terms when the finite structural test cannot settle it; no Lean conversion or type-checking API is invoked. Source correspondence and the proof obligations, rather than an opaque name, confer the binding. The current family route accepts literal open residual; it asserts no finite residual cardinality. Existing finite witness/empty continuation remains available on the finite route.

Explicit source operand selectors add two bounded forms alongside lexical `stateBinder`. `functionOperand := true` selects one exact raw function occurrence and abstracts its single explicit data argument; dependent output, proof/type arguments and incompatible modes reject. `stateOperand := some path` abstracts one exact data subexpression through nonempty raw `fn`/`arg` edges within the selected observation, without entering definitions or binders. Its optional `booleanPredicate := true` realizes that precise predicate by `decide` and reconstructs the complete original statement with only those selected predicate clauses reified as `decide P = true`. The original equivalence proof remains mandatory. Both forms use `stateBinder = 0` as a mode sentinel and retain the original raw occurrence identity and lexical scope, before abstraction. No alias search, theorem body entry or inference of operand paths occurs.

An existing finite catalog may use this source evidence through the typed Registration family record and finite realization bridge. The original finite registration, unit, contract and catalog address remain authoritative. The Reg contract checks the bridge at the exact original `LegacyPrimitiveRealization` statement and arena, and binds the original generated theorem unit. The producer reconstructs a Unit-indexed family with the same complete role/anchor types, the lift of an arbitrary finite realization, definitionally equal full Laws for that arbitrary realization, and all actual whole state functions and anchor values. Only after these checks may source provenance additionally include that exact finite arena's data/type dependency closure, including its fixed finite dictionaries. This is an explicit provenance extension; arbitrary registration helpers receive no such authority, and unsafe/external heads still reject before source lookup. The existing source-selection, identity rejection, full reconstruction, variation, sensitivity and actual dependence gates remain required. The certificate adds `finite_projection` with exactly `family_arena` and `bridge`; both named dependencies and the original finite arena are fingerprinted. The strict consumer requires the two extra extraction dependencies together with the source, realization and original finite arena dependencies, and joins each to its unique compiler import owner. Missing/cross-source bridge, altered Law, realization, unit, owner or stale selector rejects. Existing budgets and the private plan frame do not change.

Successful source-bound certificates use `bridge_kind: source-equivalence` and carry `source_binding` with original owner/name/type identity, the original leading telescope and universe counts, coordinates and their `coordinate_paths`, scoped readout paths/identities and registration identity. Each readout retains `scope_size` and `scope_paths`, the ordered raw body paths of all its enclosing binders, separately from `telescope_size`. The consumer bounds the leading telescope and coordinate count by 64, lexical scope by 256, and each path by 256; it checks strict coordinate order, nested ancestor paths, and exact coordinate-path agreement at each selected ordinal in every readout. Named entry additionally carries `definition_entry` with the explicit raw occurrence `path`, its raw constant `reference_identity`, `owner`, `name`, raw `type_identity` and raw `body_identity`; the four declaration identity fields are duplicated as the exact corresponding extraction dependency. The strict consumer checks this closed shape, rejects absent or mismatched entry/dependency pairs and reference/path mismatches, and joins the source theorem and named definition to their unique actual import owner. For named entries, the production collection join additionally loads the source theorem's addressed `statement-v1` material and requires exactly the named constant at the root or under literal `Not`, with the theorem's ordered rigid universe parameters. It checks the imported definition's raw nullary `Prop` type and universe arity, and recomputes both the reference and complete raw theorem identities in the existing compact source dialect. Same-wire identity agreement alone is insufficient; report source hashes and material addresses supply the existing current-declaration input contract, while generation provenance fields are not a second independent anchor. Other certificate variants retain their existing shape. Source identities use a canonical shared raw Expr encoding with unchanged 524,288 work, 256 depth and 65,536 retained-byte bounds; an independent height check prevents shared deep subtrees from bypassing depth. Raw source proof subterms, metadata, levels and binderInfo are retained. Existing mathematical `statement_id` and the legacy raw identity dialect do not change. Lake compiler traces, utility inputs and the report format remain the reuse mechanism. These checks do not assert that an author's chosen Law is the unique or natural explanation of the theorem.

**A5.4 Compiled contract evidence.** Production report generation reads the serialized declaration table and module parts for each requested owner. `ArtifactAssessment` discovers the compiled `Contract` heads, decodes constructor trees and safe closed constant references, and evaluates every registration through the compiled-data enrollment, evidence, gate and join calculators. Reachable catalogs and seals undergo complete snapshot, membership, uniqueness and vector checks. No Environment, command replay, native seal driver or input-code execution participates. Companion constants remain immutable report views and are never installed as Lean declarations. A contract value requiring evaluation, a missing declaration part, an unknown layout or an invalid reference fails with the owner, declaration and reason under `contract.decode_failed`; raw-artifact failures identify the missing or unreadable component. There is no source-parser or environment fallback.

The `information_templates` partition has schema `{schema_version,inventory,registered,records}` with schema version `1`. `inventory` and `registered` carry the target owner's occurrence keys. Each joined record carries its occurrence key, source and statement identities, unit and realization names, escape slots, bridge kind and assessment state. A `declared_validated` record carries the complete binding certificate; a `declared_unresolved` record carries its actual assessment diagnostic; an `undeclared` record identifies the missing declaration. These states result from individual assessments, never from batch placeholders. Mathematical proof authority remains with the Reg compiler; producing a structural certificate does not construct or recheck a proof.

The producer validates the evidence partition and its current compiler ownership and dependency joins. Source paths and hashes retain the existing report input contract, while source commands and spelling supply no assessment evidence. Downstream consumers project selected owners before checking four-slot certificates; unresolved, malformed or incomplete selected records remain named evidence failures. Report reuse follows A14.9's compiler traces, utility inputs and report format identifier.

Assessment plans and results are invocation-local data, with no environment assessment cache or separately importable verdict authority. An undecoded value receives no binding certificate. Structural diagnostics do not establish validity; every validated record requires the complete compiled-data assessment and the downstream certificate checks.

**A5.5 Declared-template judgment (SL-031).** `DeclaredTemplateBindingRule` is a named obligation of SL-031, dispatched by `UtilityAdmissionRule` alongside the utility obligation in A5.1. Its registration domain is present Reg Lean modules that are candidate-new or byte-changed relative to the protected base. Rename/copy destinations participate. Separately, `RepositoryRules.ChangedOrFirstPinD5Modules` selects changed, new or first-pinned D5 source owners for newly public theorem lookup; D5 template payloads are never registration evidence. Judge, FILEMAP, compiler or toolchain changes alone do not select unchanged registration owners. An empty selection produces no declared-template findings. Every newly public `theorem`/`lemma` in a selected D5 source (no public theorem/lemma with that name in the base bytes of the same path) must have at least one `declared_validated` four-slot registration in its exact `Reg/D5/<source path>.lean` mirror or a Reg module explicitly imported by that mirror. The theorem's compiler source owner, not its namespace spelling, determines the mirror. Only matching theorem occurrences in that import closure are assessed; an unimported registration cannot supply coverage. A first pin alone creates no new authored theorem. Missing coverage emits `DTR-Unregistered` (Observe). Private and internal-detail declarations, `def`, named source `instance`, `example`, and machine-generated companions are outside this obligation. Explicitly authored theorem names do not acquire a suffix-based exemption. Base bytes are read only through the repository snapshot gateway; base code is never executed. Known limitation: anonymous proposition-valued instances elaborate as theorems and cannot be distinguished in the report. The finding names are exactly `DTR-Undeclared`, `DTR-Evidence`, `DTR-Unregistered` and `DTR-Declared`; every one of them is Observe. The obligation collects registration state as warnings and never blocks admission; changes to this judge remain owned by the registration research line (#5214), while theorem authors and reviewers own the delivery evidence described below.

**Author delivery and registration completion.** For each new public D5 theorem/lemma selected above, except the designated refutation result, authors must attempt faithful four-slot registration in the same delivery under CLAUDE.md §3.9. Completion requires at least one `declared_validated` four-slot registration per audit target in the source owner's mirrored Reg change, compiled Lean proof terms and current binding evidence. D5 content-template theorems/lemmas are included. Helpers in `Reg`, `Interface` and `Impl` create no recursive D5 audit target and retain their existing kernel, `sorry` and axiom checks. CI success or an Observe warning is not registration evidence; missing, invalid or uncompiled evidence leaves the audit unfinished. Registration proofs create no mathematical GID, Scribe, deposit or utility obligations, and zero-new-Lean reuse creates no artificial audit wrapper.

**Registration work and blocked delivery (CLAUDE.md §3.9).** Reuse lawful templates; author missing content templates, Reg support/enrollment and mirrored registrations when required for the selected audit targets. If registration is missing, invalid or blocked, open or reuse an issue naming the original theorem, the concrete template gap or failure verdict and readings, and the missing evidence; link that issue and disclose the unfinished audit in the delivery, then continue mathematical development and delivery. No exhaustive encoding, repeated retry, immediate repair lane or separate waiver is required. Existing validated evidence remains intact. An issue grants no proof or new machine status; kernel, `sorry`, axiom, freeze and other required checks remain in force. Do not modify the judge, force a wrong template, weaken the original theorem, add bind-only wrappers, require historical backfill or introduce a debt ledger.

The compiled-data assessment executes template E1–E8, evidence, registration gates, complete joins and seal checks. Independent snapshots retain multiplicity, membership, statement identities and contributor modules. Seal consumption checks source core facts, uniqueness, qualified-name collisions, maximal catalogs and every element of the finite compiled vector; row conclusions retain their Reg-compiler proof authority. Seal mathematical obligations remain in the kernel-checked Reg contract; the report emits no state partitions, primitive statistics or role buckets. Companion constants are immutable report views, not installed declarations. These expression calculations do not provide a route for evaluating a computed top-level contract input. Decode failures identify the owner, declaration and reason.

Report artifacts are isolated by compiler traces, utility inputs and the report format; configuration identity belongs to the aggregate. Raw enrollment inputs are compiler products of their original owners. The report records compiled contract inventory and structural decode diagnostics; it does not persist a checked plan or verdict authority. Plan identities contain neither source-file byte hashes nor manifest-derived values. Judge implementation and rule changes retain historical report artifacts; compiler input changes select reassessment.

Registration ownership determines the evidence domain. The reader collects each selected owner's current producer evidence and its inline claims, then performs A5.4's strict join and certificate checks. Owner addresses are inspected to route claims before their payloads are validated; either a selected registration source or a selected registration-module address retains a record, so inconsistent addresses cannot hide a selected claim. Other modules cannot overlay a selected registration; their unrelated registrations are outside its evidence domain. Outside the selection, registration payloads are not read as evidence, judged or used as a failure cause at the report loader, evidence reader or rule layer. Missing, malformed, unresolved, stale or incompatible template evidence for unselected registrations cannot fail admission.

Whole-artifact integrity remains global: canonical UTF-8 JSON bytes and report structure, complete module membership, source path/hash structure, declaration material content addresses, and the bundle provenance and attestation checks in A14.9 still apply. These checks establish the integrity of the producer artifact consumed by the repository; they do not validate its unselected registrations. `RawLeanReportArtifact` retains template payloads without interpreting them, and `InformationTemplateEvidence` projects the selected owners before applying the strict template reader. Global artifact validation must not turn an unselected registration's template evidence into an admission obligation.

The closed finding-name allowlist for this obligation is exactly the following table. Evidence-reader diagnostics are message details under `DTR-Evidence`, not additional finding names.

| Finding | Effect | Judgment |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `DTR-Undeclared` | Observe | A selected occurrence remains `undeclared` or lacks `escapeFrom` / `continuation` after joining claims. |
| `DTR-Evidence` | Observe | Selected producer evidence is missing, malformed, unresolved, inconsistent or lacks a valid declared-template certificate; current-file digest comparison is not a DTR-Evidence check. |
| `DTR-Unregistered` | Observe | A new public theorem has no validated four-slot registration. |
| `DTR-Declared` | Observe | A selected occurrence is `declared_validated` with all four slots; reports `escape_from`, `escape_continues`, `bridge_kind`. |

In the four-slot rule below, `escapeFrom` identifies `ObjectDomainArena.Domain` for an ordinary object-domain bridge and the finite arena's `State` otherwise. An object-domain bridge retains the finite arena for E1-E8 without identifying its `State` with the source domain. This check establishes definitional type identity and occurrence in the source statement; it does not establish that the readout function depends on an object of that type.

To be reported `DTR-Declared`, each selected occurrence declares four slots: (1) `escapeFrom := some <term>` supplies a constant or binder type occurring in the raw elaborated statement and definitionally identifies what the closed arena's State represents; absent occurrence gives `dtr.escape_from_absent`, wrong State gives `dtr.escape_from_state`. (2) `readout := some <T args>` passes the existing enrolled-template E1–E8 and exact extraction checks. (3) The realization bridge retains exact statement/arena/primitive-bundle checks; `EscapeRecord.EscapePrimitiveRealization` proves only statement ⇒ Law and requires valid variation and sensitivity (`dtr.forward_bridge_requires_sensitivity`), while the legacy equivalence and exact native-Law forms remain available. (4) `continuation` supplies a named `EscapeRecord.EscapeResidualWitness` value or an `EscapeRecord.EscapeResidualEmpty` proof for a named `LayerChain` over that same arena (`dtr.escape_continues_arena` rejects another arena), or `.unknown`. Membership is kernel-checked through the witness field; the judge checks types and names without evaluating states, chains or certificates. The `.unknown` constructor is a truthful unknown-residual marker without a certificate: it proves neither undecidability nor inexhaustibility and cannot replace the realization bridge, any required variation/sensitivity proof, template application or binding evidence. When all other required evidence validates, an explicitly open residual is still a complete honest registration.

A D5 `utility` declaration with `basis=refutes` exempts only its result theorem from escape registration, registration-completion criteria and `DTR-Unregistered`. The result selector uses the utility resolver: exactly one included compiled declaration in the designated module has that final name component; its actual full name supplies the exemption, including an unqualified name or any namespace. Missing or ambiguous selectors exempt nothing. Other new public theorems in the same module retain their obligations. SL-031 still requires the result to prove the literal closed `Not claim`, with the standard kernel and axiom checks.

Registrations have the compiler type `Contract.Registration …` with typed fields for the target, realization, readout, variation, sensitivity, escape origin and continuation. Templates use `Contract.TemplateEnrollment`; declaration inputs contain no judge code. The delta rule imposes the four-slot obligation on selected occurrences. Valid registration completion requires at least one complete verified registration per new public D5 theorem/lemma in scope, with its compiled proof and current binding evidence in the mirrored Reg change; registration work follows the delivery rule above. Reuse a lawful existing template; if none applies, add a meaningful parameterized content template with shared enrollment in `Reg/Support`. No forced template fit, judge relaxation or bind-only D5 wrapper is allowed. Multiple registrations on different arenas coexist under their original occurrence keys; there is no completeness or naturalness judgment. A selected Reg module with an empty inventory still requires current producer evidence; new public D5 theorems use the separate mirror lookup. Other admission obligations remain independent.

**A5.6 Declaration package and dependency direction.** Escape declarations are metadata about mathematics and live apart from it. The D5 package holds mathematics and the content-level arenas, templates and record types; it requires Mathlib only and imports neither a judge module nor a declaration module. The judge is two Lake packages, each with its own lakefile and manifest: the interface package (typed contract structures, inductives and sort-valued index families, with their D5 mathematical dependencies) and the implementation package (E1–E8, gates, assessment and evidence), the implementation requiring the interface. Declarations live in a third Lake package, `Reg`, with its own lakefile and manifest, requiring the D5 package and the judge interface only. The implementation and Reg both depend on the interface. Implementation tests and analysis consumers live in a separate downstream Lake host requiring Reg and the implementation; no edge points from D5 to the judge or to Reg.

The production reader uses `RawArtifacts.Store` for every module and does not build an Environment, initialize extensions, invoke elaboration, Meta, the type checker or the kernel. It computes report rows from compiler declarations, constant references and contract constructor trees. A safe reference may be followed within the bounded raw reader; a value that requires evaluation, a missing part, an unknown format or a read failure produces a named `contract.decode_failed` or raw-artifact error and has no fallback path. Mathematical obligations are checked by the Reg compiler; the report consumes their compiled fields without constructing or rechecking proofs. Utility refutations compare the raw claim type `Prop` and result type `Not claim` literally, without unfolding, reduction or a Reg certificate. Warm report reuse is selected by compiler dependency traces, utility inputs and the report format identifier.

A batch plans a fixed, import-closed base consisting of modules used by more than one target (including utility claims and enrollment imports). The planning pass retains only detached names and input flags and releases its module parts. Each target loads its remaining compiler parts into a separate store. Constant and owner indexes use Lean’s existing staged map: the shared base retains its hash table and target and companion insertions use the persistent hash stage, without copying the imported bucket array. Module positions and provenance classes are compiler-fact indexes extended in dependency order during loading; assessment contexts read those indexes directly. They contain no plans, verdicts or freshness state. After material acknowledgements and report-row flush, the target frame destroys its assessment, expression/axiom caches and row; the store drops all maps and metadata before its regions are explicitly freed in reverse order. Cross-target generated-name keys own their strings. The base remains fixed until process exit; it is reported separately by `STRATALINT_INSPECTOR_PROFILE`, along with target-region/root counts and boundary RSS. Native batch packing, aggregation and aggregate validation consume one module at a time; only membership, origin, material-address and offset indexes persist. Batch size remains 100; neither the report format nor reuse conditions include these observations. The downstream C# admission reader consumes one completed report and retains its per-module template payloads and material archive objects; it is outside the report-production target loop.

The declarations for the theorems of `D5/<path>.lean` are written in `Reg/D5/<path>.lean`, the same path shape; catalog roots, seals and shared enrollments are `Reg` modules that import those leaves. A declaration names a theorem of an imported module and uses the four-slot grammar of A5.5 unchanged. Reg contract types index original mathematical obligations by their target, arena, actual realization, primitive bundle and catalog indices. Lean checks their proofs during Reg compilation, including finite variation and slot sensitivity, source-family obligations, and seal lowering/triviality, closure membership, catalog redundancy and retained kernel collisions. Seal contracts and reports contain no count, state-partition, primitive or role-bucket statistics. The judge binds the arena, catalog identity and complete ordered unit vector to the registrations in the import closure. Finite seal catalogs require arena nondegeneracy and bundle nonemptiness for their exact vectors; unsealed finite registrations retain their existing scope without an added nondegeneracy requirement. Checked per-readout sensitivity implies output nontriviality and needs no duplicate field. Missing, unknown, absent and unsupported submissions remain representable without asserting the obligation. The report consumes these compiled fields; it neither synthesizes nor rechecks those mathematical proofs. The report records the compiled input inventory and emits named unresolved diagnostics when the structural reader cannot decode an input; it does not claim an assessment certificate for an undecoded value. Compiled inventories identify typed declarations independently of their source spelling, declaration modifiers, suffixes, options or elaboration commands. Constructor trees and constant references are decoded as data; values requiring computation fail by name. Source writing rules and a Reg self-assessment text scan do not participate. Every module in the entire Reg package, including additional libraries and executables, has a transitive import closure free of implementation modules. The structural gate also checks package requires and defaultTargets. Judge implementation changes rebuild zero Reg/D5 modules; registration reassessment follows the changed compiler dependency closure; unaffected module artifacts retain their bytes. There is no manifest of module names, no additional cache and no registration-specific freshness mechanism.

`DeclaredTemplateBindingRule` stays delta-only and observe-only (A5.5). Its selection covers changed `Reg` modules; a newly authored public theorem/lemma declaration in a changed selected D5 source module is looked up in its mirror `Reg` module by path. The resulting `DTR-Unregistered`/`DTR-Undeclared` readings remain warning-only for admission. Registration work and delivery follow CLAUDE.md §3.9’s audit and linked-issue rules; the evidence standard in A5.5 and runtime assessment remain unchanged. Helper declarations authored in `Reg`, `Interface` or `Impl` do not create another D5 selection or recursive Reg/D5 audit target; their existing kernel, `sorry` and axiom checks remain. `Reg` modules are report modules of `lean-report-inputs.json`; the report follows A14.9's compiler traces, utility inputs and report format.

For author delivery, compile all Reg sources actually retained by that delivery and retain successful audit proofs and binding evidence. If no valid Reg source can be delivered, do not invent a mirror or require a nonexistent build target. Remove this attempt's failed audit source additions and imports if they would break the normal build; do not remove pre-existing or successful sources to hide failures. Report the actual obstacle under A5.5. Every retained Reg source keeps its existing build, report, kernel, `sorry` and axiom obligations.

D5 mathematics imports neither judge nor Reg. The SL-001 import-edge rule rejects a new edge,
restricts any protected-base debt to a shrinking set and judges the full tree once that set is empty.
The current D5→judge/Reg edge set is empty. Reg registration inputs use only interface contract types;
report-time discovery reads their compiled values through the single typed-input path.

**Reg package admission**

The interface contains contract types and sort-valued index families with their D5 mathematical dependencies; it contains no recorder, environment extension, command syntax or judge elaborator. Reg declarations implement those types with ordinary Lean definitions and kernel-checked proof terms. The implementation reads exact typed heads and constant data from serialized compiler parts and emits structural rows or named decode diagnostics. Contract proof fields are consumed as compiled data. Report-time calculations reconstruct enrollment plans, companion views, joined assessments and structural certificates without creating an Environment, elaborating expressions, installing declarations or constructing or rechecking mathematical proofs. Report rows and receipts are transport artifacts, never importable authority. Implementation changes rebuild no Reg modules, and an intact report is reused; only new or changed registration inputs are assessed. Interface changes require atomic migration of all uses, deletion of the old representation and compiler-driven rebuilding and reassessment of affected Reg modules, with no historical compatibility.

`Reg/lakefile.toml` and `Reg/lake-manifest.json` are program artifacts on the judge admission plane. An empty `Reg` library remains lawful; it requires no dummy module. Its library has `srcDir = ".."`, roots `["Reg"]`, and globs `["Reg.+"]`. Outputs use `../.lake/build/reg`, and dependencies share `../.lake/packages` with the repository root.

Before cache ensure or writer admission, and in current-tree structural configuration validation (SL-015), the harness reads both manifests as data. The multiset of git tuples `(name, url, rev, inputRev, subDir, configFile, manifestFile)` must equal the root manifest, and every Reg git package is inherited. Reg has exactly two non-inherited path entries: `trureturing` at `..` with `lakefile.toml` and `leanInspectorInterface` at `../tools/lean-inspector-interface` with `lakefile.toml`; each uses `lake-manifest.json`. A path package listed by the root manifest that Reg does not require itself may reach Reg through `trureturing`; it is then admitted at most once, as an inherited path entry at `../<root dir>`. The current root manifest has no path packages. `packagesDir` is exactly `../.lake/packages`. Missing either required package file (including both files absent), malformed input, or any disagreement fails closed before Lake may materialize shared dependencies.

The only declaration source families are `Reg/D5/**/*.lean`, `Reg/Support/**/*.lean`, and `Reg/Catalogs/**/*.lean`. These are content-plane executable declaration data with supporting kernel proofs, verified by `lean-build` and `lean-inspector`. The suffix after `Reg/` for a D5 mirror must satisfy the existing D5 formal path grammar and controlled stratum/domain relation. Support and catalog paths consist of CamelCase module segments. Package configs and the three source families have disjoint FILEMAP entries. These exact three content data patterns may be empty while both package configuration files are tracked and separately registered as judge programs; other empty committed patterns retain population checks. The data-verifier registry binds `lean-build` to `tools/scripts/worktree/lean-cache-run.sh` and `lean-inspector` to `tools/lean-inspector/inspect.sh`, requiring each implementation to exist and resolve to one program entry.

A Reg source is a report member, with module name obtained by removing `.lean` and replacing `/` with `.`. Report membership does not confer mathematical identity: Reg is not a stratum or a GID theory. Reg sources carry no Scribe, GID, canonical Lean header, mathematical deposit, or computational utility (SL-031) duties. They are excluded from D5 theorem candidates and mathematical truth projections. Their supporting proofs remain subject to kernel, sorry and axiom checks; a mirrored frontier path grants no exception.

Registration-only D5 addresses removed during relocation are retired by explicit `ledger-align --retire-registration D5/<path>.lean` requests. Repeat the option for each removed address. The source must already be absent, and no current source or report import, including a Reg import, may refer to it. Duplicate or conflicting selectors, unknown retirement members, existing source modules, non-D5 retirement paths, and missing pinned sources without explicit retirement fail before publication. `--selector D5/<path>.lean` selects a retained module for re-pinning; `--add` registers only the requested new Closed modules. Each selected retained module needs its own scoped report, including its compiled imports and utility inputs, and only its own state fragment is written when its content changes. Retirement does not implicitly select or rewrite retained descendants and does not require an event dependency DAG or a whole-ledger closure check.

The writer removes only the explicitly requested registration-only state fragments in rollback-protected publication; it writes no action record or retirement tombstone. Repeating retirement of an absent member fails as an unknown member and writes nothing. State-fragment deletion remains subject to the existing deletion judgment; this selector supplies no general unfreezing authorization. History remains in Git.

The retirement selector asserts a registration-only relocation; the writer cannot infer that classification or proof preservation from module statement pins. Content reconciliation must establish the pre/post declaration mapping, preserve mathematical statement/proof bytes in retained D5 owners, account for statement-id changes caused by extracted metadata, and rebuild generated Reg proofs under Lean. It must also migrate or remove the retired addresses' Scribe documents and coverage references. Existing Scribe validation (`DescribeRepositoryValidator`/`DeclarationCatalog`) owns document checks in the final current/delta delivery. Coverage references are reconciled through the explicit target digestion commands; admission does not scan all digestion edges. Ledger-align does not load a second document/coverage inventory or certify those checks; successful retirement alone is not completed content reconciliation.

The report selection includes optional `Reg/**/*.lean` and requires `Reg/lakefile.toml` and `Reg/lake-manifest.json`. An empty Reg adds no module rows. Module report traces bind the compiler closure, utility input and report format; configuration identity belongs to aggregation, and whole-report receipts bind the registered report-module and configuration inputs.

`make lean` without explicit targets demands root, Impl, Reg and the downstream test host defaults in dependency order. Root `lakefile.toml` requires Mathlib and owns `Trureturing`/D5. Interface requires the root package for the mathematical dependencies of its contract types and owns `LeanInformationAuditInterface`; its outputs are in `.lake/build/lean-inspector/interface`. Impl requires the root package and Interface, owns the default `LeanInformationAudit` library, the `InformationSourceFixture` library and the `reportInspector`/`inputDiscovery` executables, and builds in `.lake/build/lean-inspector/producer`. Reg requires root and Interface, owns only the default `Reg` library, and builds in `.lake/build/reg`. The downstream host `tools/lean-inspector-reg` requires Reg and Impl, owns the default `LeanInformationAuditRegTests` library, the explicit `LeanInformationAuditRegAnalysis` library and the `compiledJudgeTests` executable, and builds in `.lake/build/lean-inspector/reg`. Source files under `tools/lean-inspector` can belong to this downstream host; physical directory placement does not determine package ownership. All four auxiliary packages share the root `.lake/packages` directory.

Explicit `LEAN_TARGETS` retain their scope: Reg module/package targets go to Reg; `LeanInformationAuditRegTests`, `LeanInformationAuditRegAnalysis` and `regInspector` package targets go to the downstream host; judge and Interface module/package targets go to Impl; D5 and other root targets go to root. A failure in any demanded build fails the entrypoint. `make lean-report` uses the downstream workspace at `tools/lean-inspector-reg`: the Impl report facet produces registered report modules and the entrypoint selects its default program targets unless the caller supplies the sorted JSON `STRATALINT_LEAN_BUILD_TARGETS` override. Successful whole-report reuse still builds the selected targets, with no program build when that selection is empty. After `make lean-cache-ensure`, package-cwd Lake builds are supported. The cache reader/writer validates shared Reg/root manifest agreement before either supported entrypoint lets Lake materialize dependencies. Lake imports own compile invalidation.

Repository root and Lake package ownership are distinct. Judge source IO locates the nearest ancestor containing the root `lakefile.toml` and `lean-toolchain` at runtime. Facets resolve the same root from the current workspace package and keep report coordination and artifacts at `<repository>/.lake/build/lean-inspector`; compiler outputs remain owned by their Lake packages, including `<repository>/.lake/build/reg`. Native commands run at the repository root with Lake's toolchain environment. Evidence retains logical repository-relative source paths, with Interface sources under `tools/lean-inspector-interface`. No build-host absolute root is embedded in an olean, so copied artifacts support repository relocation and roots containing spaces. Compiler dependency traces, utility inputs and the report format govern module report reuse; the whole-report receipt binds registered report modules, configuration and execution inputs.

A sealing root supplies a typed `Contract.RootCatalog` with exact expected occurrences, an independent source snapshot, retained baseline rows and an optional companion prefix, together with a `Contract.Seal`. Reg/Catalogs/**/RootCatalog.lean owns exactly one RootCatalog and no Seal; SealedCatalog.lean owns exactly one of each. Root IDs equal the owning module. Every other Reg module is ordinary and owns neither structural entry. Requirements come from the loaded Reg import closure and canonical source paths before discovery; expected rows come only from RootCatalog, with no fallback. Expected membership, statement identities and contributor modules are compared with registry output, and source snapshots must retain baseline rows in all three coordinates. Independent expectations are never derived from the registry being checked. Catalog and seal inputs are decoded from readable constructor trees; computed values and unsupported layouts terminate decoding with a named failure. Catalog membership remains optional for individual structural inventory rows.

Registration bindings belong to their Reg occurrence owner. Delta selects changed Reg owners plus exact mirrors and explicitly imported variants for newly public D5 theorems; it does not scan other modules for claims. All four DTR findings remain Observe, and unselected malformed registration payloads remain outside the evidence domain. RawLeanReportArtifact checks module source hashes against the candidate snapshot. Module report reuse is decided by compiler dependency traces, utility inputs and the report format; whole-report reuse additionally checks its registered inputs and sealed bundle bytes.

**A6 Lean 声明命名**(承 mathlib)定理 `snake_case`(主语_性质);类型 `CamelCase`;命名空间=路径;上游通用层居 `Metallic.*` 且以参数 D 陈述(H10)。

**A7 任务码** `THEORY"-T"<四位>`(D5-T0042,永久不复用);工单块只要求包含 `TASK D5-T0042`,块内为自由散文:
```lean
/-- TASK D5-T0042
    可自由记录目标、线索、失败战史与后续条件。 -/
```

**A8 常数码** `D5/E/values--json` 是 Scribe 对 Lean 常数定义与外置计算实例的 canonical 投影;十四个正式定义唯一住在 `D5/S3/Constants/Values.lean`,计算参数作为数据住在 `Golden/values-kernels.toml`,程序集只保留 schema、fail-closed loader、计算核与 writer。根 schema 为 `{attestation,constants,schema_version}`,其中 `constants` 按 `id` 严格排序且恰含十四项。每项含 `{id,lean_gid,lean_statement_sha256,status,definition,formula,refs,value,decimal,error,exact_value,method,provenance,reference_value,reference_error,comparison,open_reason,kernel_receipts}`;`provenance` 必须等于该项具体 `lean_gid`,共享 attestation 的 `provenance` 必须列全十四个 GID,不得再写裸 `Lean`。`status∈{emitted,registered-open}`,未足以转译者须 value/error 为 null、收据为空并给 open_reason,不得以 appendix 观测值补洞;误差条为最坏项负责。含 libm 的浮点投影固定发射十四位小数(value/window result 按最近值,error 按该十进制网格向上取整),量化位数与策略须入 kernel receipt,raw kernel 参数不因旧观测值调谐。SL-018 机器绑定 GID 唯一存在、声明 `kind=def`、标准三公理闭包及 inspector statement SHA-256;因这些定义是 `noncomputable ℝ`,十进制结果不冒充 Lean kernel 求值,attestation 必须以 `numeric_binding=not-kernel-evaluated:noncomputable-real` 明示该边界。修订史只归 git,工作树不保留历史兼容层。
**A8.1 复合常数**(验收补丁):非标量常数用点分子键——`D5/delta.mean`、`D5/delta.amp`、`D5/delta.period`(δ-亏项之形态学三元);家族共享 `D5/delta._meta` 记法。
**A8.2 关系式校验**(验收补丁):派生常数须声明 `formula` 字段(如 `formula: "2*sqrt(5)*T0 + (137-61*sqrt(5))/24", refs: {T0: D5/T0}`);其语法独立于 GID 字符集,严格采用 A2.1 的 ASCII 算术表达式文法,且只能引用 `refs` 已绑定键;CI 以语法树重算关系式,超出合成误差条即黄牌 issue——**验收穿行首捕:c₁↔T₀ 差 3.6×10⁻⁶(即账本在册之 T₀ 双法仲裁残差),此机制使挂案永不隐身。**

**A9 假设码与升级级联** `Assumptions.<CamelCase>` + REGISTRY(active/proven/refuted)。**级联律**:假设被证 ⟹ 结构获实例 ⟹ lint 全库扫 `(h:Assumptions.X)` ⟹ 自动开除氢任务;被反证 ⟹ errata 级联,依赖证书全体降回 Frontier。

**A10 实验码** `THEORY"-X"<四位>`;spec YAML `{id,hypothesis,method,budget,tolerance,target_stratum,outputs:observation|refinement|negative}`。

**A11 论文码** `THEORY"-P"<三位>`;recipe `{id,decls:[GID],blueprint:[GID],evidence:[GID],narrative_order,venue}`;冻结 = 内容哈希+tag+DOI。

**A12 文献码** bibkey `<姓><年><首词>`;根桶 `Library/notes/<bibkey>.md` 或容量分裂后的受控疆域桶 `Library/<Domain>/<bibkey>.md` 之 canonical YAML front matter schema 为 `{bibkey,authors,year,title,doi:DOI|null,url?:HTTPS,claim,strata_touched:[GID],license,triage:anchor|task(GID)|rejected(理由)}`。`authors` 是非空规范署名串,`year` 是四位整数;文件名、`bibkey` 必须逐字一致,bibkey 在全 L 平面唯一,分裂桶名必须已入 `Meta/domains.yaml`;DOI 用统一 typed parser 验语法并按大小写无关键全 L 平面唯一;`task(GID)` 必须解析 canonical GID 并参与悬空检查。authors/year/title/DOI/URL 只在该 note 定义,Describe 与发射投影只携对应的 `D5/L/<bibkey>` 或 `D5/L/<Domain>/<bibkey>` typed 引用及 `lit/<bibkey>` 锚,禁内联复制文献元数据。`literature-attested` provenance 必携可解析 note 的 `LibraryNoteRef`,其学术发射至少携 DOI 或稳定 HTTPS URL 之一，二者可并存，并由 note 投影 author-year-title-source citation，并存时依次列 DOI 与 URL；无 DOI 时写 `doi: null` 加 `url`；凡给出的 `url` 均通过 `System.Uri` 验证绝对 HTTPS、无凭据、规范字节；被引用 note 的 locator 段须包含其声明的每个来源值，DOI 按大小写无关比较，URL 按字节比较；URL 的持久性由来源核实承担，离线语法检查不声称证明其长期可达性;悬空 L/GID 或 citation 元数据缺位即红;硬门全程离线,DOI 在线解析与标题一致性只列 Observe。

**A13 编年码** `D5/C/<YYYY-MM-DD>/<slug>`;物理路径由 A2 双射至 `Chronicle/<YYYY>/<MM>/<DD>-<slug>.md`;LEGACY.md 存旧评注号(27.x)映射;当前条目只保证当下正确,改删历史由 git 查证。

**A14 版本码与冻结状态表** 版辑 tag `E<n>`+Zenodo DOI;工具链钉版。环境升级的政策要求是同一 PR 原子更新 `lean-toolchain`/`lakefile.{toml,lean}`/`lake-manifest.json`,并通过 `make lean` 执行全量 clean 构建;强制无缓存 clean build 不是现役机器谓词。

**A14.1 冻结成员。** 冻结成员是显式登记的 Closed 模块子集。`Golden/Frozen/state/<模块路径>.lean.json` 存在即冻结;Closed 而没有状态片是合法的未冻结状态。deposit 登记所请求的新 Closed 模块,不把全部 Closed 模块自动登记为冻结成员。状态表是冻结成员资格与陈述 pin 的唯一真源;数学真值仍只由 Lean 声明、证明项及其 axiom 闭包承载。删除状态片与改 pin 同为合法的成员变更:该模块退出冻结成员,其已验证陈述的数学真值不变,历史由 git 查证;删除不产生判词,合法性与模块源码是否仍在无关。此后 SL-008 不再检查该模块,重新登记按首次冻结判定。

**A14.2 状态片记录。** 每个冻结模块恰有一个文件。对 canonical 模块路径 `M.lean`,状态片路径恰为 `Golden/Frozen/state/M.lean.json`,移除固定前缀和 `.json` 后缀即可反解模块路径,两者双射。文件为单行规范 JSON 加恰一个 LF;对象闭键恰为 `{declarations,statement_id}`。`declarations` 包含当前 report 中该模块全部 `include_in_statement` 声明,每项闭键恰为 `{declaration_name_key,kind}`,按名称键、种类的 Ordinal 顺序排列且唯一,不得省略、增加或重复声明。

`statement_id` 是 `sha256:<64 lowercase hex>` 形的现行模块陈述摘要。其算法沿用 `FrozenContentAddress.ComputeModuleStatementId` 与 `CanonicalStatementWriter.WriteModule`:对 `module-statement-v1` 的规范前像作现行域分离哈希,前像含模块路径与全部参与陈述声明的名称、种类、逐声明陈述摘要。逐声明摘要用于计算模块 pin,不写入状态片;状态片也不存任何形式的前置依赖、事件字段、时间或动作历史。

**A14.3 模块写入。** 对模块 M 的登记或重新钉定仅写 M 自己的状态片;规范内容相同即不写。writer 不改写其他模块的记录,不因 import 关系连带重钉下游,不要求整库报告,不做全账闭包检查。所需证据是 M 的当前范围报告,包括其编译 import 与 utility 输入;缺失或无效的所需证据仍拒绝写入。多模块请求逐模块遵守同一写集合边界。`--retire-registration` 的显式请求、源已移除、无残留 import、声明与证明保全及 Scribe/coverage 清理义务见 A5.6,不依赖事件依赖图。

writer 不提供事件追加、事件替换、`Reattest`、`Revoke`、`Supersede`、`EnvironmentRecoordinate` 动作,不提供 `ledger-append` 别名或 `--from-accepted` 物化入口。

**A14.4 依赖与读取。** 模块依赖由 Lean 源码 import 关系承担,通过 `LeanImportAdjacency.BuildFromSources` 一类源码读取现算,不在冻结记录中持久化。deposit、重新钉定、cover/消化评估、冻结声明索引、mathlib 升级授权、SL-008、truth-export 与 truth-graph 只读取状态表及所需的当前 report/源码,不读取事件身份或事件图。cover 核对既有覆盖目标时,要求其模块 pin 等于当前 report 重算的模块陈述摘要;声明解析仍使用当前 report。mathlib 升级授权的旧侧声明根取自受保护基线的状态片声明清单,新侧取自候选范围报告,不重建或重验旧 Lean 环境。该源码比较的保证限于仓内源码等价。无法定位私有声明源码归属时,仅在其类型化私有模块前缀精确对应完整仓内 import 闭包中的模块、其余可定位声明的来源与种类核对通过,且根及完整闭包的每个文件在受保护基线与候选原始字节均相同时,比较完整闭包的源码哈希及根的完整声明名称键/种类清单,不擦除证明、归一化声明根或按声明名筛选依赖。原始字节相同但因缩进声明而无法提取的仓内文件及所有传递导入它的受检根,也使用该完整闭包比较;此路径中任一字节变化均拒绝。其余可解析的仓内命题及其定义依赖仍按源码提取比较。全局源码解析失败、闭包源码缺失或其它提取失败均拒绝。真实 toolchain/mathlib pin bump 后仓外同名常量、定义体、实例或名称解析变化的一般逻辑等价仍未验证,不能以源码指纹相同或新环境 kernel 通过冒领该保证,保留 `open(EXTERNAL-PIN-BUMP-SEMANTIC-EQUIVALENCE)`。外部模块归属、外部 rev 是否不可变地指向所声称 source、未知 library root alias 及非 git/path/浮动依赖的语义稳定性仍属 `open(EXTERNAL-IMPORT-SEMANTICS)`。

旧侧删除的 `T.toCtorIdx` 根仅可通过可选 `ledger-reanchor-mathlib --base REV --old-report PATH` 见证作局部规范化。旧报告只提供内存中的完整 included 声明摘要投影,不作为当前 Lean 证据;先用完整投影重算模块 pin 并核对受保护声明清单,再独立构造并哈希精确材料 `statement-v1(uparams=[],type=ep(bd,ec(T,[]),ec(ns(n0,3:Nat),[])),value=ec(T.ctorIdx,[]))`,认证旧声明名称键、`def` 种类及直接别名内容。名称按类型化 Lean name key 解析,不得由显示名或字符串前缀推定来源。`T` 与 `T.ctorIdx` 必须在两侧保留为 `inductive` 与 `def` 根,`T` 在两侧均精确对应同一模块中的仓内 inductive 源声明,包括私有模块前缀;两侧不得有显式同名 index 源声明,候选完整声明表不得仍含被删除的 alias。仅去掉获证旧 alias 后,根清单必须精确相等,并继续通过普通源码来源/种类检查及完整仓内 import 闭包原始源码哈希比较,包含证明、meta 及生成器代码。其它根、有效 pin 变化、全部 retained 候选声明的标准公理检查及写入失败回滚不变;缺失或不匹配见证不取得此特例。该见证不要求旧材料归档或旧源码 SHA 一致,不认证显示名、imports、公理等未被声明/模块承诺绑定的旧报告字段,不认证生成历史或新旧编译体一般语义等价;新冻结 pin 仍来自真实完整候选报告。

**A14.5 准入。** `Golden/Frozen/accepted/` 不是 FILEMAP 登记路径;任何被跟踪文件出现在该目录下,均由 required 检查中的现有 FILEMAP 未分类路径判词拒绝,不另设路径禁令规则。严格状态 loader 只接受 A14.2 的闭键形,拒绝只含 `statement_id` 的记录及其他非闭键形;旧工具写出的不合规形不得与当前状态片并存。事件与状态片配对、改动事件 pin 核对两项过渡检查不存在。

SL-008 检查当前树中的每个状态片:路径与 schema 合规,对应模块存在且为 Closed,pin 等于当前 report 重算的模块陈述摘要,声明清单与当前 report 一致。该检查范围不因 writer 的模块写集合缩小。状态片 pin 相对受保护基线变化时,以 Observe 点名模块及旧、新 pin;状态片删除不产生判词(A14.1)。

**A14.6 并发合并。** 同一模块的两份不同规范记录位于同一路径的同一行,Git 三方合并报冲突;内容相同的并发写入正常合流。不同模块的记录更新写集合不相交,一个模块的重新钉定不写其他模块的记录。路径双射与严格 schema 保证每个冻结模块只有一份记录;记录不含前置记录地址,不存在悬空的前置记录引用。这些性质只约束冻结状态片,不承诺 PR 中其他源码改动之间无冲突。

**A14.7 对外投影与发布。** `Generated/truth-export.v1.json` 与 `Generated/truth-graph.v1.json` 的现有字段及含义保留,由状态表、当前 report 与源码 import 现算,不合成虚拟事件。truth-release 的发布方言见 A22;已发布的 release 不改动。

**A14.8 TOWER 引导边界。** TOWER 不保存或验证 `genesis_event` 的事件存在性见证。bootstrap 保留其 commit/PR 外部信任根边界及 `ASSUMED-UNVERIFIED` 状态,不以状态摘要替代事件见证。

**A14.9 Inspector-owned report artifacts.** `lean-report-inputs.json` is the sole FILEMAP-registered report input manifest. Its strict JSON schema selects report modules, Inspector sources, configuration and the `lean-report` / `scribe-content` producer scopes through explicit include/exclude patterns. The optional `dependency_sources` path set registers additional local Lean sources that may occur in reported modules' native compiler dependencies; absence declares no additional local inputs. It is an allowed source inventory, not a producer compatibility fingerprint. Required registrations without files, malformed or duplicate fields, unsafe paths and unsupported schema identifiers fail with a named diagnostic. The manifest contains no judge semantic version. Module evidence and origins contain no global judge version. The report format identifier determines strict wire acceptance, input coordinates, all module report traces and whole-report receipts. Extraction semantics or report format changes update that identifier and re-extract every module. Judge implementation and rule changes retain historical reports; changed registration and contract inputs are reassessed through their compiler dependency closures. Missing required evidence or old wire shape rejects with no older-version reader. Neither judge source bytes nor a producer implementation digest defines evidence validity. The reader `tools/scripts/report/lean-report-selection.py` expands only those registrations, with no MSBuild evaluation or script/call-graph discovery. The existing Scribe projection and describe checks consume the registered scope, including deleted paths.

For admission, these template-evidence requirements apply only to A5.5's selected registration owners and the producers of their selected binding claims.

The pinned `tools/lean-inspector` Lake package owns per-module complete row/material bundles and their aggregate under root `.lake/build/lean-inspector`. Native module report facets trace the report format identifier, source bytes, private/server/import/transitive compiler artifacts, each module's authoritative utility record and external claim artifact closure; registered configuration identity belongs to aggregation.
