The Inspector executable must compile successfully through native Lake dependencies, but its job trace and registered producer implementation bytes are not report regeneration dependencies. Lean/Python/C#/script or Inspector Lake implementation edits reuse valid module and aggregate artifacts. Contract edits rebuild and reassess affected Reg compiler closures; the same delivery migrates all uses and deletes old paths. Input membership uses the exact Contract.Discovery type heads and compiler owner rules, never module prefixes, path lists, source tokens or report-array contents. Discovery errors and illegal contracts are named failures, not empty membership. The small compiler input fact stores only type/owner/name projections and traces the compiler closure. Utility input is generated once per invocation. Current module membership determines aggregation, including removals. Native Lake trace and artifact reuse select misses. The Inspector asks Lake through its native `noBuild` artifact operation, preserves each module's input/output traces, and composes the selected work through a shared native job. Expected internal misses become continuations; other failures and a caller's actual `--no-build` propagate. Batching selected misses shares import work without a second invalidation engine or per-miss blocking promise wait. Ordinary root defaults and audit builds remain required. Like an olean, a module report artifact is validated completely once, when it is produced; an artifact that fails is not written. Reuse is decided by its Lake trace alone, which includes the module's and its claims' compiler artifacts, so a changed olean invalidates exactly the dependent reports; a traced or cache-restored artifact is not validated again. Aggregation concatenates the traced rows in registered order and moves their deflated materials unchanged. Publication of the aggregate and reuse under a matching receipt check the bundle envelope and input currency, not the rows.

本地 `make lean-report` 通过显式参数选择 `fetch-or-fail`。完整种子包含报告、四个 sidecar 和成功的整份报告收据。种子缺失、不完整、不可读或收据 `inputs.report_format` 不符时，进入既有受限恢复路径；具体范围见 A22：GitHub Actions 跳过生产 Release 下载，linked worktree 要求 warm-main/reseed，本地主检出才可从 Release 恢复私有 build。恢复沿既有 resolved-mathlib/shared-platform 分区与完整 Release cache key；检查、恢复和消费持有既有私有缓存写锁。规范种子仍缺失或不兼容时，`LEAN_REPORT_CACHE_INCOMPATIBLE` 以 exit 4 在报告提取或选定程序构建前失败。格式匹配允许注册输入变化后的正常增量，不要求整份收据输入相等。`make lean-report REBUILD_REPORT_CACHE=1` 绕过整份报告复用并允许报告构建，但仍可复用原生模块产物，不等于历史登记无缓存重评或强制 clean。普通 required CI 的报告入口显式选择 `LEAN_REPORT_CACHE_MISS_POLICY=fetch-or-fail`；独立 manual/PR/自动 Actions producer 与 Release publisher 选择 `reuse-or-build`，后者不执行 Release recovery。环境变量不能替代入口显式策略。直接 `inspect.sh` 默认 `reuse-or-build`，可显式选 `--cache-miss-policy fetch-or-fail`。不存在判官语义兼容版本或旧格式适配器。

Lean-cache publication invokes the existing current Inspector entry once before packing. The Inspector entry builds its selected program targets and the native `:report` facet in one invocation on a report miss; required input, build or complete report validation failure prevents publication, including when a run snapshot already exists. The root buildDir archive carries native Inspector artifacts and the canonical report/material/origin/attestation outputs. Publication retains the resolved mathlib/platform partition and run/attempt snapshots; restore accepts the newest snapshot of the same resolved mathlib from any partition in the host's shared seed family (macOS and Linux arm64, `SHARED_SEED_PLATFORMS` in `tools/scripts/worktree/lean_cache.py`), because Lake retraces the archive's native artifacts per platform and rebuilds them while reusing its `.olean`/`.ilean`/IR; Actions cache keys stay per platform. published draft state and transferred inventories remain validated. Compiler traces, utility inputs and the report format decide report reuse after restore. Optional transport failures cannot turn a required build or report failure into success.

Inspector privately stages, canonically validates and publishes raw-v3 rows, all declaration materials including excluded helpers, ZIP digests, provenance-v4 and current-input attestation. Invalid required inputs, production validation failures and Lean failures remain failures. The existing coordinate/attestation wire fields `producer_sha256` and `repository_inspector_sha256` carry the report format token `SHA256("schema=stratalint-raw-lean-report-v3\n")`; they are not executable-byte evidence. Report source coordinates select only `report_modules`; Inspector sources remain registered producer provenance and native compiler inputs. Configuration digests retain their registered byte preimage. Interface contract sources are declared in the existing `config_inputs` inventory, so a whole-report receipt cannot skip a changed interface; native Lake then rebuilds and reassesses only affected compiler closures. Each native module bundle includes a strict origin record containing `module`, its singleton raw `report_sha256`, `input_projection`, `producer_sources_sha256` and `inspector_executable_sha256`, captured at generation. Origin sidecars contain no raw-source hash map. Lake's normal `transImports` supplies the dependency closure, constrained by `report_modules` and `dependency_sources`; unregistered local inputs fail explicitly during production. Fetched packages remain pinned by the registered toolchain/Lake configuration. The shell input verifier checks origin integrity, module membership and source-path ownership; publication staging checks only the bundle envelope and input coordinates. Producer-only files do not become input dependencies merely by being registered. The source fingerprint hashes the registered producer path/content digest inventory, including the canonical report-policy projection. Aggregate provenance-v4 identifies the report format and retains these exact records in `module_origins`, keyed by module and checked against canonical singleton row bytes. Mixed generation origins are valid; publication may update only invocation mode and never relabel a reused row as produced by the current executable. Origin records are validated when each module artifact is produced; Lake trace reuse and receipt reuse do not reinspect them. The public shell input helper exposes `address`, `verify`, `modules`, `producer-paths`, `scribe-producer-paths` and canonical `coordinates`; it does not own artifact reuse. The thin pair interface invokes one candidate Inspector producer.

The optional `report_execution` manifest declaration names the supported tools (`lake`, `lean`), their required registered identity file (`toolchain: "lean-toolchain"`), platform coordinates (`system`, `machine`) and explicit semantic environment variables. Report reuse binds the authored pin and those coordinates; it does not execute compiler version commands or require installed compiler binaries. Missing, changed or excluded pin registration cannot yield reusable evidence. CI restores optional project/dependency caches and installs the pinned toolchain as needed. Selected program builds and report misses still require the normal toolchain; if publication rejects a seed after planning, the normal entry obtains the missing toolchain and builds, retaining build failures. The normal Inspector entry first seals a `.reuse.json` sidecar after a successful native `:report` invocation with the Inspector default program targets or the caller-provided sorted JSON `STRATALINT_LEAN_BUILD_TARGETS` and complete publication. A receipt hit checks the bundle envelope and input coordinates on a private snapshot and republishes the report while separately fulfilling the selected program-build obligations. It binds the report format identifier, the declared report-module and configuration inventory with file modes, the execution environment and the five-file report; producer program files, including the Lean Inspector and audit library, are never part of this binding. Program compilation is an independent obligation selected by the Inspector default targets or the sorted JSON `STRATALINT_LEAN_BUILD_TARGETS` override: a receipt hit still builds those targets through Lake, and a miss builds them together with `:report` in one invocation. Empty target selection permits report reuse without restoring heavy Lean caches. Unscoped calls build the Inspector default program targets. A selected program build failure remains nonzero and removes the output reuse receipt. Reusing this successful invocation requires the report format and all bound inputs to remain identical, the private snapshot to match the receipt's bundle bytes, and its envelope and input coordinates to be current; the rows were validated when produced and are not validated again. An absent, old, damaged or mismatched receipt requests native Lake work; invalid registration and actual build/check failures remain failures. Producer implementation and judge rule changes do not prevent reuse and do not invalidate per-module artifacts. Contract interface changes require migration of every use and deletion of old paths in the same delivery; affected Reg compiler closures rebuild and are reassessed automatically. Hashing producer or judge program bytes into a reuse condition of a data product is prohibited. A per-module report artifact traces the module's own compiler closure, utility inputs and report format identifier; the Inspector executable is demanded and awaited without entering that trace. Judge fixes do not retrospectively reassess unchanged registrations; delta-only DTR does not consume unchanged registrations. A requested historical reassessment uses an explicit uncached complete report. Changes to extraction semantics, including declarations, statement identity or axiom closure, or to the report format update the format identifier, reject old reports through strict readers and re-extract every module.

A22 的报告路径在 current 中恢复可用的 dependency/project/toolchain 缓存，随后生产或复用一份聚合 Lean report；纯理论路径不使用 Lean report。报告、材料和项目编译产物随同一 project buildDir 运输，current/delta 消费聚合报告，各 unit job 分别承担自己的测试或反证结果。原生模块产物仍供报告生产和聚合复用，没有移除完整报告架构。truth-release bundle 离线验证不选择 CI run 或下载报告。没有独立报告 cache/store、暂停后续跑协议、baseline adapter 或 delta/cohort planner。`ci-current.yml` 的 detect 只按其单元模式选工；Scribe current 消费 `Meta/ReportProducers/scribe-content.json`，不读 base。


**Compiled contract production and inputs.** Production report generation reads each requested module and its imported declarations from serialized compiler artifacts through `RawArtifacts.Store`. It does not initialize extensions, construct an Environment, invoke elaboration or Meta, or run a content-supplied driver. Typed inputs are identified by their compiled Contract type heads and owner facts; constructor trees and safe constant references are decoded as data. Values requiring evaluation, missing parts, unknown layouts and read failures receive named diagnostics and have no fallback path. Each row is computed from its own artifact data. The raw reader checks compiler header identity and constant-table structure; native Lake traces select the compiler dependencies.

**Native executable boundary.** The transitive Lean import closures of `reportInspector` and `compiledJudgeTests` contain no D5, Reg or Mathlib modules. The implementation statically imports only contract foundations without mathematical dependencies. Mathematical contract heads use fully qualified constant names; `RawArtifacts` reads the compiled interface and input declarations at runtime. Tests enumerate contract name literals from compiled implementation expressions, require each name to exist in the compiled interface closure, and inspect both executable roots through their actual compiled imports. Root `make compiled-judge-test` delegates only to `tools/scripts/compiled-judge-test.sh`, which uses `make lean` to build the general test library, `compiledJudgeTests` and `reportInspector` before running the general native audits, including retained ordinary-registration inventory and source metadata assertions. The `current` consumer runs this entry when the explicit `tests-lean` unit is selected. Root `make compiled-fib-test` delegates only to `tools/scripts/compiled-fib-test.sh`, which separately builds the FIB test library, `compiledFibTests`, `auricFibAnalysis` and `reportInspector`, then executes source-contract checks, the compiler-to-consumer fixture, incremental source perturbations and finite input batches. The selected report consumer compares complete custody-bound Application inputs in the previous published report and the current native aggregate, including unavailable inputs and additions/removals in every supported owner. Changed inputs invoke this canonical entry after the report writer returns and before publication. Equal Application inputs require no FIB regressions unless an independent selected program or fixture obligation requires them. The explicit `tests-fib` whitelist additionally requires the same single invocation for compiler, discovery, analyzer, fixture, configuration and consumer dependencies; Application data follows its complete compiled closures inside that consumer, so unrelated owner edits do not force the FIB suite. Every retained runtime assertion must belong to a consumer selected for its actual input class: ordinary Registration inventory and metadata are checked by the general native audits selected for Reg inputs through `tests-lean`, independently of Application equality. The `tests-fib` unit retains the D5 imports required by its analyzer and fixture programs; its job requires the successful `current` consumer without preparing another Lean cache. No test receipt or second owner registry is persisted. Native FIB extraction uses the existing temporary module spool to compare complete leaves before Lake fetches the arithmetic analyzer; equal or unavailable leaves require no analyzer preparation. Failed FIB regression commands prevent new changed-report publication and retain the previous published input data for retry; preexisting reuse may still republish already accepted identical historical data. Production source-binding regression execution follows the explicit `tests-declared-template` unit. The general test library's directory glob includes `LeanInformationAuditRegTests/Fixtures/CompiledInputs.lean`; its imports register the runtime Reg inputs for compilation. The separate FIB library owns the FIB fixtures and checks. Lake builds fixture artifacts and their dependencies separately from the executable import graph; dispatchers carry no copied Reg target list. Missing artifacts, missing names and malformed structures retain named failures. Kernel checks, contract types, registration data, report custody, report format and report reuse conditions are independent of these entrypoints.

Report artifacts are isolated by compiler traces, utility inputs and the report format; configuration identity belongs to the aggregate. Raw enrollment inputs are compiler products of their original owners. The report records compiled contract inventory and structural decode diagnostics; it does not persist a checked plan or verdict authority. Plan identities contain neither source-file byte hashes nor manifest-derived values. Judge implementation and rule changes retain historical report artifacts; compiler input changes select reassessment. Module evidence and binding records omit raw-source hash lists; judge implementation paths do not become persisted binding validity inputs merely because the fixed driver executes them. The raw reader checks serialized compiler identity and constant-table structure. Lake trace hashes provide cache coherence, not cryptographic compilation attestation; the pinned compiler and its outputs remain within the native trust boundary.

The exporter emits an owner-bound `information_templates` partition for every requested module. Native row production validates complete current membership, evidence structure and material integrity; for a present `information_templates` partition, native validation checks its single current shape; the report format belongs to the aggregate and receipts, while the C# consumer rejects missing evidence and malformed records within A5.5's selected registration-owner scope. `inspect.sh` delegates both unchanged and changed work to native Lake report facets and propagates phase failures. C# separately performs A5.4's complete occurrence and ownership join for the selected owners and A5.5's delta judgment. Selected records check fields, canonical occurrence keys, ordering, source ownership and certificate consistency. Stored hashes are not compared with current snapshot bytes; compiler dependency traces, utility inputs and the report format govern report reuse. The explicit `--statements-only` fixture operation omits `information_templates` and cannot satisfy the declared-template consumer for selected owners.

Downstream projection tools cannot provide registration verdicts or replace production evidence.

**A15 提交与 PR 文法** `COMMIT := <官>"("<GID>"): "<动词短语>`;PR 模板 = 四段判词(立了什么/依赖什么/试了什么死了什么/账平声明勾选:无既有 closed 被推翻)。


**A17 Scribe:文档即代码(v7.14)** 叙事层(Blueprint/Papers,渐及 spec)的 canonical 源=C# 类型化文档 AST(`tools/StrataLint.Scribe`):文件头/章节/段落/公式(自建封闭 Formula AST→total `LatexWriter`,逐节点构造式确定性发射,不解析 LaTeX 文本)/GID 引用(经 Engine Gid 构造期解析,悬空即失败——取代已废的行号/片段哈希位置锚)。正式陈述统一为 `DocumentBlock.Describe`:文档内唯一的 typed local `DescribeId` 与 kind/statement/provenance 均构造期必填,kind 封闭为 `{definition,theorem,proposition,lemma,example,remark}`,statement 必为 `Formula` 或 `LeanDeclarationRef`,provenance 必为 `{literature-attested,repo-derived,suspected-novel,unassessed}`;旧 `Proposition/Theorem/ComputedValue/RenderedStatement` 类型一次迁移后从程序集删除,不得留兼容读者。学术发射以 `DocumentHeader.Digest` 生成 Abstract,Describe 按类型化 AST 深度优先序自动编号为 `1.n`,定理类生成 `Theorem/Proposition/Lemma` 陈述及显式 `Proof`(Lean declaration+axiom badge+终止符),原人类散文归 Commentary;`literature-attested` 以类型强制携 A12 的 L 引用并投影 author-year-title-source citation,其余三态投影规范 Source 且不携文献元数据。Markdown 与 QuestPDF 使用同一编号、陈述、证明、citation 和 commentary 数据流。红项=缺 kind/statement/provenance、公式字段非 Formula、TextRun 裸 `$`/`\\(`/`\\[` LaTeX 定界符、L/GID 悬空、citation 元数据缺位、DOI 语法或唯一性坏、producer 契约漂移及旧节点残留;Observe=纯文本/Unicode 疑似公式、代码跨度、Lean docstring 公式、DOI 在线解析与标题一致性,Observe 永不使离线硬门出网。`scribe describe-report [--json]` 离线读取预计算 Lean material 验 selector,以案号 `DESCRIBE-NODES` 发射机器账:逐节点 `GID#describe/DescribeId` 稳定 ID、kind/provenance 统计、`suspected_novel` Papers 候选清单及 `open_count=unassessed` 存量;人工/文献勘正只需改 typed provenance,报告自动消化 open。`DocumentHeader.Anchors` 为 `Anchor` 封闭联合类型,Lean 六行头仍以 `anchors: [string, ...]` 作序列化边界;canonical MD writer 由 first/second 双次序列化断言同进程确定性。跨版本 producer 契约只对测试自有的固定合成 Formula/Document 语料作长度分帧聚合 SHA-256,该指纹路径不发现或读取仓库 Scribe 数据;独立覆盖证明读取真实 Scribe 语料,并要求其 Describe `(kind,provenance,statement)` 元组及 Formula 父/子渲染上下文均为固定合成语料组合的子集,缺项判词点名具体组合。两者均不读取 tracked Markdown;`Blueprint/**/*.md` 是 FILEMAP `kind=generated` 的可刷新人读投影,`emit --check` 只作作者 freshness 便利,不进入 CI、admission 或 producer 契约。PDF 经 QuestPDF(钉版,社区条款,许可年检入账)。依赖闸门在 Lean 与 NuGet 两侧共用同一许可/证据原则:Markdig/CSharpMath/MathNet.Symbolics/AngouriMath 未准入(证据闸门),iText7 永拒(AGPL)。现役 `Anchor` 封闭联合仅有两型:文献 `lit/<bibkey>` 与外部 Lean 模块族 `mathlib/{module,decl}/<Lean.Name>`;二者均由 typed parser 强制 ASCII、ordinal 与严格 round-trip。`gict`/`pzg` 的理论卷、章节与编号只作 authoring provenance 注记,不是 Anchor scheme,机器不解析。`spec/<payload>` 在 `Anchor` parser、`AnchorScheme`、`Anchors/` 实现、D5 Lean 头及 Scribe 实例中均无现役定义或实例;其历史意图无法由仓内事实恢复,故记 `open(SPEC-ANCHOR-CLASSIFICATION)`,且在该 open 闭合前不得作为 Anchor 输入。现役 Lean 头成员资格进一步收窄为可由 import 图判定的 `mathlib/module/<Lean.Module>`;`lit` 与 `mathlib/decl` 虽是现役 typed Anchor 形,但 SL-017 对 Lean 头 fail-closed 拒收,其各自现役消费边界见 11.23。外部锚**不设注册表、包目录或 catalog**:依赖权威是 Lean import 图,版本权威是 `lake-manifest.json`,直接依赖声明在 `lakefile.toml`。手维护的 `anchor-catalog.v1.json` 及其 typed manifest 已退役:历史证据精确为 0.51% 覆盖率、198 个被 import 的 mathlib 模块、登记 1 个。任意 Lake-resolved package anchor 泛化为 `open`;仅由独立 harness change 解锁,该 change 必须实现 manifest/lakefile-derived generic external package anchor type/parser、package-to-module resolution、import-closure validation 与 red/green tests。第三方 Lean 依赖现役可机器验证的事实仅为:在 pinned toolchain/mathlib 下可 resolution、现行 build 通过、axiom/sorry closure 不扩张;现行 build 可复用 cache,不等同于 A14 所要求的 clean build 谓词。public source、immutable rev、license compatibility 均为 `open`;其机器谓词落地前不能驱动 automatic admission,且永不委派给人。

**A17.1 Describe 公式溯源(v7.15)** 生产契约为 `StatementFormula: Formula?` 及构造期可判的 `FormulaProvenance∈{hand-authored,lean-derived}`;不存在 `LatexStatement` 类型。`StatementProjectionFixtureLoader.FromLean` 是 `lean-derived` 的唯一构造入口,由 `StatementProjector` 对 Lean declaration 的 kernel `Expr` 生成 Formula;普通 Formula 参数零仪式地标为 `hand-authored`。对每个 `{theorem,proposition,lemma}` 且 statement 为 `LeanDeclarationRef d` 的 Describe,机器执行 `Project(d.Expr)`:若 `Projected(f)`,则 `StatementFormula` 必由 `FromLean(d)` 生成并等于 `f`,否则 SL-023 `Block`;若 `Unprojectable(reason)`,允许手写 Formula,但 `scribe describe-report` 必在 `DESCRIBE-NODES` 下逐节点记 `open` 及投影器原生 reason。此 open 是机器能力边界,不得终止于人审。准入门必须对其已校验源哈希的 candidate Lean report 执行 projection fixture/live-report reconciliation;缺失、陈旧或不一致均 fail-closed。

**A17.2 第三方 Lean 成果的两种准入形(v7.16 R3)** 精确命中的第三方 Lean 成果有且仅有两种合法准入形——**依赖**与**移植**;**重证禁止**(CLAUDE.md 第 11 条:重证已库有之定理=制造第二真源,与冒领同罪)。立本条之由:第 11 条有序路径的 ③ 指向「按 spec A17 可准入的第三方 Lean 生态」,而 A17 的三条准入谓词(public source、immutable rev、license compatibility)现役全为 `open`,故 ③ 事实上关闭;若不另立移植形,路径塌缩为 ②→④本地证明,恰是同条所禁之重证。**形式选择由机器判,不由偏好判**:Lake 每个包名只解析一次且 `lean-toolchain` 全局,故任一第三方包与本仓必须同意同一 toolchain 与同一 mathlib rev;比较候选上游的 `lean-toolchain` 及其 `lake-manifest.json` 中 mathlib `rev` 与本仓同名值,**不等即依赖形不可行**,无须再议;相等且 A17 三谓词已落地方可取依赖形;不等则只余移植形或放弃。此判据的实质是:依赖用于**与本仓同步演进的协调点**(mathlib 本身即此,故钉它而不移植它),移植用于**已冻结于某一 rev 的叶子成果**(论文交付物不会为本仓升级)。**移植形的义务**(逐条,机器可判优先):① 许可证随代码保留版权与许可全文,上游若带 NOTICE 链(Apache-2.0 §4 之类)则整链带入;② 入仓后即普通仓内内容,GID route、六行头、SL-001 import 偏序、SL-003 容量一律照常执法,不因「来自上游」豁免;③ 移植声明的 axiom/sorry 闭包不得扩张,须只含标准三条;④ **退役条件必须对本仓自己的钉版可判**,形如「本仓已升级到的某个 mathlib rev 中存在等价声明时删除本移植并改为直接引用」,**禁止**以「上游被 mathlib 接受」为到期条件——该事件不受本仓控制且已有反例(见判例),无可判到期条件的移植即第 6 条所禁之永久兼容层。**判例(读数,2026-08-13)**:`D5-T0019` 三距离——上游 `dkunert/three-gap-theorem-lean`(MIT,单文件 1486 行,sorry-free,无自定义 axiom)钉 `leanprover/lean4:v4.29.1`,本仓钉 `v4.31.0`,依赖形机器判否 ⇒ 取移植形;另 mathlib PR #40037(`feat(NumberTheory): the three-gap (Steinhaus) theorem`,+625 行)于 2026-06-09 以 mathlib AI 投稿规范关闭未合并,故上游收录无确定期,不得作到期条件。`D5-T0018-F` Weil 显式公式——上游 `anthropics/zeta-23-lean`(Apache-2.0)含 sorry-free `Zeta23.WeilEF.EF_lit_zetaZeroConfig`,钉 `v4.33.0-rc2`,依赖形机器判否;其 `Zeta23.WeilEF.Main` 的项目内 import 闭包实测 57 模块、893 KB、18,105 行,而该闭包直接 import 的 89 个 mathlib 模块在本仓钉版 `v4.31.0` 中缺 0 个,故移植形可行;本轮未执行,该事实记于 `D5-T0018-F`。
