**A16 零信任合并门(v7.12, CLAUDE.md 第 19 条之 spec 形)** 提交者身份(维护者/agent/fork)与准入无关,一切 PR 过同一道纯机器门。CI 的目标检查集、current/delta 分工、候选与 base 身份及最终 required check 名由 A22 定义。门脚本、判官与 workflow 取自候选合并树,base 仅以数据参与跨树检查。SL-029 仅报混面警告,SL-022 保护面标注、dev push 检测与独立评审继续适用;strict=false。机器 exit 语义仍为 0=通过、1=违规、2=输入/基础设施失败、3=SL-022 标注。SL-030 与 SL-032 继续按现役实现执行,不把早反馈冒称为不存在 base 判官的保证。

**SL-029 混面警告与删除路径分类**:合法混面 changed set 恰报一条 `ADMISSION-PLANE-MIXED`,为 `DisplaySeverity.Warning` + `AdmissionEffect.Observe`,随后继续全部普通验证。完整 `check` 与 `check-delta` 均在普通结果中保留该警告;不得提前返回成功、以仅含 Observe 的结果拒绝或吞掉其它 Block。单面不报该警告。混面不削减适用的完整 engineering 义务,生产 CI 的测试/检查选择由 CI workflow 内的单元白名单承担。SL-029 不再强制分面,不能防止同一 PR 改弱判官并修改内容;此残余不由警告消除。缺失、畸形、歧义、不安全的 FILEMAP、错误的策略文件分类及无效删除/base 来源仍作基础设施失败。变更路径只有在候选原始快照中缺席、且在不可变 protected-base 原始快照中在场时,才按 protected-base 的 FILEMAP 分类;候选中找不到登记不构成删除证据。每个删除路径必须在 base FILEMAP 中恰有一条有效登记,base FILEMAP 缺失、无法解析、登记缺失或多重匹配均 fail-closed。非空 delta 的候选 FILEMAP 仍须通过准入分类元数据校验,即使所有变更路径均已删除;未涉及实际删除时不要求 base FILEMAP。存续、新增与修改路径保留既有候选 FILEMAP 分类语义。重命名的删除端与新增端分别参与分类,复制源不作为变更端。删除判官文件可同时移除其字面登记;删除内容文件与判官变更同交仍报 `ADMISSION-PLANE-MIXED` 警告并继续验证。候选准入使用这一分类器,base 仅作为字节数据读取。engineering 与 current 不读取 base;测试执行由唯一 required check `required` 汇总，删除项目同步删除其作业与白名单段，不要求它在 candidate 登记表中保留。

**A16.2 准入证书 v3 与规则缩域** `AdmissionCertificate.FormatVersion=3` 的规则处置闭集恰分为 `ExecutedRules`、`SkippedRules`、`DeferredRules`;三者两两不交且并集恰为本轮 catalog。active rule 仅在其具名 `IsAffectedBy` 输入闭包命中时进入 executed 并实际求值,未命中时只进入 skipped;deferred rule 从不调用 affected/evaluate,只以 `(rule_id,case_id,title)` 进入 deferred,不得冒充 executed 或 skipped。证书 fingerprint 的 UTF-8 material 恰为逐行 `admission-certificate-v3`、`input_scope:admission-inputs`、`canonical:<sha256>`、按 catalog 顺序的 `executed:<rule_id>`、`skipped:<rule_id>`、`deferred:<rule_id>:<case_id>`,末尾恰一 LF,再作 lowercase SHA-256。canonical 身份只绑定 FILEMAP 政策以及已读取文件的 `(path,length,sha256)`；未读取的消化辅助正文及其路径清单、文件 mode、base 和 delta 不参与此身份，未读取的必需准入正文导致基础设施失败。格式、scope 标记、规则处置或已读输入变化按对应 material 影响 fingerprint。完整路径与 custody 每轮独立检查，相同 fingerprint 不授权复用准入结论。不同格式的指纹不得直接比较,现役 writer 不双写或迁移旧 fingerprint。每条 active repository rule 必须声明实际消费输入与 affected closure;只有真正的全仓不变量可保留无条件执行且须给依据。缩域测试必须同时钉住未受影响时进入 skipped,以及候选真实修改该规则任一权威输入时仍进入 executed;每条可阻断 active rule 另须有候选坏输入仍唤醒并拒绝的放行侧测试,防止 silent over-scoping。

**A18 FILEMAP 文件与 Evidence 政策（schema 6）** `Meta/FILEMAP.toml` 是唯一 manifest 权威。`files` 条目是唯一的路径成员源，声明五类 `{truth,program,data,generated,ledger}`、`admission_plane`、producer/consumer/verifier、runtime disposition 与适用的生成物、symlink 字段；全体 tracked/unignored 文件必须恰命中一条 pattern，根文件与 agent 章程使用字面条目，reports 仍要求字面登记。成员资格之后必须通过 canonical path/GID/domain 检查，glob 命中本身不能放行错误坐标。`evidence.artifact_kinds.<kind>` 严格声明 `{profile,selectors,path_selectors}`；七种已登记格式（含当前无实例的保留格式）只在此声明，缺表、未知 profile、非法 scope、重复或大小写冲突均 fail-closed。`Meta/domains.yaml` 仍为独立的 strict 词汇真源。`digestion_source = true` 在对应 files 条目上承担非理论源与理论源族的摄入资格，无平行文档清单。Engine 提供纯模型、strict loader 和确定性 TOML writer，CLI 取字节并 join domains，Scribe 只作投影。canonical snapshot schema 3 以 `filemap_sha256` 绑定规范政策，并以 `input_scope: admission-inputs` 声明正文范围。准入快照保留全仓路径、mode、缺失成员和链接原始字节，FILEMAP 与 custody 继续检查完整路径集合；理论正文、消化账目和 CAS 仅在被当前功能实际选中时读取。未读取的辅助正文不进入 canonical entries；已读取的目标 atom 与源 metadata 仍绑定其真实正文哈希。canonical entries 只含已读文件的路径、字节长度和 SHA-256，不绑定未读辅助路径清单、文件 mode、base 或 delta；全路径与 custody 的独立检查不能凭相同 fingerprint 省略。发布工件的 `SnapshotContentDigest` 继续绑定完整仓库内容，不与准入身份混用。changed FILEMAP/domain/structured Evidence 的字节与重编码不动点在写入门检验；无关 delta 不重放已入库的非规范历史字节。历史 admission-plane 与 symlink reader 只取同快照自身 FILEMAP，不把旧快照强制送入当前写 schema。FILEMAP conformance 保留 actor/verifier、generated inventory、目录纯度、路径引用与 run-local 守卫，缺失政策无默认、无旧源回落。

The exact rows partition remains registered when empty, both before seeding and after discharge. This population exception requires all the above fields and a tracked, separately registered judge-plane activation authority; it grants no exception to other empty globs or to activation/seed/residual/deletion checks. The store and rule verifier names resolve through the FILEMAP implementation registry to production program paths. Missing or dangling implementations retain the existing `FILEMAP-DATA-VERIFIER` / `FILEMAP-DATA-VERIFIER-DANGLING` failures.

**A19 收缩义务会计(v7.12,机器已退役)** v7.12 曾以 P0 机制安装 → P1 注册 → P2 单次消费定义保护面与现役规则义务的收缩会计,并以 `RESIDENCE-EPOCH` 完成过一次历史消费。保守扩展重放、C0 与契约纪元机器已于 2026-08-12 整体退役,工作树现无其声明、比较器、事件账或收据;收缩义务的重新机器化当前为 `deferred`(无执法机器),重启须先建门,不得冒领为现役 admission。

**A19.1 负例地板与观察期义务(v7.14)** 负例地板只覆盖 `RuleLifecycle.Active` 且 `AdmissionEffect` 为 `Block` 或 legacy identifier `HumanGate` 的规则;后者在本条中仅是现存枚举名与“可阻断 effect”分类,不得解释为触发、等待或请求外部评审。此类规则每条都必须在候选树的 Golden corpus 中有真实 blocking witness,缺失即 infrastructure fail-closed。`Observe` 规则仍属 active、照常执行并报告 warning,但其语义结构上不参与 admission 阻断,故与 SL-007/009/014 的 deferred 规则同样递延地板义务;不得为了凑红例把构造器、typed loader 或 emitter 的独立 fail-closed 冒充该规则的 blocking witness。规则从 `Observe` contract 为 `Block` 时,contract PR 必须同时加入该规则在候选树中的 blocking fixture;合入后下一基线立即把它纳入地板。该递延只校正不可满足的见证义务,不关闭、不软化任何检测路径。〔勘注 2026-08-15:负例地板执法机器已随保守扩展重放于 2026-08-12 整体退役,本条见证义务当前为 `deferred`(无执法机器);上文保留为设计记录,重建时须先建门。〕

**A20 Reusable collaborative worktrees and disposal** The creation grammar is owned solely by `WorktreeCommand`: `<creation-namespace>/<kind>/<task-code>`. Lifecycle ownership covers every nonempty descendant of `LifecycleNamespaces`; historical namespaces are not creation aliases. The main checkout stays on dev for authorized synchronization and observation. For reading, search, statistics, diff, viewing commits/PRs/issues and creating/editing GitHub issues, use the main checkout or a suitable existing worktree by default; these tasks do not require a new worktree. For pinned-revision reads prefer `git show <commit>:<path>`. Never switch a shared checkout's branch for reading. Repository edits, staging, worktree-mutating tests, mutation verification, checkout changes, incompatible builds and builds with write side effects first reuse a suitable linked worktree after checking registration, repository identity, branch/commit and pin compatibility, existing material and cooperative scopes; create one only when isolation is needed. Builds that write are not read-only. Every lane, seat, delivery and research tree is a linked worktree of the main checkout's clone; additional clones, including shallow, local-path and independent dev-root clones, are not created. Automatic cache seeding finds its donor only inside the same clone; unless `STRATALINT_LEAN_CACHE_DONOR_REPOSITORY` names an explicit donor, the main checkout of an additional clone provisions a separate `.lake` (Release restore or rebuild) that shares no clonefile blocks with the main clone, and `clean-lanes`/`clean-all` see only the invoking clone's registrations.

A task branch, exact commit and freshly confirmed remote reachability provide durable recovery. Directories retain compatible caches between tasks, without a permanent bijection between directories and sessions. `make worktree KIND=... NAME=... [DEST=...] BASE=...` prefers a compatible attached task tree or idle claim when DEST is omitted, and otherwise follows the Claim rule below; isolation can be requested through an explicit unused DEST. An explicit DEST validates an existing target's registration, repository identity, toplevel/backlink, exact branch, base ancestry and pin compatibility. Compatible dirty trees can be reused across sessions without initialization or overwriting material. Current Lean pin/manifest/cache gates retain their own ownership; reuse does not materialize caches. Busy, damaged or incompatible trees are preserved. For incompatible ordinary reuse, continue on another legal branch/directory, or fetch a confirmed remote commit and use it as a new tree's BASE; owned failed initialization follows same-destination recovery. Never force duplicate checkout of an occupied branch or move another participant's ref without coordination.

**Merged-task return.** After required checks, PR MERGED and first dev push/affected-entrance verification, the host joins this task's writers and build jobs and runs `make worktree-return PR=N WRITERS_JOINED=1` inside the task tree. This calls `StrataLint worktree return --pr N --writers-joined` and the Python protocol under exclusive tree, index/current-ref and new-idle-ref scopes. The joined flag asserts host joining, not observation of other tasks. `make pr` and `pr-watch` report checks only, never merge certificates. `gh pr view N` must report MERGED, base dev, head branch equal to the attached task branch, and headRefOid an ancestor of its observed HEAD. CLOSED, OPEN and auto-merge pending have distinct refusals. Resolve exactly one origin push endpoint; freshly pin its advertised dev tip D and fetch that object without moving tracking refs. The PR mergeCommit must be an ancestor of D; head ancestry alone is not merge evidence. Checkpoint new ordinary work on the task branch through the common checkpoint interface using the held scopes, including scoped native-lock clearance and its receipt observations when needed, and report commits not in D. Retain the task ref, create `lane/governance/idle-<token>` at D, switch without overwriting ignored files, and verify clean state and no unfinished operation. Keep the directory and `.lake`/tool caches. Failures preserve work for retry; an already idle clean tree at freshly pinned D reports `already_idle`, after validating the PR and retained task ref.

**Unused-task return.** The same `make worktree-return WRITERS_JOINED=1` entrance accepts an omitted PR only for a clean task tree, including ordinary untracked files, with no unfinished Git operation and an attached task branch whose tip and HEAD both equal its allocated base B. Creation and claim both create the task branch at B: establish B from the new OID of the first retained native branch reflog record, whose old OID must be all zero. Missing, unreadable, linked, malformed or partially expired creation evidence refuses with `unused_allocation_unknown`. Later reflog history does not affect eligibility. Preserve host joining, pool/tree/index/ref exclusivity, registration/identity and intentional/initialization-lock guards, and freshly confirm B is retained by the single origin push endpoint's advertised dev tip D before creating the idle branch at D. Thus no commit on the task branch lies outside dev. A reset-away commit is outside this current-state rule; return retains the task ref, its branch reflog and repository objects without pruning, while a subsequent idle-pool eviction for capacity or disk pressure removes the tree's per-worktree metadata, including its HEAD reflog. No new ledger is used. Retain the task ref for the existing orphan-branch retirement policy. A task tip different from B or dirty/unfinished state requires the MERGED-PR return path or explicit removal; supplying a PR still validates all merged-task evidence above. A PR-less retry on a genuine idle branch refuses with `unused_return_already_idle` without reacquiring its held ref scope; `already_idle` remains the PR-backed behavior described above.

**Idle pool and eviction.** An idle tree is attached to `lane/governance/idle-*`, clean including ordinary untracked files, without an unfinished Git operation, with HEAD equal to the branch tip and a dev ancestor. Its branch suffix is a fresh 32-character lowercase hexadecimal token. Its retained first branch reflog record must be a zero-old-OID creation at that tip with a nonfuture timestamp and the exact message `worktree-idle:<token>`, written by return before switching to that branch. Ordinary task creation and claim never write this message; an `idle-`-prefixed task name alone does not release a tree or bypass allocation protection. Detached HEAD alone is never idle. There is no ledger. Return, claim and pool eviction coordinate through the clone's OS-descriptor `operation:idle-pool` scope. The single Python pool owner serves return, clean-lanes and clean-all. When the pinned local dev cannot certify an idle HEAD, the pool confirms that HEAD against the single origin push endpoint's advertised dev tip and fetched object without updating tracking refs. A remote dev that does not retain HEAD refuses eviction. Retain at most 4 idle trees per clone; evict oldest first by the idle branch creation record, breaking equal timestamps by physical path. Below the existing 5 percent available-disk threshold, evict idle trees until restored or none remain. Revalidate identity and idle state under exclusive tree scope before common removal; its index/ref scopes and preservation rules still apply. A canonical refusal during eviction revalidation retains that tree with its owning reason, including confirmed disappearance or identity change; an operational failure remains failed. The unresolved sweep still fails its aggregate and defers unprocessed candidates. Busy, damaged, locked or unverifiable candidates retain with their exact blocker; a blocked eviction can leave the bound temporarily unmet and is reported. Only verified idle candidates bypass ordinary 3-hour/24-hour selection, including when inside capacity. Every pool sweep, including return-triggered capacity or disk-pressure eviction, preserves all completed per-tree outcomes, native-lock observations, predecessor recovery roots and checkpoint/confirmed/candidate recovery details in its receipt across any later failure, including native timeout/subprocess errors, refusals, OS errors and confirmation/fetch failures. An unresolved operation names its candidate or pool scope, stage and error, fails the aggregate and public exit status, and leaves unprocessed candidates deferred; completed removals remain reported as completed. Clean-lanes maps unresolved sweep/checkpoint operations to failed and indeterminate deletion to partially removed, preserves recovery details, honors failed aggregates even without an item failure, and defers extra sweeps. `make worktree-remove` remains explicit named removal with its existing semantics.

**Claim.** Without explicit DEST, first route an attached task branch with an initialization marker through the same-destination recovery rule; otherwise validate compatible ordinary reuse, then search idle trees whose lean-toolchain and lake-manifest.json bytes equal the requested pinned base. Claim under exclusive tree, index and old/new ref scopes, revalidate idle identity, create the task branch at that base using native tracking policy, switch, write and verify the immutable allocation record below before exposing the claim, and delete only the old idle ref with its observed OID. Apply scaffold/restore steps and report the actual path in the success receipt. If no idle candidate fits, or the pool scope is busy before an implicit claim enters, create at the default path; a contention fallback records `busy_scope:operation:idle-pool` in the creation receipt's `idle_claim_fallback`, while successful claims report `claimed` and their actual path. Explicit DEST admits same-task reuse, same-DEST initialization recovery or claim of that exact idle tree; occupied incompatible paths refuse. Concurrent claims cannot allocate the same tree. Once a branch has been created for an allocation, a failure retains that material and refuses without falling through to another allocation. Claim does not materialize or certify caches; existing cache pin/freshness doors remain authoritative.

**Participation and affected scopes.** `StrataLint worktree with --path DIR [--read PATH] [--write PATH] [--git] -- COMMAND [ARG ...]` holds shared entry through OS descriptors. Independent editors run concurrently. Declare overlapping edits and semantic read dependencies. Named file/subtree scopes exclude conflicting operations, with shared ancestor intentions; same-scope readers currently serialize. `--git` covers this tree's index and current branch/HEAD; `--ref REF` covers additional shared refs. Checkout-wide transformations use `--exclusive` only for that operation. Initialization holds target entry and ref scopes. No session-long whole-tree writer lease exists.

Descriptors survive exec/fork. Closing one copy does not release surviving descendants or other participants. Protocol Git commands and `with` jobs receive an empty `core.fsmonitor` (disabled both where Git reads a boolean and where older Git reads a hook path), `maintenance.auto=false` and `gc.auto=0` through the Git configuration environment, so no builtin fsmonitor daemon or detached automatic maintenance starts under a scope: such background Git processes outlive their command and would retain the inherited scope descriptors. Automatic maintenance is therefore deferred to explicit or unscoped `git maintenance`/`git gc` runs. All launches while worktree-operation scopes are held, including initialization restore, `with` jobs and native-lock holder inspection, receive `MSBUILDDISABLENODEREUSE=1`, `DOTNET_CLI_USE_MSBUILD_SERVER=0`, `UseSharedCompilation=false`, `UseRazorBuildServer=false` and `_RazorForceBuildServer=false` from the Python protocol policy, disabling reusable MSBuild nodes, the MSBuild server, shared compiler servers and the independent Razor build server so they cannot outlive the command holding inherited scope descriptors; foreground work retains descriptor inheritance and stays inside its scopes. A protocol Git command that exceeds its time limit receives SIGTERM, which lets Git remove its own lock files; only a process still running after the grace period receives SIGKILL, which can leave `index.lock` behind. A runtime that closes inherited descriptors must retain the descriptors listed by `WORKTREE_SCOPE_FDS`, or enter again before using the tree. The host owns actual job/descendant handles and joining. No daemon, lifecycle controller or custody/handoff ledger is added. Cooperative users enter before accessing the tree; deliberate bypass is outside the guarantee. Scope release neither asserts publication nor deletes a tree. Git initialization markers, native transaction locks, cooperative descriptor locks and intentional worktree locks retain their separate meanings.

**Ordinary checkpoint and publication.** Checkpoint with `make worktree-checkpoint MESSAGE=FILE` after every recoverable unit of work, before long checks, before hand-off and before normal end. Label unfinished snapshots WIP in the message. A checkpoint certifies saved bytes only; it does not certify tests passed, publication or merge. Use no heartbeat or empty commits. The checkpoint uses the real index with native `git add -A` and `git commit` on the current branch. It consumes the current tracked and ordinary untracked working snapshot under normal Git ignore rules, replacing pre-existing staging. No private index, path attribution or separate staged-version preservation is maintained. Unmerged entries and unfinished merge/cherry-pick/revert/rebase/sequencer operations are refused before staging. Native hooks and index/ref failures remain failures; no automatic conflict resolution. A clean snapshot needs no empty commit. Index/ref scopes cover the compound Git operation. Cooperative editors remain permitted; changes after staging belong to a later snapshot. `make worktree-publish HEAD=BRANCH [COMMIT=REV]` resolves exactly one push endpoint, uses non-force publication there and freshly verifies required-commit equality/ancestry from that endpoint's advertised tip and object. Multiple push endpoints and an endpoint resolving to this repository are refused. Tracking refs or push exit alone do not certify retention. Failure retains local source/index/commits.

For normal finalization the host first joins this task's writers/children, then calls `worktree finalize --branch NAME --writers-joined [--message-file FILE]`. The adapter checkpoints the ordinary snapshot and publishes; the option asserts host joining, not observation or joining of other tasks. Abnormal exit performs no publication. Failed initialization uses the same idempotent recovery path as a same-KIND/NAME/DEST rerun, after its owned commands settle. A WORKTREE_FAILED receipt names stage, target, retained material and the same-DEST recovery or named reclaim action; allocating another directory is not the default recovery action.

**Lock taxonomy and recovery.** An initialization marker is a Git worktree lock with reason `worktree-init:<token>`. Git transaction locks are native `index.lock` and ref `.lock` files. Cooperative locks are inheritable OS-descriptor scopes in `<common-git-dir>/worktree-operations/*.lock`; their files are never unlinked as stale. Any other Git worktree lock is intentional and retains the 24-hour rule. The C# initializer and Python mirror initializer hold exclusive `tree:<physical path>` and branch-ref scopes. A busy exclusive tree scope retains an initialization-marked tree with `initialization_in_progress`. Python qualification owns initialization liveness and intentional-lock age. Clean-lanes obtains per-tree results for locked candidates through one batched qualification invocation per pass; the preview may create cooperative scope metadata and briefly acquire exclusive entry, but performs no checkpoint, ref update, unlock or removal. Qualification and its clean-lanes/removal adapters (C# identity probes and FORCE rereads, batch selection, per-tree preview, removal preflight and final scoped requalification, including the remove/qualify process boundary) classify each tree as eligible, refused (a canonical eligibility refusal, skipped with its exact reason), failed (native Git failure, filesystem error, inner/outer timeout, abnormal exit or malformed/incomplete receipt; retained and reported failed with a bounded causal diagnostic), or deferred (unreached when the bounded budget expires; retained with a deferred failure reason). At the remove process boundary, such failures are `failed` only when established before mutation; otherwise dispatched removal is `partially_removed`. Every obtained failed/deferred result is accounted before later structural or selection guards; those guards cannot erase its failure or diagnostic. Failed/deferred outcomes make the aggregate and public exit nonzero and defer extra sweeps. C# identity rereads refuse vanished or unregistered targets and changed HEAD, branch, lock or Git directory with `race_changed`. Intentional-lock qualification compares lock-file identity and modification metadata across its reads. Confirmed disappearance or change refuses with `observed_identity_changed`; a metadata-read failure with unchanged or unverified identity remains failed with its original cause. Python qualification preserves its stage-specific refusal reasons, including `missing_or_linked_tree`, `git_pointer`, `git_pointer_identity`, `repository_identity`, `path_not_registered` and `observed_identity_changed`. Qualification's failed native identity read rechecks disappearance or identity drift; a confirmed change refuses, while an operational failure with the observed identity still present remains failed with its cause. Initial identity failures are accounted before idle routing; any such failure makes the pool batch observation-only, including paths returned to idle after the initial inventory. Every obtained failed/deferred path remains protected from later mutation in that invocation. These outcome contracts cover qualification and the listed adapters; other lifecycle consumers and shared Git-scope acquisition retain their existing exception contracts. The qualification batch budget is min(600 seconds times the number of unique paths, 3600 seconds), covering snapshot reads and qualification; native Git termination has its existing additional 30-second grace, while the consumer enforces the same outer budget. The stream emits a per-path start event before qualification: an outer deadline fails the active path with its timeout diagnostic and defers only never-started paths. On the outer deadline the consumer settles the child, then drains complete emitted events to EOF within five seconds before assigning outcomes; the execution budget does not increase. Incomplete trailing events grant no observation. Streamed completed per-tree observations survive deadline exhaustion; an unsuccessful process boundary still fails the aggregate even if all observations completed. Final removal requalifies under its required scopes; preview results grant no mutation authority. An acquired exclusive scope stays held throughout recovery or removal and makes the marker reclaimable regardless of age. Resume additionally requires token equality with the branch reflog creation message (all-zero old OID, new creation OID), branch tip equal to that creation OID, and matching registration, backlink and common directory. An owned branch whose registration never completed is reused at the same destination at its creation OID, independently of later movement of the requested base; foreign or changed identities refuse. Resume pins differing ordinary partial-checkout bytes in a recovery commit/ref before completing missing checkout files, preserving extant bytes, restoring dependencies and unlocking. Completed failed-initialization recovery records `worktree-recovered:<token>` in the registered tree's HEAD reflog before unlocking. Repetition uses shared ordinary reuse, retaining the creation tip even when the requested base descends from that creation OID only when the matching recovery reflog entry, creation witness, unchanged branch tip and pins matching the creation commit remain present. Successful ordinary initialization records no recovery entry and retains ordinary requested-base ancestry and pin compatibility requirements; incompatible trees refuse. Mirror markers have no branch-creation witness and permit reclaim only.

**Blocked Git operation native-lock clearance.** Only an ordinary checkpoint blocked by its index or current branch/HEAD ref lock, or an owned initialization recovery blocked while loading its native index, may clear that exact lock, under exclusive tree, index and all relevant ref scopes with registered identity verified. Complete inspection of the exact path through macOS/Linux `lsof`, or Linux `/proc/*/fd`, must report no holder. Unavailable, denied or incomplete inspection retains with `native_lock_unverifiable:<path>`; a holder retains with `native_lock_held:<path>`. Immediately before unlink, device/inode, size and modification time must match the inspected file; replacement retains with `native_lock_changed:<path>`. Canonical checkpoint and removal receipts include every observed lock path, size, age and outcome, including clearances before a later failure and during removal-record reconvergence. Age and size are observations, never certificates; no minimum age applies. Unlink only the certified file, then retry the blocked checkpoint or initialization index load once. Packed-refs and unrelated locks are never cleared. Non-cooperative Git that closes its lock descriptor before committing is outside this guarantee. Ordinary checkpoints and compatible reuse retain shared tree entry. Initialization and native-lock recovery require exclusive entry, as do return, claim and idle validation under their rules above.

**Common worktree removal rule.** Named, automatic, mirror, runlocal and aggregate registered-tree removal use `worktree_preservation.py`. Selected unlocked worktrees are eligible. Intentional Git worktree locks (manual, empty or unrecognized reasons outside worktree-init:) require at least 24 hours since lock-file modification; future or unavailable timestamps refuse removal. Initialization markers instead follow the exclusive-scope liveness rule above. Keep repository/backlink/registration identity, main checkout and nested registration protection. Preflight the entire named batch before any checkpoint mutation and recheck each target before removal. An eligible tree checkpoints ordinary outstanding changes using its real index. For detached HEAD, it creates a new local `lane/governance/recovery-<unique-token>` branch at the resulting HEAD before removal; native branch creation never replaces an existing ref. The detached HEAD/index scope covers the compound operation; the new ref scope runs from branch creation through removal. A clean detached tree retains its existing HEAD without an empty commit. Removal retains the current or newly created recovery branch and reachable common Git objects. Before content deletion, removal records the checkpoint commit and recovery branch in the tree's Git metadata. It deletes all contents except the root `.git` link while retaining registration and that readable record. A content-deletion failure or interruption leaves the tree registered for retry; native `git worktree remove` runs only after no other contents remain. A native unregistration failure therefore leaves no content payload. A later removal of a tree carrying that record resumes deletion without a new checkpoint when HEAD and the recovery branch still resolve to the recorded commit and the working tree differs only by deleted tracked files; if the snapshot moved, it first pins the recorded commit under a new non-replacing recovery ref, checkpoints current ordinary work into a new snapshot, verifies both recovery roots, and atomically replaces the record while carrying predecessor commit/ref pairs. Every resume acquires the recorded predecessor ref scopes, validates their commit/ref pairs and carries all predecessor roots through removal and the invocation's orphan sweep, including after successful record replacement followed by interrupted content deletion. Content-deletion interruption leaves the record and recovery objects intact; deleting the record is never a recovery action. Other later tree changes retain the tree with the concrete reason. Unmerged/unfinished operations, failed commits, failed branch creation (including name collisions), or ordinary changes remaining after checkpoint retain the affected tree with the concrete reason. A failed branch creation leaves any completed detached checkpoint at the registered tree for retry. Failure reports distinguish a confirmed recovery branch from an attempted candidate whose creation may have failed. Earlier completed removals are not rolled back or misreported. The recovery branch and predecessor recovery roots are excluded from the entire invocation's orphan sweep, including when clean or local-only. No empty commit is created for a clean tree. Ignored/cache files and intermediate staged versions are not recovery obligations. Ordinary removals without a blocking native transaction lock perform no holder inspection. Process, open file, cwd, mapping, activity or cache use do not otherwise govern deletion; initialization liveness and blocked-checkpoint native-lock recovery use the scoped checks above. Git index/ref operation coordination only protects checkpoint/removal against competing Git operations. Native force removes ignored material, and double force handles an elapsed Git lock without first unlocking it. Ordinary removal requires no automatic push or remote confirmation; timely publication is separate, while merged-task return and idle eligibility follow their specific remote-dev rules. Native `git worktree add PATH BRANCH` recovers the committed snapshot while its branch and objects remain intact; permanent local-ref retention is not promised.

`make -C tools clean-lanes` previews; `FORCE=1` executes through the same automatic selection. Each invocation pins `refs/remotes/origin/dev^{commit}` as immutable D and reports it in the summary. BASE only supplies the original behind count; merge judgments, including orphan branches, use D. After main/identity/registration, nested-tree, intentional-lock age and initialization-scope protection, an observed H is eligible for the merged shortcut when `git merge-base --is-ancestor H D` exits 0, its working tree has no tracked/untracked changes or unfinished Git operation, and both its HEAD committer age and allocation age are at least 3 hours. Known unmerged trees and known merged trees with outstanding work require both ages to be at least 24 hours and at least 300 commits behind BASE. The last update of observed HEAD H is its committer Unix time (`git show -s --format=%ct H`), independently of lock-file age. Author time, directory timestamps, reads, checkout/reset and later reflog entries never renew it. Allocation evidence belongs to the tree independently of its current attachment. Its anchor is the maximum of the first retained HEAD reflog timestamp, explicit allocation-record timestamps, and corroborated legacy branch-creation timestamps since the last explicit allocation record. For every HEAD `checkout: moving from X to B` record since the latest explicit allocation, classify B under Git's native `git check-ref-format --branch` rules, excluding `HEAD` and revision expressions that Git expands to a different branch name. Only destinations that cannot themselves name a local branch, such as `HEAD~1`, `@{-1}`, `x^0`, `a..b` or `:path`, supply no legacy allocation. Every syntactically valid local-branch name, including full 40/64-hex object IDs, `refs/...`-prefixed names, remote-looking names and tags, uses native local-ref identity and retained local branch logs with no spelling exemption. For each such record, with new OID C at Unix time T, use exactly one class: (a) allocation, when a surviving same-generation branch creation record has all-zero old OID, new OID C and timestamp T; (b) existing-branch checkout, when that generation's creation strictly precedes T; (c) ambiguous otherwise, including absent local ref/log evidence, creation after the checkout, rename/name reuse or copied history, equal timestamps with different commits or any unprovable historical identity. Native `git switch -c B` supplies the creation/checkout pair. Reconstruct historical spelling through retained native branch-renaming records; a copied history does not establish destination identity before the copy. Current spelling or revision resolution does not establish a generation's identity at T. Each class (c) record protects the tree only within the applicable 3-hour or 24-hour allocation grace measured from its own timestamp, then imposes no selection constraint; this supplies only an age bound, never a corroborated allocation anchor for validating other branch evidence. Class (b) supplies no allocation timestamp. Explicit allocation records remain authoritative. Read the retained HEAD history even after detaching or switching elsewhere, and validate each surviving branch log against that branch's current tip rather than against the tree's HEAD. Missing, invalid or contradictory required surviving local evidence retains with `update_unknown`; reset and reads supply no allocation evidence. Without an explicit allocation record, also validate the attached branch log. Missing or invalid required legacy evidence with surviving local identity retains the tree instead of dropping its protection. If the first retained branch record is no longer a creation record and is later than the already established allocation anchor, retain with `update_unknown` and `selection_detail=branch_allocation_unverifiable`; an already dominated first-record timestamp supplies no new allocation anchor, and unprovable checkouts still use class (c). With timestamps ordered across related creation/checkout records and each reflog, prefix expiry cannot shorten the applicable allocation grace: later retained anchors or bounded historical ambiguity preserve it, while damaged surviving evidence retains; no such guarantee is made for nonmonotone clocks. A fresh tree or a newly claimed branch at an old commit must pass the separate allocation grace, including before its first commit. Ancestry exit 1 means unmerged; other exits retain with `merged_state_unknown`. Unresolved D retains with `dev_unknown`; missing, empty, damaged or tip-mismatched allocation reflogs or unreadable commit time retain with `update_unknown` and a concrete `selection_detail` (`reflog_*` for HEAD, `branch_reflog_*` for attached or historical branches, `allocation_record_invalid` for malformed explicit evidence, `head_commit_unreadable` for the commit). Future committer or allocation timestamps retain with `age_unverifiable`. Dirty trees never use the clean shortcut; ordinary new bytes must be checkpointed successfully before removal regardless of the old commit age. Conflicts and unfinished Git operations retain through the preservation preflight. The shortcut reports `merged_to_dev`; the original rules report `selection_reason=original_selectors` alongside their selector outcome. FORCE reobserves H, branch, registration, lock and Git directory, then recomputes the selection result before preservation/removal; any changed observation or selection result retains with `race_changed`. Original-selector removal checkpoints ordinary changes, including changes made after selection. Detached and non-managed branch trees participate in this selection. Explicit named removal bypasses these automatic selectors. Unknown identity or selection readings retain the affected target. Unresolved attachments, locks, refused or partial removals defer extra sweeps. Orphan ref retirement retains its separate ref scope, attachment/observed-tip checks and remote retention policy; branches observed attached or created for recovery during this invocation are excluded, even after successful tree removal. Remote refs are never deleted. Unregistered snapshots and other non-worktree host artifacts retain their independent preservation/activity policy. `clean-runlocal.sh` routes registered manifest targets through the same removal rule after its manifest/root/age selection; non-worktree entries retain their own policy. Native errors report partial/indeterminate effects without a rollback promise.

**Immutable allocation record.** A new allocation of an existing tree writes a native record into that tree's HEAD reflog, under the exclusive tree and index/current-ref scopes, after its task branch is attached and before the claim is exposed. At observed commit C and allocation Unix time T, run `git -c core.logAllRefUpdates=true update-ref -m "worktree-allocation:<token>" HEAD C C` with `GIT_COMMITTER_DATE="@<T> +0000"` and a fresh 32-character lowercase hexadecimal token. This is an ordinary same-OID ref update, not a commit; verify the appended HEAD record has old OID = new OID = C, timestamp T and the exact message. Its timestamp remains evidence after attach, detach, checkout, reset or retirement of the task branch. The latest such record supersedes earlier branch-log associations; subsequent legacy branch allocations remain independently readable. Ordinary observation, checkout and reset never emit this message. Tree creation already supplies its initial HEAD record; the claim entrance must emit the explicit record for every new allocation of an existing tree. The reader accepts this record format independently of a claim entrance. Later reflog entries neither overwrite its timestamp nor substitute their own for it. A retained explicit record with a malformed token or unequal OIDs retains with `update_unknown` and `selection_detail=allocation_record_invalid`.

The disk gate checks the source and nearest existing destination parent before .NET starts: below 5 percent available rejects, exactly 5 percent passes. Explicit `ALLOW_LOW_DISK=1` only overrides that threshold. `clean-all` routes registered trees through the same `clean-lanes --lanes-only` entrance. Its independent host-artifact sweep preserves owner, activity, mount, flags, links, readability, representable-path and fingerprint guards. Artifact age defaults to 24 hours; host-root override parameters remain. Credentials, settings, skills, plugins and runtime databases are outside that artifact inventory, and Codex artifact selection and preservation rules remain unchanged. Python 3.9+, Git and .NET are required for worktree cleanup. The general macOS/Linux activity inspector belongs to the separate host-artifact sweep; its failure is reported after worktree cleanup and cannot prevent worktree removal. Exact native-lock holder inspection is confined to blocked checkpoints and initialization recovery under the scopes above. Physical release is measured by `disk_available_before` and `disk_available_after`; apparent sizes and `du` readings do not promise physical release.

Temporary and sshx run artifacts are ordinary candidates even when they contain `.git` directory skeletons, repository metadata directories, gitdir files, bare repositories or nested repositories. Their entire tree must meet the ordinary age (24 hours by default), owner, mount, flags and activity guards. Git status, refs, reflogs, commit reachability, stash, repository locks, unfinished Git operations and shallow/partial-clone state do not independently retain these temporary artifacts. Registered worktrees and the main checkout remain protected, including candidates containing or lying inside those registered trees. Temporary candidates use the registrations and activity read once at the start of the sweep; before deleting an sshx or Codex candidate, refresh activity and registrations. Every candidate's fingerprint is rechecked before deletion. FIFOs and sockets in temporary and sshx candidates are reclaimable like empty files. Codex artifacts retain their separate repository and special-file preservation rules; device and other unknown entry types retain the baseline `special_file` result.

The host sweep does not split retained or recent candidates to reclaim `.lake/build`; packages, source and Git metadata remain governed by the candidate's ordinary result. `.lake/.release-<token>` cleanup is the sole narrow staging exception and is owned by `lean_cache_release.py`, which supplies the inventory and cleanup function used by both Release fetch and host cleanup. Only direct `.lake/.release-<token>` directories qualify, excluding files, links and `.release-backup-*`; shape is empty or contains only `build`. The entire residue must be at least 24 hours old and meet the filesystem guards. A `.lake` on a different device from its containing checkout retains staging as `mount`. The caller supplies the current time; cleanup derives its fixed age cutoff from that input and any stricter caller cutoff, without reading the wall clock. Two checks and deletion occur under the physical `.lake` path's exclusive nonblocking LeanCacheGuard; busy locks or failed checks retain the residue. Coverage includes the main checkout, registered worktrees and repositories found under artifact roots. A directory that vanishes or cannot be read during that search is reported as `vanished` or `unreadable` and skipped; the sweep continues with the remaining candidates. Registered worktrees are protected in their entirety; cleanup of `.lake/.release-<token>` staging is the sole exception.

**A20.1 Named registered worktree removal** `make worktree-remove NAMES="DIR [DIR ...]" [FORCE=1]` calls `StrataLint worktree remove --names "DIR [DIR ...]" [--force]`. Match complete final directory names exactly, Ordinal, against Git registration. Names are whitespace-separated and deduplicated, without prefix/branch/path fallback; names containing whitespace are unsupported. Make passes raw NAMES through the existing environment-variable boundary, never as shell code.

Resolve the entire batch and check every member before any deletion. A refused preflight deletes none. Naming bypasses automatic Git-update age, behind and PR selection; it retains the intentional-lock 24-hour duration rule, initialization-scope liveness and main/nested identity protection. Native removal has a 300-second timeout; explicit FORCE only removes that timeout. A timed-out or killed removal resumes on a later request under the common rule. Identity inspection retains its own hang guards. Do not prune, kill, auto-unlock, manipulate PRs or delete branch/remote refs or other trees' caches. Report actual removed, retained and partial/unknown effects; no cross-tree transaction is promised.

Per-item JSONL has name/path/outcome/error and the confirmed recovery branch; failures also identify any attempted recovery-branch candidate. The last line is `WORKTREE_REMOVE_RESULT exit=<code> removed=<n> failed=<n> refused=<n>`. CLI codes: 0 success, 64 usage, 65 not_found, 66 ambiguous, 67 main, 68 lock duration unavailable or not elapsed, 69 inventory, 73 identity refusal, 74 execution failure or indeterminate effects. GNU make returns its own 0/2; read the classified final line. Local checks follow A22 and do not certify remote required CI or integration qualification.

**A21 性能账(2026-08-26 退役)** τ=0 owner 以 #3365 的机器负载相关失败裁决:挂钟与机器性能不得承测试判词,性能实验须与功能测试分离。退役前复核 `preflight.sh`、`local-harness-gate.sh` 与 `ci.yml` 对 `perf-report|PerfBudgetComparator|over-budget` 为 0 命中,三条预算全为 `warn-only`,`false_positive_rate_percent = "unmeasured"`,且无性能预算阻断事故;故按第 20″ 条与第Ⅵ节「为道日损」删除事件采集、账本 writer/report/comparator、预算数据、CLI/Make 入口及其测试,不留兼容动词或空壳。历史 revision notes 只作审计记录,不构成现役接口或义务。

**A22 CI 单元作业与 current/delta 契约**

**入口与选工。** 普通检查由 workflow `ci-current.yml` 承担，PR 与 push 各自触发；独立缓存入口见本节缓存契约。每个独立程序或测试项目是其中一个单元作业，互无前置依赖的作业之间并行，作业内部可串行。`detect` 作业只取提交与目录树（blobless、sparse 检出 `tools/scripts/workflow` 和 `Meta/ci-units.json`），经 `ci-entry.sh` 核对候选并列出改动路径，再由 `ci_detect.py` 按 `Meta/ci-units.json` 中唯一 `CI_UNITS` 与执行声明一次判定各单元和 current 子检查是否命中，输出命中表；不使用 GitHub 事件级 `on.paths` 过滤。单元作业以 `if:` 读取命中表，未命中即跳过，不占用 runner。检测校验并按白名单匹配完整改动路径列表，不设路径数量截断。唯一 required check 是始终运行的 `required` 作业：`ci_required.py` 要求检测成功、白名单段与其 needs 中的作业一一对应、命中单元成功、未命中单元跳过，否则红；身份、对象或输入异常以 exit 2 失败，不能静默免跑。FILEMAP 管路径归属、custody、准入面、symlink 和 Evidence 格式，不声明 CI 选工。

PR 检出固定 `GITHUB_SHA=M`，验证 M 有两个父提交，第二父是触发 PR head，第一父 `B=M^1` 只作路径比较和 delta 的数据。push 核对 `HEAD=GITHUB_SHA=event.after`，按完整 `event.before→event.after` 端点差异判 hit；初始 push 的全零 before 按无 base 全部命中，不构造空 diff。schedule/dispatch 同样核对 HEAD 与 GITHUB_SHA。取得所需固定对象后移除 remote 与 remote refs；固定对象及显式网络 URL 仍可达，不宣称完全网络隔离。只执行候选代码，不 checkout、编译或执行 base 判官。

**独立命令。** 各入口验证自己的必需输入与真实结果，不要求其它 workflow 的执行证据：

| 入口 | 义务 |
|---|---|
| `make -C tools test TEST_PROJECT=tools/tests/<项目>/<项目>.csproj` | locked restore/build、该项目完整测试与 `StrataLint.TestEvidence` 的 owner 程序集及真实成功 TRX 校验；证据工具只引用 Engineering 与 Runtime；缺失结果、失败或 infrastructure hang guard 不能记成功。 |
| `make -C tools selftest` | 执行 selftest 的放行与拒绝判据。 |
| `make -C tools compile-proof PROOF=capability-proof` / `PROOF=banned-api-proof` | 构建并调用 `StrataLint.TestEvidence` 验证预期的编译拒绝及具名诊断，任意编译失败不替代反证；CLI 的同名入口复用该实现。 |
| `make lean-report` | 经受保护的 Lean 缓存入口和原生 Lake 增量生产规范报告。 |
| `check-source --current [--protected-base <40-hex-sha>]` | 仅运行报告前的源码前置子集：SL-003 current 容量与 SL-015 路径/组合保护；显式 base 时另取原 delta affectedness 与容量核心。没有 Lean closure 或完整准入证书；普通 push 不传 base，纯理论仍用原 report-free check-delta。 |
| `check-current --candidate-lean-report FILE` | 当前树有效性；context 无 base 或 changes，可在根提交、无 remote 的仓库中独立运行。 |
| `check-delta --protected-base <40-hex-sha> [--candidate-lean-report FILE]` | 读取固定 base 数据；无报告仅接受非空且完全位于 `docs/develop/theory/` 的变更，沿既有快照、FILEMAP、路径保护与 SL-003 理论文档容量检查。其它变更必须提供报告，验证跨树保护、分区、首次冻结、棘轮及候选测试拓扑；删除测试项目不因基线登记缺失而阻断。 |
| `tools/scripts/workflow/scribe-content-checks.sh REPORT [SCRIBE_DLL] [PATHS_FILE]` | 调用 `content-check --report REPORT [--paths-from PATHS_FILE]`，在一个进程中加载一次显式报告，依次运行 `projections --check`、`describe-report --check` 与 `markdown-check`；保留各检查的输出与首个失败退出码。 |
| `filemap-conform` | 独立核对当前 FILEMAP、路径、producer/verifier 与生成物契约。 |

`make test` 运行 lean-report 与 check-current。`make gate BASE=<sha> [PURPOSE=pull-request|push]` 与 `local-harness-gate.sh --base <ref> [--purpose ...]` 消费 `Meta/ci-units.json` 中唯一的 `CI_UNITS` 与执行声明，通过同一个 `ci_checks.py` 运行所有适用单元、实际子检查、互补测试过滤器和 TRX 校验。默认 purpose 为 pull-request；push 保留普通非理论变更不运行 delta 的策略。`make gate-plan` 只输出选择与身份，不声称执行成功。`make -C tools check-fast` 只提供快速 .NET 结构反馈，不声称 Lean 或完整准入通过。不同程序仍可独立运行；本地检查不能替代远端 required checks。

**共用检查边界。** `ci_contract.py` 消费唯一声明，不解释 workflow、不建立影响图或通用调度器。current 的报告适用性、理论快路、PR delta 模式及编译审计、Fourier source evidence 的选择取同一契约；单元的 project/command、dotnet、lean 与依赖恢复声明也在该契约内，不在本地或 workflow 维护副本。检查所有者输出实际 check id、原始 exit 与解释；protected-only delta exit 3 显式标明并映射为成功早反馈，不授权评审或合并，exit 1/2 不被吞掉。超时、取消保留原始退出；前置拒绝门失败时，其守护的后继明确 blocked，不声称已执行，也不等同未适用；已进入阶段中前置满足的独立适用检查继续给出结果，保留原有可达性与退出语义。make 外层仍遵守其原有 0/2，分类诊断以检查所有者输出为准。

本地显式 BASE 必须解析为固定 commit，不可用时 exit 2，不能当作 hosted unavailable-base。本地比较 BASE 与实际候选工作树，合并 staged、unstaged、untracked 的完整 NUL 路径；addition、deletion 和 rename 两端均参与选择。候选内容身份涵盖路径、文件类型、模式和字节（包括未跟踪文件），与 base、contract revision、purpose 一起输出供两端配对；空变更不等于无 base。hosted PR 保留 M/B，push 保留 before/after，只有取不到 hosted base 时才全部命中。本地不删除 persistent remotes，不伪造事件认证、NEEDS 或 required 结果。hosted adapter 承担事件、权限、缓存运输、发布和真实 job 结果；本地已有缓存和工具提供准备，恢复后仍按原生 Lake 增量和 fetch-or-fail，不暗中冷构建。每个 current/unit 的各计算阶段共用累计 7200s，不因共享 helper 重置；下载和依赖恢复在预算外。

FILEMAP conformance 使用既有候选快照 reader 的当前路径和模式，纳入未跟踪文件并排除已删除文件；coverage、pattern population、依赖和生成物集合检查同样覆盖该候选。Architecture 的源码枚举及 hang-guard 路由检查同样消费现有候选快照 reader；canonical-copy 策略扫描原有完整可执行源码范围中的 tracked 与普通 untracked 工作树字节，遵守 Git ignore，不要求先 stage。run-local 工件的禁止入索引条件仍读取单独的 index 身份，不能以未跟踪文件冒充已入索引，也不能以本地验证要求用户先 stage。`make ci-unit UNIT=<id>` 提供同一执行声明的定向检查入口，不声称完整 gate 成功。

**前置拒绝与执行次序。** required 参与检查须按实际前置依赖和实测成本组织，将低成本、确定性的源码输入、路径、配置、文件数与行数拒绝置于不必要的 Lean 缓存运输、报告生产及昂贵检查之前。只允许真正不依赖报告的规则在报告前执行；所需判官准备与编译仍须完成。文件/行数限额及 current/delta 适用谓词复用既有判官所有者，不在 shell/Python 复制，也不以仅审计 index 的 capacity-audit 替代。前置关系与执行沿用上文唯一选工/执行契约，本地与远端按相同固定 base、purpose 和实际 staged/unstaged/untracked 候选字节配对；重排不扩张豁免，不改变纯理论适用条件。

选中 current 时，共享 executor 的 source 前缀先恢复/构建候选判官，再用既有快照与策略 loader 检查实际候选字节。current 容量保留 materials/excludes、800/1000 行及目录 192 容差；仅原 delta 适用时检查 touched 目录 96 上限和候选/基线成员包含、Library added/modified 3000 行与理论 added/modified 5000 行。源码混面仅运行这个前置子集，不能把完整无报告 check-delta 扩张到混面。FILEMAP、快照、配置及判官 bootstrap 保留原基础设施失败分类；只有完成的确定性源码拒绝阻止受守护后继，bootstrap 或输入失败不冒充这种拒绝，输入仍有效的原报告 fallback 与独立检查保留可达性。

hosted 的 source 作业是同一个 current 的前置 phase，直接读取 detect 的 current 命中，不新增 CI_UNITS 项。所有原单元保留命中谓词；current 未命中时 source 跳过，不能给其它变更新加全局检查。其余选中作业等待这个窄边界的原生 job 完成后并行 fan-out；source 拒绝、取消或超时阻止其启动，缺失输出不能放行。source 新构建判官仅在既有 writer/cache-miss 资格、judge-ready 与 continuation 输出均满足，且 continuation 仍需报告判官或重试未 accepted 的纯理论检查时，按原 key/path 保存以供同次运行的 current 恢复；基础设施失败的有效 continuation 不以 source 整步成功为条件。这个必要交接属于原生 job 完成依赖，保留其释放成本；无消费 continuation 的拒绝、超时、取消不上传该判官。纯理论已 accepted 时没有剩余判官消费者，source 不保存，current 不恢复、准备或重编判官，仍保留 current 的成功执行身份。required 直接核对 source 的实际结果、accepted/continuation 输出与全部原命中作业；前置失败、blocked、cancelled、缺失和未命中不能相互替代。source 与 current continuation 共用原累计 7200s：ci_compute 导出已扣除判官编译和源码执行的剩余秒数，continuation 仅接受有限的 [0,7200] 剩余量并独占初始化新 job 的本地预算文件；缺失/非法输出失败，不能重置已有文件或重新分配 120m。两个作业之间的准备、下载与运输不计费，不新增持久账本；各单元默认 120m、平台 360m、长 producer 360m 与 Release 120m 仍独立。

报告就绪后按固定顺序运行 source evidence、Scribe content、check-current、原本适用的 check-delta、compiled audit。这些检查消费已验证报告与判官，互不消费对方的通过判词；顺序依据既有完整 job 的 9s、15s、85s、426s phase 读数，delta 样本无可比耗时，保留其在 current/Scribe 之后的位置。上述为历史成本依据，不是候选性能承诺；嵌套 timing 不重复求和，缺失的 <1s 记录不当作零。任何独立验证器拒绝后，其余具备前置的验证器仍执行。current continuation 在这些已就绪诊断之后才保存其 fallback 新构建的判官；资格为未取消、明确成功的 judge 构建、原 writer 与 cache-miss，不额外要求报告有效或整个 job 成功，构建失败或取消不能取得成功资格。project snapshot、上传与确认也在这些诊断之后，仍仅按原 report/work/adoption/writer 资格及既有顺序执行；独立规则拒绝不使有效报告失去发布资格。运输仍可推迟最终 required，诊断提前与 required 总耗时分别验收。

确定性前置失败必须阻止由其成功守护的昂贵后继启动，最终 `required` 仍为失败；前置阻塞与未适用分列。成功前缀不认证余下检查，全部命中义务仍须成功。互无前置依赖的后续工作保留有效并行，不得按历史耗时排序将全部 hosted 作业串行化；YAML 文本顺序不建立执行依赖。本地串行次序与 hosted 依赖边可采用不同宿主布局，但保留共享的选中检查、前置关系和判词语义，不新增计时数据库或调度器。

仅为未来运行复用的可选缓存上传及确认运输须位于已就绪诊断之后，不能仅因运输而先于本可执行的检查。唯一保留的同次运行判官交接是上述 source 保存到 current 恢复的既有依赖，不推广为所有上传的豁免，也不为移动等待而增加重复构建、运输协议、缓存版本或调度器。有效性与发布资格仍由本节既有 judge-build、report/work/adoption 及 writer 契约分别决定，不以整体准入成功替代；独立后续检查拒绝时，仍保留符合既有资格的上传与确认，前置失败不得制造合格缓存。运输排除在累计计算预算之外，仍须在关键路径证据中单独评估：以真实事件分别测量交接耗时、字节、实际复用与构建工作，并验证首次失败反馈及成功释放路径。首次构建耗时不等于假设的第二次构建耗时；未取得可比前后读数不得声称整体提速，静态检查也不证明原生 job 调度或交接已验收。

验收在同一受保护 integration 流程中具名覆盖适用的源码输入缺失、非法路径/配置、超文件数及超行数等负例，测量首次有意义的失败反馈时间，并核实受该前置守护的昂贵后继未启动；合法正例须完成全部命中义务并测量成功关键路径。下载/准备与计算分别测量，沿用既有 required checks、保护、预算及稳定计数，不另开验证流程、不弱化守卫或遗漏检查，不以缓存命中或总挂钟单独证明改善。上述为规范要求，不表示实现、部署或 hosted 验收已通过。

hosted 成功单元输出实际执行的 unit id，current 在所选检查成功后输出自身 id；required 同时核对该输出与命中表及 needs 的作业名一致。缺失或错配的成功执行身份也拒绝；本地不生成 NEEDS 或认证远端结果。

current/delta 在谓词级分开，不能用空 changes 或 base=candidate 模拟 current。delta-only 定义域及债务收缩范围保持，包括 SL-029 混面告警与 SL-030/031/032。delta 不合取测试或其它作业的执行证据；合取由 required check `required` 承担。测试拓扑仍检查候选中的生产/测试关系和测试间依赖；删除测试项目同步删除其单元作业与白名单段，不再要求它留在当前登记表中。

**登记与材料。** `Meta/engineering-projects.json` version 1 严格声明项目 path、assembly、role、include/exclude、references、owner、owned_test_assembly、test_partition、root_namespace、namespace_exclude、global_namespace_exceptions，及顶层 rule_build_inputs。程序集身份、测试归属、Compile 与 namespace 检查按登记消费；历史 reader 只读取自身需要的项目声明，不执行历史程序。不从 MSBuild、目录名称、反射、调用图或 IO 效果推导 CI 选工。

单元白名单 `CI_UNITS` 以 `[单元 id]` 开段，段名等于作业 id；`[*]` 段的模式适用于全部单元且不得排除。其余非空、非 `#` 行各为一个整路径模式：`*` 匹配任意文本（含 `/`），`?` 匹配一个字符，前导 `!` 只从本单元排除；含 `[`、`]`、`{`、`}`、`(`、`)`、`|`、`\`、`@` 的模式被拒绝。路径命中某单元，当且仅当它匹配该单元或 `[*]` 的包含模式且不匹配该单元的排除模式。重复段、重复模式、无包含模式的单元及无单元的白名单均失败。无 base 的事件（新分支、取不到 before、非 PR/push 事件）全部命中。白名单是选工谓词，不从工程登记、FILEMAP 或调用关系推导；工程依赖或测试读取的文件改变时，同一变更修改对应单元的白名单。普通单元作业经 `ci-unit.yml` 以 unit id 读取同一执行声明并调用 `ci_checks.py`：`project` 为测试项目时执行 `make -C tools test TEST_PROJECT=...`，否则执行非空 `command`，二者恰设其一；`tests-fib` 直接调用共享 executor 的 `acknowledge-current` 轻量确认命令，检查当前调用显式提供的成功结果与 unit 身份，不经过 ci-unit 准备，不启动 shell 或测试；`dotnet` 为布尔值，`lean` 为 none/toolchain/build。current 的判官缓存键由 runner 平台、实际 `dotnet --version`、同一契约的 `judge_build_args` 配方摘要与 `JUDGE_INPUTS` 所列 git glob pathspec 选中的已跟踪文件路径与 blob id 的 sha256 组成，精确命中时跳过判官编译。

`Meta/ci-checks.json` 仅保留 SL-003/SL-015 的 materials 与 material_excludes，供现役规则材料谓词读取。`Meta/ReportProducers/scribe-content.json` 声明 statement projection 的项目根与 `lean-report-inputs.json` 中的 scope；非空 changes 使用显式登记及项目引用闭包决定谓词影响范围，null changes 仍完整验证。它不承担跨 workflow 计划或执行证据。

**Workflow 与报告消费。** 测试项目、selftest、两类反证、FILEMAP 与 current 分属独立作业，`ci-unit.yml` 只提供 workflow_call 的公共执行步骤。选中 current 的 source phase 在 Lean 准备前恢复 judge，miss 时构建 judge；continuation 复用原 judge cache，缓存保存/恢复失败时仍以剩余计算预算构建，不使用另一个材料转交协议。source 仅按上述实际消费 continuation 资格保留同次运行交接，current fallback 新构建输出按原 key 在已就绪诊断之后保存，二者资格与运输成本分别核验。可运行的 judge 输出 bundle 提供 Lean producer，否则报告入口保留独立 producer 构建。进入报告阶段后，judge 构建失败仍不截断原本可达的报告生产；需要 judge 和报告的审计、source evidence、current、Scribe 和 delta 仅在各自前置成功后执行。通过前置拒绝门、进入该阶段后，这些独立验证器不因另一验证器失败而失去可达性，作业仍保留任何失败。纯理论变更在 current 的 source phase 直接运行无报告的 check-delta，成功后 continuation 不重复执行：PR 使用事件 merge commit 的第一父，push 使用完整 event.before→event.after；入口须由真实 diff 确认全部路径属于理论目录。该路径不下载 Lean 缓存、不准备 Lean report、不运行全局 current 或 Scribe，仍执行已有文件/路径保护与 SL-003 的 5,000 行规则。无可读 base 的事件保留完整路径。其它变更在前置拒绝门成功后串行生产一次 Lean report，再运行 check-current 与 Scribe；PR 命中 delta 白名单时运行 check-delta，普通非理论 push 不运行 delta。唯一 required check 为 `required`，确切名称与 Actions app 绑定取自实际 ruleset 和真实运行。watcher 与 contribution queue 按配置的 required set 汇总固定 head 的红绿，不认证旧嵌套运行拓扑。保持 strict=false。

`StrataLint topology` 读取 remote default branch 固定提交的 `ci-current.yml`，检查其 dev pull_request 触发与 current 作业中的 check-delta 步骤。它只报告安装拓扑，不能证明实际执行版本或分支保护，且不门控 current/delta；新 workflow 尚未安装不会阻止候选检查。Tower 的 ci-jobs 消费 `ci-*.yml` 中作业的 dev PR/push 声明：engineering-ci 的成员是 `required`，current 另须满足 delta gate 契约。

离线 truth release bundle 接口验证完整性及调用者声明的成功 check 名称；来源真实性、保护策略完整性及发布授权须独立核验。该接口不查询 CI，也不选择可发布 dev 提交或下载 push 报告。

truth release bundle 的 manifest 以 schema 标签 `truth-release` 标识，恰含五个 SHA256SUMS 绑定工件：source snapshot、truth graph、raw Lean report、truth export 和 Blueprint index。source snapshot 以 schema 标签 `source-snapshot` 标识，闭键恰为来源仓库、commit、tree、Lean toolchain、mathlib rev、producer package commit，以及 truth graph、raw Lean report、DAG Markdown、truth export 四个摘要；它不承载消化账目、残余工件或冻结事件字段。冻结成员资格由 `source_tree` 绑定的 `Golden/Frozen/state/**` 状态片决定，bundle 不另设账本头、事件计数或状态表摘要。manifest、source snapshot 与 publication 坐标的 transport 文件名为 `release-manifest.v1.json`、`source-snapshot.v1.json` 与 `truth-release-publication.v1.json`。reader 只接受上述标签与闭键：其他 manifest 或 source snapshot 标签、manifest 的 `frozen_ledger_head` 槽位及 source snapshot 的账本字段均被拒绝。已发布的 release 不改动。

发布命令签发前对每个状态片执行与 SL-008 相同的当前树核对(A14.5)：对应模块存在且为 Closed，pin 等于当前 report 重算的模块陈述摘要，声明清单与当前 report 一致；任一不符即以 `TRUTH_RELEASE_REJECTED` 具名诊断拒绝签发，且不写出 bundle。truth export 的冻结字段按 A14.7 由状态表、当前 report 与源码 import 现算。签发不替代 Lean、Scribe、冻结来源或工件完整性的验证义务。

<a id="a22-ci-cache-flow"></a>
**缓存角色与数据流。** 下表中的构建产物是检查的证据输入，缓存发布不签发准入结论。普通检查由 `ci-current.yml` 选工；缓存 producer 和 Release publisher 是独立入口。

| 角色 | 责任与边界 |
| --- | --- |
| 短 CI | 准入与检查消费者；`current` 计算累计限时 120 分钟（2 小时），`ci-unit.yml` 默认同为 120 分钟计算预算；两者外层 job 平台挂钟上限为 360 分钟。缓存与工具依赖下载准备不扣计算预算。按 `CI_UNITS` 执行命中的检查和测试，恢复种子后由 Lake 原生增量补齐。 |
| 独立长 Actions producer | manual、已选择准备的 PR 当前 head、自动 protected push 共用 `.github/actions/lean-cache-produce/action.yml`，限时 360 分钟；构建完整可用种子及所需原生程序、测试可执行文件和编译夹具，并按上文 Native executable boundary 完成报告所需的依赖范围 FIB 回归；其它测试仍由短 CI 命中单元执行。完整指产物齐备，不要求强制 clean；有效 Lake traces 仍可复用。 |
| PR 合入前准备、合入时启用 | 标签 dispatch 到受保护目标 ref；长构建检出固定 PR merge candidate，仅上传 Actions artifact。源 PR 可显式取回；目标短 CI 在实际合并与事件检出相符时直接采用，不等待另一次发布作业。 |
| GitHub Actions Cache | 按 ref 可见性运输 project、dependency、elan 与 judge 编译产物；命中只提供构建起点，不是报告读取成功或检查通过。 |
| GitHub Release cache | 发布不含 mathlib 等 `.lake/packages` 依赖的项目 buildDir 种子；有独立 manifest、资产校验和恢复入口，与 Actions Cache、上文 truth release bundle 分责。 |

```text
source + selected path changes
  +--> detect / CI_UNITS --> selected current source prerequisite
  |                          +--> short current + parallel selected units --> required
  |                          | theory-only delta: SL-003 / SL-015; no Lean transport
  |                          | Lean: adopt matching preparation OR ordinary restore
  |                          |       -> native increment -> report/check consumers
  +--> opted-in PR head --> dispatch(target ref, pinned PR/H/M/B)
  |                      -> long shared build + strict validation
  |                      -> Actions artifact (native.tar.gz + preparation.json)
  |                           | source PR: same PR/H/target, current-head required
  |                           | target: actual merged K = event checkout, same PR/H/target
  |                           +--> direct adoption before ordinary restore
  +--> manual / protected push fallback --> same long core
                             | adopt matching preparation first; otherwise restore
                             +--> native build + validate -> ordinary Actions save

Actions Cache (visible ref) --restore--> job-local workspace <--eligible adoption
  .lake/build: compiler artifacts + reports + materials + sidecars + receipt
  .lake/packages: dependency artifacts; ~/.elan: independently pinned toolchain
  judge cache: compiled .NET outputs (separate from report evidence)
                             |
  validated report + (positive work OR validated merged adoption + known zero work)
                             +--> ordinary target project key -> confirm availability
  PR preparation: no ordinary project/dependency/elan writes in target scope
  source M has parents B/H; workflow runtime T and actual merged K remain distinct

  .lake/build --Release publication entry--> GitHub Release cache
  GitHub Release cache --local fetch entry--> .lake/build
  schedule --> own Release job: optional Actions restore -> report -> publish
```

**短 CI 计时边界。** `current` 的准备与计算交错，`ci_compute.py` 在 source 与 continuation 各自 job 的 `RUNNER_TEMP` 中独占保存剩余秒数。source 初次初始化，continuation 仅从上文已校验的 job-output 余额恢复，不重置或另给 120 分钟；文件本身不跨 job 运输。各串行计算调用使用同一累计余额、单调时钟和 GNU `timeout`，累计计算阶段挂钟而非 CPU 核秒，不是每步各给 120 分钟；超时返回 124，TERM 后最多 5 秒清理宽限，必要时 KILL（退出 137）。余额文件缺失、损坏或并发占用直接失败；启动前先清零，异常中断不留下未扣费余额。普通命令失败保留原非零退出码与输出。此文件不是缓存、receipt、调度器或持久账本。

| 阶段 | 计算预算 |
| --- | --- |
| checkout、事件身份核对、选工、Actions cache / PR preparation 下载、校验与解包、工具链安装、NuGet restore、缓存准备及写回运输 | 不计入；仍受各自网络错误处理、元数据请求超时及平台 360m 总上限约束 |
| judge / fallback Lean producer 的 .NET 编译，报告输入核对、复用判定、capture、prepare、publish，Lake 原生编译与报告生成 | 计入同一剩余余额 |
| compiled audit 的实际编译及执行、所选 .NET 测试及 TRX 检查、current / delta / Scribe 规则 | 计入同一剩余余额；测试程序内部的夹具构建、restore、安装和挂起防护属于被测执行，保留原 guard，不属于 CI 工具准备 |

source 在 judge cache 恢复后初始化余额；judge 与命中测试的 NuGet restore 经共享 executor 的准备路径执行，不扣余额，编译 judge 使用 `--no-restore`。continuation 在所选依赖准备前恢复剩余余额，使独立准备失败仍可保留有效输入上的报告 fallback。报告入口的 fallback producer 先独立 restore，再计费编译；工具链和 `ensure-cache` 是准备阶段。`with-cache-reader` 在原 writer guard 内完成自身嵌套准备后才给实际命令计费，保留冷构建拒绝、原失败与 guard 释放。报告外层 supervisor 在该普通 CI 调用中使用 360m 的准备/计算总上限，实际计算仍扣 120m 余额，其他调用和测试 guard 不改。报告 `reuse` 在 GitHub Actions 下不下载 Release：既有 Release fetch 与 freshness 分支在网络取回前跳过；本地 Release 的既有策略不变。缓存辅助工具的 bootstrap 属于准备；项目与报告编译不能搬到准备阶段。

`ci-unit` 按 project 或所声明锁文件闭包先 restore NuGet，再由同一预算 owner 初始化 job 本地余额文件，用一次 GNU `timeout` 调用包住所选 test/command；`timeout-minutes` 输入为 1..360 的整数，默认 120；相应 canonical .NET 入口使用 `--no-restore`，测试新建项目保留自身 restore 行为。嵌套的计费入口继承外层测试计时，不重复扣费，也不暂停测试计时。普通缓存恢复不触发完整冷报告、扫描或迁移；缺失/不兼容仍由现有入口失败。纯理论路径只做适用文件/容量规则和所需 judge 准备、编译，不触发 Lean/toolchain/artifact 工作。

PR artifact `gh api` 保留流式输出、原生网络错误及非零退出，不设固定 600s 整包截止；控制面 API 超时保留。平台 360m 上限涵盖准备、计算、上传和清理，可能在准备阶段终止 job，不承诺无限下载时间；缓存 producer 的既有 360m 与定时 Release 的 120m 仍是各自独立 job 上限。

普通 compiled-judge 检查与可选 `make compiled-fib-test` 分离，后者保留编译夹具、compiledFibTests、通用 FIB 行为与 analyzer 测试。`tests-declared-template` 使用 `lean:none`，仅执行不含 FourierBridgeSourceEvidenceTests 的补集；current 在报告产物就绪后执行该 Fourier 子集。两端使用 `Meta/ci-units.json` 中同一执行声明。

内容检查与基础设施行为测试分责：Lean、Scribe 及既有可执行源码全集的复制策略 guard 由 architecture 单元消费；普通内容变更不选择重量级 worktree 行为测试。worktree 实现、夹具及实际构建依赖变更仍由 `CI_UNITS` 显式选择行为测试，不通过运行时推导测试影响范围。原生报告的编译与模块 trace 仍是唯一增量权威；模块注册只建立 Lake 依赖，不逐模块同步等待编译输入分类；分类完成后由依赖 continuation 准备对应模块；FIB 先将完整编译 Application 输入及可复用 reading 写入既有 spool，仅实际 generated 工作大于零时才准备 analyzer，再完成并校验模块。保留原有输入 trace 和失败传播，不把判官程序身份混入报告 trace。共享程序构建与被选中的审计执行分责，缓存复用不免除审计。一次构建同时包含 Inspector 与下游审计程序时，在既有下游 Lake workspace 中共同构建，避免对同一程序闭包重复注册与缓存准备；单独 Inspector 或 Reg 入口保持原职责。Release 传输契约按既有 transport、verification 与本地/旧名 case 所有者分别执行，各组保留完整用例并独立使用原有子进程 hang guard；CI 单元选择、累计计算预算和功能判据不随分组改变。

每个 job 的本地 `.lake/build` 是编译、原生模块报告、聚合报告和材料共同的承载目录，跨 job 通过运输恢复，不是共享可变目录。`Meta/ci-cache-paths.json` 登记 project 在 `.lake/build` 下的原生路径集合、dependency 为 `.lake/packages`；elan 与 judge 是辅助编译缓存，不是额外 report store。current 实际消费聚合报告，模块输出供原生生产与聚合复用。普通与长 producer 共用的 canonical 聚合报告入口在 writer 返回后、新变更报告发布前，按完整编译 Application 输入差量执行 `make compiled-fib-test`；输入相同且未显式选中时不执行 FIB 回归。`current` 由同一选择结果显式传入 `STRATALINT_FIB_REGRESSIONS_SELECTED=true|false`，覆盖继承的环境值，转交 `CI_UNITS` 选中的 FIB 实现与夹具覆盖；`tests-fib` 只确认该次 `current` 成功及其 `unit=current` 身份，成功后才输出 `unit=tests-fib`，不再准备工具、构建报告或运行套件。hosted 通过显式参数传入 needs 的结果与身份，本地 gate 由实际 current 执行返回值完成同一确认；独立调用不从继承环境取得成功上下文，缺少显式成功 current 时拒绝。required 同时核对 source 前置、current 与该确认的选择、结果和执行身份，失败、取消、错误跳过、非命中执行或身份不符均拒绝；其它测试仍由普通 CI 命中单元执行。准备、采用与复用的材料校验不构成测试执行记录；producer 成功不等于 required 成功。

**触发、身份、读写与预算。** 表中写入资格还须满足下文工作量与成功条件；producer 不在 `required` 的 needs 中，不能替代实际选中的检查。

| 入口 / 触发 | 固定源码与读取范围 | 写入范围 | 计算预算 / 平台 job 上限 |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 普通 PR | merge candidate `M=GITHUB_SHA`，`B=M^1` 只作数据；先显式取同 PR/head/target 的准备；未取得时 project 先自身 merge ref，再 base branch | required job 不写 project；dependency、elan、judge 只写该 PR ref | current 累计计算 120m；unit 默认累计计算 120m；各 job 平台上限 360m |
| 普通 push | `HEAD=GITHUB_SHA=event.after`；完整 before/after delta；匹配实际合入时先采用准备，否则 project 查自身 branch ref | dev/integration 可在报告成功后写 project 增量；辅助缓存各依自身条件 | current 累计计算 120m；unit 默认累计计算 120m；各 job 平台上限 360m |
| `pull_request:opened/reopened/synchronize/labeled`，保留标签 `lean-cache-produce` | 检出事件 `M`，校验 `M^1=B,M^2=H`；只读内容、可写 actions 的 dispatch job 固定事件 PR/run/attempt | 不编译、不下载或保存种子；自动向受保护目标 ref dispatch 并输出具体 producer run ID；dispatch 成功不代表生产成功 | 5m |
| `workflow_dispatch`，`operation=build`（默认） | `source_ref` 必须等于 dispatch ref，且为受保护 `refs/heads/dev` 或 `refs/heads/integration-*`；非零 40 位小写十六进制 `source_sha` 必须可达，检出该 SHA | 同一已校验 branch scope | 360m |
| `workflow_dispatch`，`operation=pr` | workflow/runtime 为受保护目标 ref 的固定提交 `T`；源码检出 `source_sha=M`，`pr_source` 绑定事件 PR/H/M/B/run/attempt/target；不要求 M 已合入 | 共享核心仅构建并严格校验；原生路径打包上传 Actions artifact，不保存普通 cache key；编译 job 的 contents/actions token 只读；完成确认 immutable artifact ID | 360m |
| 自动 protected dev/integration push | current 结束后且未取消，`lean_required=true`、`project_seed_available!=true`；使用实际 push SHA | 同一 branch scope，复用长 Actions 核心 | 360m |
| schedule `0 */6 * * *` | 仅 `refs/heads/dev`；固定事件 SHA，发布入口另校验受保护 dev 来源；可先恢复 Actions 种子 | 独立 Release 发布入口；不写 Actions 种子 | 单 job 120m |
| 显式本地发布 / 取回 | `make lean-cache-to-github-without-mathlib` / `make lean-cache-from-github-without-mathlib`；沿既有来源、分区与锁校验 | 发布项目 buildDir / 取回本地 buildDir | 非 Actions job |

本地已完成的 buildDir 可用 `make lean-cache-to-github-without-mathlib LEAN_CACHE_MODE=candidate LEAN_CACHE_SOURCE_COMMIT=<完整SHA> LEAN_CACHE_PUBLISH_ONLY=1` 发布。该模式要求干净且已存在于本仓 GitHub 的固定提交，并验证当前完整报告与资产；`LEAN_CACHE_PUBLISH_ONLY=1` 禁止调用报告构建，缺失或陈旧报告直接失败。候选使用隔离的 verification Release 名称，不进入普通 production 查找或裁剪。`LEAN_CACHE_REPOSITORY` 可显式指定产物所在仓库目录。

长 producer 的手动输入 `release_seed_tag` 与 `release_seed_producer` 共同选择一个本地候选种子。恢复核对完整 producer SHA、报告格式/执行分区、全部资产摘要与安全归档成员，支持已声明的 ARM64 平台族；失败保留原 buildDir 并退出，不改投其它 Release。只有来源已核验的长任务可以在 CI 使用该显式入口；普通 CI 的 Release 自动恢复仍关闭。恢复后继续执行当前树的 Lake 增量构建和报告核验；其成功报告可将该显式输入写入 Actions 缓存，即使本次增量计算量为零。缓存输入不签发检查结论，完整 required、冻结与 Scribe 检查仍分别执行。

每 6 小时是 schedule 触发间隔，不是 120 分钟 job 的运行预算，也不是 360 分钟 Actions 核心的触发周期。定时 job 独立占用资源、产生结果并调用自己的 Release 发布入口，不等待或复用长 producer 的成功判词。manual 与自动 producer 按 branch concurrency 串行，`cancel-in-progress: false`；目标上下文的 PR producer 按源 PR concurrency，`cancel-in-progress: true`；标签 dispatch 本身不等待长构建，占用的短 job 只返回具体 run ID，由调用者等待该 run。

纯理论免报告只接受真实 delta 非空、全部路径为 `docs/develop/theory/` 下理论文档且非删除输入已经读取的情况：PR base 为 `M^1`，push base 为固定 `event.before`，沿既有快照/FILEMAP 与 `ExecuteTheoryDelta` 执行 `SL-003`、`SL-015`。不恢复 Lean 缓存、不生产报告、不运行全局 current/Scribe、不触发自动 producer；其它命中单元照常运行。混合变更必须提供报告；空 delta 即使进入无报告入口也会拒绝，未读取的理论输入也拒绝，均不构成免检。无可读 before 的事件走完整报告选择，不用未知 delta 猜纯理论。

**状态与决策。** 兼容种子须具有规范报告、四个 sidecar 与成功收据，并满足 A14.9 的格式和工件检查；格式相符不表示输入未变。

| 已观测状态 | 必须执行的后续动作 / 结果 |
| --- | --- |
| 兼容种子，报告绑定输入未变 | 复用整份报告；选中的程序目标仍单独完成 Lake 增量构建义务，程序失败保持非零。 |
| 兼容种子，源码/配置/utility 等报告输入变化 | 交给 native Lake traces 选择模块增量并聚合，复用行保留原来源；不加第二套 FIB 兼容扫描或迁移。 |
| 种子缺失、不完整、不可读或格式不符 | 短报告入口使用既有 `fetch-or-fail` 恢复路径；恢复后仍无兼容种子，`LEAN_REPORT_CACHE_INCOMPATIBLE` 以底层 exit 4 在提取或选定程序构建前失败。make 可能返回 2，须保留原诊断。 |
| 格式仍为 v3，但实际载荷被既有 consumer 拒绝 | 保留该 consumer 的真实错误及阶段；不能伪称 cache missing、静默兼容或自动迁移，也不为 FIB 新增探测器。 |
| 短任务超时、编译失败或报告失败 | 保留实际失败阶段、退出/取消状态与诊断；独立 producer 是补救入口，不把本次失败转绿、不静默改为完整构建或抬预算。 |
| 报告成功，当前工作收据中 report + programs 已知为正 | 有写入资格的 writer 在已就绪诊断之后准备并保存 project 快照；独立准入拒绝不阻止具备资格的普通 push 发布。短 CI 保存/确认传输失败不改变检查结果。 |
| 报告成功，工作已知为零 | 普通恢复仍为 `no-build-work`，不重复上传；已接受普通 Actions project 种子时避免自动长任务。有效的合入准备采用记录允许按既有 target key 保存，即使 work=0；源 PR 不获 project 写入资格。 |
| 工作缺失、畸形、陈旧或未知 | `build-work-unknown`，禁写 project，未知不等于零；长 producer 无可用种子则失败。 |
| 长 producer 新建种子 | 必须完成报告/程序生产并验证可用性，保存后核同 ref 的实际 cache key；不能仅凭 save 调用结束宣称已发布。PR 准备入口仅上传 artifact，并确认 artifact ID、producer run 与当前源 PR 身份；该成功不等于普通 cache 可用。 |
| PR 准备构建工作为零 | 仍执行既有报告/程序生产及严格验证，再包装已复用的原生材料；不伪造正工作量，不写普通目标 cache。 |
| PR dispatch、来源、构建、artifact 上传或身份确认失败 | 对应 dispatch 或目标 producer 非零并保留原始原因；标签 job 的 dispatch 成功不掩盖目标 run 的失败。受控交接的调用者须重新取得实际准备与 required 成功；旧有效缓存仍可使用，不在短 CI 自动修复。 |
| 准备缺失、过期或 producer attempt 未成功 | 不算交接成功；未采用时继续既有普通恢复。短 CI 仍为 `fetch-or-fail`，长 producer 可按既有完整生产职责补救。下载期间 API 失败保留原错误并失败，不伪报采用。 |
| 准备身份、partition、原生归档或实际报告载荷无效 | 身份/运输失败保持 `PR_CACHE_ERROR`；报告/程序由既有 consumer 判定并保留原始判词。无扫描迁移、额外兼容版本或静默冷构建。 |
| Release 发布 | 构建/内容校验失败保持硬失败；可选传输失败可返回 0 并给出 `failed`/`skipped`，清理失败记录 `prune_error`。新发布成功须 `published` 且远端 published 状态、manifest/资产清单已验证；exit 0 本身不足，`exists` 只表示已核对既存快照。 |

`fetch-or-fail` 不扩大 Release 的使用范围：`lean_cache_release.fetch` 在 `GITHUB_ACTIONS=true` 时明确 `skipped`，CI 不下载生产 Release 种子，随后仍须通过兼容种子检查；本地主检出可走 Release recovery，linked worktree 依既有 warm-main/reseed 边界拒绝直接下载。恢复边界不授权短 CI 冷项目构建。旧模块载荷违反当前严格契约时，不接受旧值、不增加兼容扫描或格式代际；使用既有显式 `REGENERATE_REPORT_ARTIFACTS=1` / `regenerate_report_artifacts` 删除原生报告及其 traces，并禁止该次调用从 Lake 工件备份恢复旧报告，再重建；保留编译程序目录、编译输入投影、数学与依赖编译产物。此修复由独立长 producer 执行，短入口不自动全量再生。

需要 Lean/report 的自动 push 仅在已确认发布增量种子，或普通 Actions 种子已接受、报告成功且本次工作确为 `no-build-work` 时免去长 producer；不需要 Lean/report 时也不触发。cache hit、unknown work 或未核实的上传均不能替代这些条件。

**写入权限与不可变快照。** 普通构建 snapshot 的 project 写入前置是 `STRATALINT_REPORT_SUCCEEDED`、已知的 report/program 工作和既有 writer 身份；通常要求正工作，当前调用已验证的合入准备采用记录允许零工作。写入不要求全部检查绿。普通 push 在报告生产成功后按既有资格尝试一次，上传与确认不作为已就绪准入检查的前置；普通 PR required job 由 `project_publication` 返回 `pull-requests-do-not-publish-project-seeds`。普通 current 的 dependency/elan 在该作业检查成功后各按条件保存，不等待其它并行 job；judge 在自身编译成功后即可保存。源 PR job 的辅助缓存写入也不能跨越 PR ref 边界。Actions save 为本次 run/attempt 创建新 project/dependency 快照，不修改旧 immutable key；无合入准备采用记录时，普通 snapshot 零工作无需重复上传。PR 长构建在目标 dispatch 上下文只准备 artifact；普通 target/project、dependency 和 elan save 均关闭，旧 reader 和 native prefix fallback 因而也无未合入候选可选。短 CI 的可选运输与实际报告/编译/判卷成败分开，缓存不是真源。

**PR 准备与合并启用。** `lean-cache-publish.yml` 是 dispatch 入口，`operation=build` 保持手动受保护提交的长构建语义；`operation=pr` 在目标 ref 的 workflow 上下文检出独立固定 `source_sha=M`。两者和自动 push 共用 `.github/actions/lean-cache-produce` 的编译、报告与严格 bundle 验证；PR 使用 `preparation-only`，跳过所有普通 cache snapshot/save。共享报告入口在新变更报告发布前完成必要的依赖范围 FIB 回归，其它测试仍由普通 CI 命中单元执行；准备和采用不替代当前选中的检查。`regenerate_report_artifacts` 仍是显式选择，不由交接自动启用。

标签 `lean-cache-produce` 是 PR 准备的持续选择。`opened`、`reopened`、`synchronize` 事件在标签仍存在时准备事件当前 head；`labeled` 只响应该标签的添加。缺少或移除标签不自动准备，重新添加标签可对失败的同一 head 重试，不建立轮询或独立重试器。新的准备仍由原生每 PR concurrency 取消旧 producer；开工、打包与上传确认均核对当前 head/target，陈旧事件不得发布可采用材料。标签移除不删除既有不可变 artifact，也不扩张其采用资格。

该 `pull_request` job 捕获仓库、事件 PR 编号、head `H`、实际检出 merge `M`、父提交 `B=M^1` 与 `H=M^2`、源 run/attempt 和目标 ref。它使用 `contents:read/pull-requests:read/actions:write` 调用 dispatch API，`return_run_details=true` 返回具体 `workflow_run_id`；缺失 ID、权限不足或 API 错误直接失败。该 job 不编译或保存缓存。fork token 无 dispatch 权限时保留原始拒绝，不引入 `pull_request_target` 或新的提权入口。

准备 producer 使用 `contents:read/pull-requests:read/actions:read`；开工核对当前源 PR open/head/target、源 run 的 event/workflow/head/attempt、受保护目标、B 在目标历史内以及本地 `HEAD=M`、父提交 `B,H`。完成打包和上传确认允许同 head/target 的 PR 已实际 merged，但不允许 closed-unmerged。`run.pull_requests` 的可变关联不参与身份判定。B 可落后 tip，M 不必为目标祖先，不启用 strict=true。真实 `GITHUB_SHA=T`、workflow ref/SHA、producer run/attempt 与源 M 分列；`ci-entry.sh` 只在自身调用中用 M 校验 checkout。

Actions artifact 名为 `lean-pr-preparation-<PR>-<H>`；这是精确发现地址，不是新 cache key 或格式代际。artifact 内仅 `preparation.json` 与 `native.tar.gz`：前者绑定源身份、producer runtime、现有 native partition 与 elan pin key，不含 schema/version；后者从 `lean_actions.native_archive_paths` 和 `Meta/ci-cache-paths.json` 展开原生 project/dependency 成员，保留隐藏文件与可执行权限。toolchain 仍由普通 elan restore/install 负责。artifact ID 是不可变取回地址；同一 run 重试上传替换名称对应的旧 artifact 时取得新 ID。消费者核对该 ID 的 workflow run/SHA、记录的精确 producer attempt 成功以及 artifact 创建时点落在该 attempt 区间；latest run 状态与可变 PR 关联不替代精确 attempt。源身份、M 父提交与 protected runtime history 继续校验。

`current` 的报告路径、`ci-unit` 的 `lean=build` 路径和共享长核心在普通 dependency/project restore 前尝试显式采用；纯理论和 `lean=none/toolchain` 不增加此工作。源 PR 仅可取自身仍 open 的相同 PR/H/target 准备，允许 event M 的基线已推进，随后仍须通过当前 head 的全部 required。目标 push 由实际 event.after/ref 与 checkout 相等，再从该 commit 的 PR 关联中确认唯一实际 merged PR、目标和 `merge_commit_sha=checkout=K`；手动 protected build 对固定 source checkout 作相同确认。K 与准备 M 分列，不要求 SHA 相同。未合并、关闭未合并、另一 PR、另一 head 或目标均不取得该准备的使用资格。多提交 push 中 checkout 不是该 PR 的实际 merge commit 时不选该准备；后续普通 target cache 复用沿原规则，不扫描历史候选。

采用前先校验身份并在临时目录解包；拒绝重复、越界、未登记成员与逃逸链接。完成后替换 job 本地 native 目录，并在运输树之外写当前 run/attempt/checkout/artifact 的采用记录。普通 restore actions 跳过这两层，`lean_actions.restore` 也保留已采用材料。既有报告与程序消费者承担实际载荷校验与 Lake 增量；运输与 trace 不证明 producer/consumer 语义兼容，不加入 producer 程序字节指纹。若实际合并树、配置或源输入漂移，必要的原生增量仍须执行并计入 120m 计算预算；下载和准备不扣该预算，外层平台上限独立。

合并本身使匹配准备对该目标事件具备采用资格；不需第二个异步 publisher 完成，不强制重复完整冷编译。调度、API 可见性和下载仍有延迟。target 报告成功且工作量已知时，已验证采用可保存现有普通 project key，真实零工作也保留 `build_work=0`；无采用的普通零工作仍抑制上传。`project_seed_available` 只认可确认的普通发布，或普通缓存已接受且报告零工作；artifact 存在、已采用或 upload 成功均不单独抑制长 fallback。若短保存失败，长核心仍优先采用同一匹配准备，不能忽略它而冗余冷构建。

调用者沿现有生命周期持有 `AUTO_MERGE=0`，等待具体 producer run 成功及当前 head required 成功，再 pinned merge；源 required 可在准备完成后重跑以取得材料，无目标 cache 时也适用。准备成功不代表 required 成功；head 改变须重新准备。此顺序不新增 required check、保护规则、调度器或全局合并门。先在同一 integration 安装入口，再以未合入源 PR 验证隔离、当前 required、实际合并首次消费、零工作普通发布和长 fallback。实际 artifact 大小、权限、API、传输成本与首次事件仍需真实集成核验，本地 helper 测试不替代；dev 安装须经普通交付，不直推解锁。

**键、格式及归属。** 以下标识分别承担各自契约，报告格式与容器/manifest 代际不得混用：

| 标识 | 归属与复用边界 |
| --- | --- |
| `lean-project-push-<resolved-mathlib>-<OS>-<arch>-<run>-<attempt>` | Actions project 地址；`lean_actions.actions_keys` 单源生成，ref 另由 Actions scope 限定，只恢复此 project 前缀。 |
| `lean-dependency-v4-<resolved-mathlib>-<OS>-<arch>-<run>-<attempt>`、`elan-pinned-<OS>-<arch>-<pin-sha256>` | 既有 dependency 和钉版 elan 运输键；elan miss 由 `install-lean-toolchain.sh` 安装，保存前列出工具链，不删恢复的工具链。 |
| `lean-cache-v2-<resolved-mathlib>-<platform>-report-<format>-env-<execution12>-<publication-id>` | Release buildDir 容器地址；前缀选候选后，manifest 必须匹配完整 `cache_key` 的报告格式和完整 execution SHA256 才下载构建归档。 |
| `stratalint-raw-lean-report-v3` | `lean-report-selection.py:REPORT_FORMAT` 单源报告线格式；FIB 仅为原报告中的可选合同分析，不定义独立 `schema_version` 或独立缓存生命周期。 |
| Release manifest `lean-release-seed-v4`、build-work `schema_version=1` | 前者管理运输资产与来源，后者是当前调用在恢复树之外的工作量控制证据；均不是 FIB 版本，不能随 FIB 字段清理删除。 |

Release 的 execution 摘要只投影声明的 toolchain pin、工具集合、归一化平台族与声明环境值；名字中的 12 位摘要不替代 manifest 全值校验。`publication-id` 是 `ci-<run>-<attempt>` 或 `local-<HEAD12>-<UTC>`，HEAD 只在这个唯一后缀中，不决定复用；报告模块/配置全集摘要与生产程序字节不进入 Release 名字。Actions 按 OS/arch 隔离；Release 跨平台共享仅限 A14.9 已声明的 arm64 族，原生产物仍由 Lake retrace。详细键生成与容器清单以 `lean_actions.py`、`lean_cache_release.py` 为源，不增设版本清单。

报告、种子、收据等数据产物不得把 producer/judge 程序字节哈希作为复用条件；模块 trace、utility、格式与整份收据的输入边界依 A14.9。judge 编译缓存缓存的是程序自身，可哈希其实际构建输入与配方。显式历史登记无缓存重评是独立请求；`REBUILD_REPORT_CACHE=1` 只绕过整份 receipt，仍可复用原生模块产物，不保证重评全部历史登记或 full clean，也不承诺旧 same-v3 载荷自动迁移。

**显式报告工件重产。** 独立长 producer 的 `operation=build` 或 `operation=pr` 可显式选 `regenerate_report_artifacts=true`，默认关闭；采用对应操作的固定来源校验及同一 Actions 构建发布核心。等价本地入口为 `make lean-report REGENERATE_REPORT_ARTIFACTS=1`。此选项强制跳过整份 receipt 复用与 Release 恢复，完成既有 ensure/worktree 参与后，在 `lean-cache-run` 的缓存写锁内删除 `.lake/build/lean-inspector` 的生成状态（模块 ZIP、trace、pending/previous、聚合 ZIP 及派生报告输入），并在同一写锁内执行报告/选定程序的 Lake 构建。该次 Lake 进程设置 `LAKE_ARTIFACT_CACHE=false` 和 `LAKE_RESTORE_ARTIFACTS=false`，阻止内容寻址仓库按旧 trace 恢复已删除的报告。保留原生状态中的 `producer`、`interface`、`reg` 编译程序目录及 `judge-inputs` 编译输入投影，以及 `.lake/build/lib`、IR、依赖及 toolchain，由 Lake 继续判定其编译有效性；不清空共享 artifact store，不修改 cache key 或报告格式。

canonical report 入口对 fresh Application 输入仍先严格验证；仅此显式恢复路径中，旧发布报告的 `fib.invalid_partition` 不可作相等输入证据，必须成功完成一次 canonical FIB 回归才可发布与封存。普通路径仍拒绝该旧分区；有效 predecessor 保留原有完整输入比较及显式选择语义。报告或回归失败保持先前物理发布成员，不写成功收据，传播原退出码；不新增 schema、缓存版本、扫描器或测试收据。

长 Actions 核心在报告构建后、snapshot/save 前调用现有 `publication.py validate` 严格验证完整 bundle；失败保留原始原因且不发布新的 project seed。显式重产失败不封成功 receipt，不触发另一种修复。普通 `REBUILD_REPORT_CACHE=1`、默认长 producer、自动 PR 标签 dispatch、短 CI 和定时 Release 均不隐式启用这一选项；没有 FIB 专用扫描、自动迁移或读取器兼容分支。此能力不证明远端故障已恢复：须安装当前候选后，在固定提交显式长重产，取得既有严格 Release 发布/取回验证及正常短 CI 消费证据，再结算远端恢复。

调用依据：[普通 CI](../../../.github/workflows/ci-current.yml)、[unit](../../../.github/workflows/ci-unit.yml)、[长 Actions 核心](../../../.github/actions/lean-cache-produce/action.yml)、[标签/手动/定时入口](../../../.github/workflows/lean-cache-publish.yml)、[Actions 运输](../../../tools/scripts/worktree/lean_actions.py)、[PR 目标生产](../../../tools/scripts/worktree/lean_pr_cache.py)、[Release 发布/取回](../../../tools/scripts/worktree/lean_cache_release.py)、[报告复用](../../../tools/lean-inspector/reuse.py)、[免报告理论检查](../../../tools/StrataLint.Engine/Rules/RuleCatalog.cs)。

project 恢复在 current 与 Lean build 单元中先查读者可见的种子：pull_request 优先取自身 `refs/pull/<number>/merge`，该 ref 无合格种子时再取 `refs/heads/$GITHUB_BASE_REF`；push 取 `$GITHUB_REF`。`lean_actions.py keys` 使用可选的 `GH_TOKEN`、`GITHUB_REPOSITORY` 与 `GITHUB_API_URL`（默认 `https://api.github.com`），通过标准库 HTTP 请求完整分页的 Actions cache 列表，显式按 `created_at` 降序分页；每行的 cache `id` 须为互异正整数，重复、缺失或无效 id，或不同 id 的总数与 `total_count` 不符，均走具名不完整列表 fallback。只接纳 ref 精确相同、key 为当前 project restore prefix 加正十进制 run id 与 attempt 的条目，按数值 `(run id, attempt)` 取最大值，不使用列表顺序或时间字段。`project_restore_key` 是独立的恢复输出，`project_key` 仍是本次发布 key。查找未配置、请求失败、HTTP 错误、列表畸形或不完整、无合格条目时具名报告原因并请求本次 key，保留现有 prefix restore；偏好 key 被淘汰或不兼容时也经同一 prefix fallback 与既有恢复入口补齐。每次请求保留固定网络 timeout；整个可选查找由单个固定 10 秒 transport hang guard 限定，涵盖名称解析、连接、headers、body 及全部分页。查找在 daemon thread 中执行；guard 到期即放弃等待，以 `lookup-transport-hang-guard` 原因输出既有 fallback 收据并继续本次 key，不重试或轮询。daemon thread 不输出收据或 key。restore 收据同时给出 requested key 与实际 matched key。此偏好仅是性能启发式；run id 大小不证明 push 顺序、构建材料包含关系或有效性，查找与缓存命中均不承载检查成功或判词。

编译依赖 trace、utility 输入与报告格式标识决定报告复用，Lake traces 决定实际模块重建；复用行保留原 producer 来源。正常报告入口仍执行原生构建与严格 publication 校验，不借恢复绕过 Lean kernel、utility/audit 或报告验证。定时 Release 缓存发布与 integration 的 cache publication 验证独立保留；验证事件、来源、run/attempt、归档材料与空目标恢复按其既有程序核对，不外推为 truth 发布成功。

**退出与部署。** raw 0 为成功，1 为候选检查失败，2 为输入/基础设施失败，3 只表示 SL-022 保护面变更标注。只有其余义务已通过才可将 3 归一为 workflow 成功；make 配方失败会折叠为 2，报告时保留底层退出码与 Block 列表。缺失报告或无效 TRX 不得成为成功。

工作流不新增文本形状测试；消费 workflow 的生产逻辑可以使用既有合成夹具。实际事件、候选版本、required 名称、权限、覆盖、缓存与性能必须依 CLAUDE.md §8.12 真跑核验。integration 保持每个原始 PR 的独立复测和稳定条数要求，部署资格与本地实现/测试成功分开报告。产物只保留正式结果、必要实验程序与读数，不保存迁移流水。

---

# 第四部:harness 执法(不变量·状态机)

## 4.1 十二不变量(执法栏为「—」者为纪律,不由机器判)
| # | 律 | 执法 |
|---|---|---|
| H1 | 向下 import(地层单调) | SL-001 |
| H2 | sorry 仅居 X_Frontier | SL-002 |
| H3 | 容量阈(目录≤96 文件、文件≤1000 行)⟹ 分裂协议 | SL-003 |
| H4 | 镜像律(B/E 存在或显式 waiver) | SL-004 |
| H5 | 编年历史归 git(无机器判官;2026-09-02 owner 裁决,SL-005 已退役) | — |
| H6 | 徽章由语法生成,禁手写状态 | SL-006 |
| H7 | 利益回避(同 PR 禁改 X_A 又用之;对手≠证师;禁自并) | 门官 |
| H8 | Hearts 冻结(可增证明不可改删陈述) | SL-008 |
| H9 | 溯源(LLM PR 必携转录+模型版本) | 门官 |
| H10 | 通用性头必填;标 G 者禁 import 实例事实 | SL-010 |
| H11 | 词表律(目录名∈domains.yaml) | SL-011 |
| H12 | 任务码永久 | SL-013 |

注:SL-007/009 保留空号(H7/H9 为门官策略非 lint);现役至 SL-023:SL-020 为 Lean 环境公理/状态律,SL-021 为未实例化坐标律,SL-022 为元层门,SL-023 为 Describe LaTeX epoch 规则。

**SL-001 包导入方向与债务收缩。** D5 数学模块不得新增对 `LeanInformationAudit`、`LeanInformationAuditAnalysis`、`LeanInformationAuditInterface`、`Reg` 本身或其点分子模块的 import；`Reg` 可导入 D5 与契约接口，传递依赖闭包不得包含判官实现，不作为地层。现有 D5 地层检查保持原判据。SL-001 的 delta 判据从受保护 snapshot 的源码数据解析 `D_base`，从候选 Lean report 读取 `D_head`，边身份为 `(source path, imported module)`，强制 `D_head ⊆ D_base`；base 数据不执行。对每个 `D_base(p) ≠ ∅` 的源模块，须满足 `bytes_head(p) = bytes_base(p)` 或 `|D_head(p)| < |D_base(p)|`，删除模块视为零边；集合包含使等量换债、移往另一模块以及减量换债均被拒绝。`D_base = ∅` 时同一判据对全树拒绝所有禁止边，不按 changed paths 缩域；具名回归测试检出删除集合包含、改作计数比较、跳过触及模块减债以及删除零债全树分支的变异。

## 4.2 生命周期状态机(四台;状态机无台账,git 历史即台账)
```
定理:Frontier(sorry)→echoed(回声过审)
      →proven(合并)→audited(K 日复审)→[generalized(上收 Metallic)]
假设:proposed→active→{proven→retired-as-theorem(级联除氢)| refuted→errata 级联}
实验:spec'd→running→{observation→C 层+候选 Frontier | refinement→X_A 之 PR | negative→归档(负知识照记)}
论文:recipe→draft→adversary-reviewed→publication-admission(`open` 直至对应机器谓词落地)→frozen→published→(errata 追加页)
```
